Stop is now the app's secondary button (`SECONDARY_LOOK`, newly exported from `Button.tsx`: the `btn-secondary-bg` fill, a `line` border, darker on hover, the square in `--ink`), both as a direct's Stop and when the main button turns into Stop; the accent stays Send's. The stop menu's trigger beside the composer is the same rounded pill, a square and a faint chevron, darker while open as on hover. The menu draws its rows as the session's context menu does: each starts with an icon of what it stops (the run ■, a background task a terminal, a group a message, a Bot, a job a board, every Bot people), and the icon and words turn to the accent on hover. A thin line parts the whole conversation (the run, the group) from its parts, and another comes before "every Bot"; a long task name is cut to one line. The trigger shows no tip while the menu is open. On a phone the sheet uses the same icons and lines. The small header trigger with the word "停止" keeps its shape. Both floor registrations (ADR 0108) follow the new colours: the square in `ink` over `btn-secondary-bg` over `input-bg`. The interface story's "hide Stop in dark" sample now sets the dark `--btn-secondary-bg` to `--ink`. The messenger was not changed; the Tauri version looks as before.

Checked in a background check copy on a throwaway story showing the three composers side by side (a direct's Stop, the main button as Stop, and the menu), captured in the dark and the light look, again with the menu open, and at 420pt wide for the phone's 40pt buttons and the sheet. Before, Stop was a white disc in dark and a black one in light; after, in both looks it is a secondary button of Send's weight, the menu has an icon per row, lines between its groups and cut long names, and no tip covers it. `nsh check` and the screen tests `interface`, `chat-composer`, `chat-composer-2`, `job-view` and `job-view-board` pass.

## 补记 2026-11-11（二十七）：对话里展开命令不再先空一下 / Addendum 2026-11-11 (27): opening a reply's commands no longer shows an empty box first

你说展开「1 条命令」时先出来一个空框，过一会儿才有内容，要是在加载就加加载效果，否则修掉。不是在加载：卡片里的行是手上现成的数据，一次网络请求都没有。原因在 NSH 内核（钉住的 7a2c11e0，上游 main 也一样）：`Scroll` 只先做视口附近的内容（content culling），其余的留着，等核对时再做；可这次核对（`commit_round` 里的 `sync_culled`）排在 `update_lists` 给列表单元格摆位之前。卡片的 `Scroll` 在对话列表的一行里，刚展开时还没有位置，核对就跳过它，等单元格摆好位置，这一轮已经不再核对了。于是行一直不做，直到窗口里别的东西让页面重新排一次（流式的字、悬停、时间），这就是「过一会儿」。无头测试关了 culling，看不出来。

后台检查副本跑一个临时故事，把命令卡片放进倒置 `List` 的一行（和项目会话一样），点开后 300ms 和 3s 截图：都是空框，和你的截图一样；发一次 `resize` 让页面重排，行立刻出来。只给 `Scroll` 挂 `onLayout`、什么都不做，截图和空框逐字节相同；在 `onLayout` 里发一次 `scrollTo`，300ms 时行就在，只有一行、根本没东西可滚的卡片也一样。滚动命令让内核把这个滚动视图重新核对一遍。

现在 `CommandActivity` 的 `Scroll` 每次挂载都把「在底部」重置为真，拿到位置（`onLayout`）时滚到末尾。这也补上了和信使的差别：信使的命令列表展开时就停在最后一条（`node.scrollTop = node.scrollHeight`），NSH 版原来停在开头，原有的 effect 只在命令条数变化时滚，展开那一刻 `list` 还没挂上。同一套检查重截：一行的卡片展开、收起、再展开，每次 300ms 时行都在；点开一条命令的输出，300ms 时内容就在，和 2s 时逐字节相同；300 行的卡片展开就停在最后一条，从松开鼠标到 `onLayout` 5ms。`TurnStepList` 挂载时 effect 已经滚一次，所以没有这个毛病。`nsh check`、`chat-cards-activity`、`chat-stage`、`chat-stage-project` 三个界面测试文件、`bun test scripts`（27）和行数预算都过。内核修好（单元格摆位之后再核对一次）、钉住的版本带上它之后，这一下滚动仍然保留，因为它本来就是信使的行为，注释里关于内核的那句要删。

You said that opening "1 条命令" showed an empty box first and its contents only a while later: if it was loading it should show that it was, and otherwise it should be fixed. Nothing was loading. The card's rows are data already at hand, with no request behind them. The cause is in the NSH core (the pinned 7a2c11e0, upstream main the same). A `Scroll` makes only the content near its viewport and holds the rest back until a pass looks at it again. That pass (`sync_culled` in `commit_round`) runs before `update_lists` places the list's cells. The card's `Scroll` sits in a row of the transcript `List`, so when it opens it has no place yet and the pass skips it; once the cell is placed, the round no longer looks. The rows stay unmade until something else lays the window out again (streamed text, a hover, the clock), which was the "while". Headless tests turn culling off and can't see it.

Checked in a background check copy on a throwaway story that puts the command card in a row of an inverted `List`, as the project session does. Captured 300 ms and 3 s after opening, the box was empty both times, as in your screenshot; one `resize` laid the page out and the row appeared at once. An `onLayout` on the `Scroll` that does nothing left the capture byte-identical to the empty box. A `scrollTo` from `onLayout` had the row there at 300 ms, also on a one-row card with nothing to scroll: a scroll command makes the core look at that scroll view again.

`CommandActivity`'s `Scroll` now sets "at the end" back to true each time it mounts and scrolls to its end once it has its place (`onLayout`). This also closes a gap with the messenger, whose command list opens at its last command (`node.scrollTop = node.scrollHeight`). The NSH card opened at its top, because the existing effect scrolled only when the number of commands changed, and at the moment of opening `list` was not mounted yet. The same check, captured again: a one-row card opened, closed and opened again had its row at 300 ms each time. One command's output, opened, was there at 300 ms and byte-identical at 2 s. A 300-row card opened at its last command, 5 ms from mouse-up to `onLayout`. `TurnStepList` already scrolls once from an effect on mount, so it never showed the fault. `nsh check`, the screen tests `chat-cards-activity`, `chat-stage` and `chat-stage-project`, `bun test scripts` (27) and the line budget pass. Once the core looks again after placing cells and the pinned rev has that fix, the scroll stays, since it is what the messenger does; only the comment's sentence about the core goes.

当天的后续：上面的写法每次拿到新位置都滚到末尾，而卡片在点开一条输出时会变高。中等长度的卡片（9 条命令，第一条输出 30 行）点开第一条输出后，整张卡片跳到末尾，刚点开的那条和输出的前 5 行滚出了上沿；信使在这时反而把点开的输出滚进视野（`scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' })`）。之前只测了一行和 300 行两种卡片：一行的没东西可滚，300 行的高度一直顶在 372pt，所以都看不出来。现在只在挂载后的第一次 `onLayout` 滚一次（`placing` 在 `ref` 里置上，第一次布局时清掉）。同一个临时故事重截：9 条命令的卡片点开第一条输出，300ms 时那条在卡片顶上、输出 1–12 行可见，和 2s 时逐字节相同；展开、收起、再展开，两次展开逐字节相同；300 条命令的卡片停在末尾，点开中间一条（thing294）的输出，300ms 时输出就在，此时外层卡片高度不变、`onLayout` 不再触发，所以输出自己的 `Scroll` 不需要另外补一下；`TurnStepList` 放进列表的一行、过一会儿再打开，300ms 时标题、四步和命令输出都在，和 2s 时逐字节相同，上面说它不受影响这才算核实。平台：网页版（手机）不做内容裁剪，碰不到这个问题；macOS 的滚动命令不管动没动都会回报，这一下才管用；Windows 和 Linux 也开着裁剪，回报没核对过。检查：`nsh check`（App、Story）、`tsc`、`bun test scripts`（27）、`port-messenger --check`、`theme-from-tokens --check`、行数预算，以及界面测试 `chat-cards-activity`、`chat-stage`、`chat-stage-project` 都过；仓库根目录的整套 `pnpm test` 没有跑，这次只动了 `apps/desktop-nsh`。

Later the same day: the code above scrolled to the end at every new layout, and the card grows when an output opens. On a mid-size card (9 commands, the first with 30 lines of output), opening the first output made the whole card jump to its end: the row just opened and the first 5 lines of its output went out over the top. The messenger does the opposite here and brings the opened output into view (`scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' })`). Only the one-row and the 300-row cards had been checked: the first has nothing to scroll, and the second always stays at 372 pt, so neither could show it. Now it scrolls once, at the first `onLayout` after mounting (`placing` is set in `ref` and cleared at the first layout). The same throwaway story, captured again. On the 9-command card, after opening the first output, that row was at the card's top with output lines 1–12 in view at 300 ms, byte-identical at 2 s. Opened, closed and opened again, the two opened frames were byte-identical. The 300-command card stayed at its end, and an output opened in the middle (thing294) was there at 300 ms. There the outer card keeps its height and `onLayout` does not fire again, so the output's own `Scroll` needs no nudge of its own. `TurnStepList`, put in a list row and opened a while later, had its title, four steps and the command's output at 300 ms, byte-identical at 2 s; only now is the claim above that it is unaffected checked. Platforms: the web build (phone) does no content culling, so it cannot meet this. On macOS a scroll command reports back whether or not it moved, which is why the nudge works. Windows and Linux cull too, and their report was not checked. Checks: `nsh check` (App, Story), `tsc`, `bun test scripts` (27), `port-messenger --check`, `theme-from-tokens --check`, the line budget, and the screen tests `chat-cards-activity`, `chat-stage` and `chat-stage-project` pass. The repository-wide `pnpm test` was not run; only `apps/desktop-nsh` changed.

## 补记 2026-11-11（二十八）：项目会话里右键又能弹出窗格菜单 / Addendum 2026-11-11 (28): a right click in a project session opens the pane's menu again

你说项目会话里右键没有弹出窗格的右键菜单。原因在 NSH 的右键规则（钉住的 7a2c11e0，`nsh-platform-macos` `input.rs` 的 `context_menu`）：右键从按下那点往外找，没有冒泡；最近的挂了 `onContextMenu` 的视图听到它，可是在那之前先碰到选区（`selectable` 视图，或可选的字），就弹 NSH 自己的「Copy / Select All」，不再往外找。窗格菜单挂在 `WorkbenchLeaf` 的根上。补记十五之后，项目会话的整段对话包在一个 `<View selectable>` 里（好让拖选跨行），它比窗格根近，于是在对话里哪里右键都只有拷贝；在那之前，每行的 `MarkdownBody` 已经各是一个选区，点在字上也一样。Tauri 版在窗格里任何地方右键都弹窗格菜单，除非里面有东西自己接了（消息菜单、输入框）；项目会话的行没有自己的菜单，所以弹的是窗格菜单。

现在 `WorkbenchLeaf` 给窗格内容提供 `PaneMenu`（新的 `ui/workbench/pane-menu.ts`，一个按窗格打开窗格菜单的函数，窗格外为 null）。`ProjectChat` 的外层 `<View selectable>` 自己挂 `onContextMenu`，打开它（同一视图上监听先于选区算；不在窗格里时不挂，留着 NSH 的拷贝菜单）。里面不再有自己的选区：每行和工作中气泡的 `MarkdownBody` 写 `selectable={false}`，你那行的字和待批准的摘要去掉内层的 `selectable`，计划卡在对话里时 `selectable={false}`、全屏时仍是自己的选区（那时它不在对话里），`TurnStepList` 新加 `selectable` 属性（默认 true，Bot 的流里不变），项目会话的步骤列表传 false。没写 `selectable` 的字照样并进外层选区（`selectable_view.rs` 的 `target`：只有明确写了 false 的字才出局）。代价：这里右键不再有拷贝和查询，复制用 ⌘C，和 Tauri 版不按 ⌥ 时一样。

无头测试的 `rightClick` 直接找最近的监听、不看选区（`nsh-testing` `screen.rs` 的 `context_menu_target`），修前修后都会过，所以在真窗口里查：后台检查宿主加一个临时请求 `rclick x y`，给第一个窗口发右键按下、抬起，并把 `+[NSMenu popUpContextMenu:withEvent:forView:]` 换成只记日志（`NATIVE-CONTEXT-MENU`）；`chat-stage` 故事的项目会话外面临时包一个记日志的 `PaneMenu.Provider`（`PANE-MENU`），对话的选区临时记下选中的字；对一个隔离的运行时。修前，agent 的字、agent 行右边的空白、你的气泡、气泡左边的空白、时间、顶上的空白、app 的提示条、日期线，八处全是 `["Copy", "Select All"]`，没有一次窗格菜单；修后八处全是窗格菜单，坐标对得上。工作中气泡的流式字、待批准的摘要、对话里计划卡的标题和正文也都是窗格菜单；全屏的计划卡（不在窗格里）仍是 NSH 的拷贝菜单。选区没变：从 agent 的字一路拖到你第二个气泡，修前修后三次增量选中的字逐字相同；在你的气泡里拖选也照旧。故事里那次运行没有步骤可展开，所以展开后的步骤列表没在真窗口里右键过。临时改动都已撤掉，没有提交。`nsh check`、`bun test scripts`（27）、行数预算、界面测试 `chat-stage-project`、`chat-stage`、`workbench`、`workbench-menus`、`workbench-drag`、`sidebar-sheets-project` 都过。

顺带查到同一类问题、这次没改：Bot 的对话里，在 Bot 回复的字、回复那一行的空白、你的气泡上右键，也只有「Copy / Select All」，消息菜单不出来，因为 `msg-reply` 是一个 `selectable` 视图，比消息的 `onContextMenu` 近。`chat-stage.test.tsx` 的「a secondary press on a line opens its menu」照样过，原因同上。只看了 macOS 的规则；Windows 和 GPUI 的 `input.rs` 也在选区里弹自己的菜单，没细查。

You said that a right click in a project session did not open the pane's context menu. The cause is NSH's right-click rule (the pinned 7a2c11e0, `context_menu` in `nsh-platform-macos` `input.rs`). A right click looks outward from the point pressed, with no bubbling. The nearest view with `onContextMenu` hears it, but if a selection (a `selectable` view, or selectable text) comes first, NSH shows its own "Copy / Select All" and looks no further. The pane's menu sits on `WorkbenchLeaf`'s root. Since addendum 15, a project session's whole conversation is wrapped in one `<View selectable>` (so a drag runs across lines), nearer than the pane's root, so a right click anywhere in the conversation gave only Copy. Before that, each line's `MarkdownBody` was already a selection of its own, so a right click on the words did the same. The Tauri app opens the pane's menu on a right click anywhere in a pane unless something inside answered it (the message menu, a text field); a project session's lines have no menu of their own, so the pane's opened.

`WorkbenchLeaf` now provides `PaneMenu` to what lies in a pane (the new `ui/workbench/pane-menu.ts`: a function opening that pane's menu, null outside a pane). `ProjectChat`'s outer `<View selectable>` takes `onContextMenu` itself and opens it; on the same view the listener comes before the selection. Outside a pane it takes none, which keeps NSH's Copy menu there. Nothing inside is a selection of its own. Each line's and the working bubble's `MarkdownBody` says `selectable={false}`. Your line's words and the approval's summary lose their inner `selectable`. The plan card says `selectable={false}` in the conversation and stays a selection of its own full-window, where it is not in the conversation. `TurnStepList` gets a `selectable` prop (default true, so the Bot stream is unchanged), and the project session's step list passes false. Text with no `selectable` of its own still joins the outer selection: in `selectable_view.rs` `target`, only text that says false is kept out. The cost: right click there no longer offers Copy or Look Up. Copy with ⌘C, as in the Tauri app without ⌥.

The headless `rightClick` goes straight to the nearest listener and ignores selections (`context_menu_target` in `nsh-testing` `screen.rs`), so it passes before and after alike. The check was in a real window. A background check host had a temporary `rclick x y` request that posts a right button down and up to its first window. It replaced `+[NSMenu popUpContextMenu:withEvent:forView:]` with a log line (`NATIVE-CONTEXT-MENU`). The `chat-stage` story's project session was wrapped for the check in a `PaneMenu.Provider` that logs `PANE-MENU`, and the conversation's selection logged its text, against an isolated runtime. Before the fix, eight points all gave `["Copy", "Select All"]` and never the pane's menu: an agent line's words, the blank right of it, your bubble, the blank left of your bubble, a time, the blank at the top, the app's chip and a day line. After the fix all eight opened the pane's menu at the point pressed. So did the working bubble's streamed text, the approval's summary, and the plan card's heading and body in the conversation. The full-window plan card, which is not in a pane, still gives NSH's Copy menu. Selection did not change. A drag from an agent line's words to your second bubble gave the same text, character for character, at each of its three steps before and after, and a drag inside your bubble selects as before. The story's run had no steps to open, so the opened step list was not right-clicked in a real window. The temporary changes are undone and not committed. `nsh check`, `bun test scripts` (27), the line budget and the screen tests `chat-stage-project`, `chat-stage`, `workbench`, `workbench-menus`, `workbench-drag` and `sidebar-sheets-project` pass.

The same kind of fault, found on the way and not changed here: in a Bot conversation, a right click on a Bot reply's words, on the blank of its row, or on your bubble also gives only "Copy / Select All" and not the message menu, because `msg-reply` is a `selectable` view nearer than the message's `onContextMenu`. `chat-stage.test.tsx`'s "a secondary press on a line opens its menu" passes anyway, for the reason above. Only the macOS rule was read. The Windows and GPUI `input.rs` also show their own menu inside a selection; they were not looked at closely.

## 补记 2026-11-11（二十九）：任务的引擎和视频预览 / Addendum 2026-11-11 (29): a job's engine and the video preview

给 README 截图时在 NSH 版里看到两处毛病，你说修掉。

一是任务标签的「应用的分工」把视频群的任务写成「引擎：通用 · 会话没选，用默认」。快照里群的 `playbook_id` 是 `video`（`/v1/snapshot` 和信使新开页面都对）；在检查副本里临时打日志看到，快照到了以后紧接着这一行就变成了 null。原因是选中会话时读会话详情（`session-history.svelte.ts` 合并详情那段）会按详情把这一行会话摘要逐个字段重写，字段里没有 `playbook_id`。信使的原文件也一样漏写，只是 Tauri 窗口打开会话后紧跟着发「已读」，守护进程回来的 `session.upsert` 带着引擎又补上了；后台的 NSH 窗口不发已读，就一直是默认。现在两边都把详情里的 `playbook_id` 抄过去（null 也抄，换回默认是一种选择；详情里没有这个字段的旧守护进程就留着原来的值）。`runtime-sync.test.ts` 加了一条，没改时它拿到的是 undefined。

二是视频和音频在预览里显示「文件不在了」，日志是 `video: unsupported URL`。加载器把文件读成 `blob:` 地址交给 `Video`，信使的 `<video>` 能播，NSH 桌面上的播放器（macOS 是 AVPlayer，`nsh-platform-macos` `native.rs` 的 `locate`）只开文件路径和 http(s) 地址。现在桌面上运行时在本机时，播放器拿到的是文件的绝对路径（`ui/overlays/media-source.ts`：工作区里的相对路径接在 `workspace_path` 后面，项目会话的文件本来就是绝对路径；附件的内容就是同一个文件，`/v1/attachments/:id/content` 也是按路径去读）；web 和远程运行时照旧用 blob。路径还要过 `nsh.json` 的 `capabilities.fs.read`（用户目录和 `/Volumes`），工作区放在别处时 `onError` 报 `ERR_ACCESS_DENIED`，预览照旧说文件不在了。能播以后又看到播放器只有 96pt 高、竖版片子缩成一条：信使靠 `<video>` 自己的比例排版（`max-height: 100%`、`flex: 0 1 auto`），NSH 的 `Video` 没有自己的尺寸，原来的 `flex-1` 又落在一个不撑满的外层里。现在视频时外层撑满预览格，框的宽高由 `onLoad` 给的 `size` 算（`ui/annotations/media-fit.ts`）：按比例占满宽度，比格子剩下的高度（减去时间轴和按钮）高就缩小并居中，最小 96pt。

检查：在演示数据上开后台检查副本，不再用重新 PUT 引擎的绕法，任务标签写「引擎：视频 · 这个会话自己选的」；预览格打开交付的 `dawn_final.mp4`，屏幕截图里是片子的第一帧，竖版按比例撑满格子，时间轴和「在此处批注」在下面。演示数据在 `/private/tmp` 下，检查用的那份构建产物的 `capabilities.json` 临时加了这个目录，仓库里的 `nsh.json` 没改。`nsh check`、新加的 `media-source`、`media-fit` 单测、`annotations-annotators`、`artifacts-files`、`job-view-board`、`plan-job` 界面测试、信使全部测试（4045）、`port-messenger --check` 都通过。

Taking the README's shots on the NSH build showed two faults, and you asked for them to be fixed.

First, a job on a video group showed "Engine: General · The conversation names none: the default" in its tab. The snapshot had the group's `playbook_id` as `video` (`/v1/snapshot` and a fresh messenger page both had it). A temporary log in a check copy showed the row turning null right after the snapshot landed. Selecting a conversation reads its detail, and `session-history.svelte.ts` rebuilds that row of the summary list from the detail field by field; `playbook_id` was not among them. The messenger's original has the same gap. In the Tauri window the read sent right after opening a conversation brings back a `session.upsert` with the engine, which hid it; the NSH window in the background sends no read, so the default stayed. Both now take `playbook_id` from the detail (null too, since going back to the default is a choice; a daemon whose detail has no such field keeps the row's). A case in `runtime-sync.test.ts` covers it; without the fix it got undefined.

Second, a video or an audio file in the preview said the file was gone, with `video: unsupported URL` in the log. The loader turns the file into a `blob:` URL for `Video`. The messenger's `<video>` plays that, but NSH's desktop player (AVPlayer on macOS, `locate` in `nsh-platform-macos` `native.rs`) opens only file paths and http(s) URLs. On a desktop with the runtime on this computer the player now gets the file's absolute path (`ui/overlays/media-source.ts`). A workspace path is joined to `workspace_path`, a project session's file is already absolute, and an attachment's content is the same file, which `/v1/attachments/:id/content` also reads by its path. The web and a remote runtime keep the blob. The path must pass `capabilities.fs.read` in `nsh.json` (the home folder and `/Volumes`). A workspace elsewhere gets `ERR_ACCESS_DENIED` through `onError`, and the preview still says the file is gone. Once it played, the player was 96 pt high, a portrait film squeezed to a strip. The messenger lays the video out by its own shape (`max-height: 100%`, `flex: 0 1 auto`), but NSH's `Video` has no size of its own, and its `flex-1` sat in a wrapper that did not fill the pane. For a video the wrapper now fills the pane, and the box is worked out from the `size` that `onLoad` reports (`ui/annotations/media-fit.ts`): across the width at the video's shape, smaller and centred when taller than the room left after the timeline and the buttons, 96 pt at least.

Checked in a background check copy on the demo data, without re-PUTting the engine: the job tab reads "Engine: Video · Chosen for this conversation", and the preview opened on the delivered `dawn_final.mp4` shows the film's first frame in a screen capture, the portrait picture filling the pane at its shape, the timeline and "Annotate here" below it. The demo data lives under `/private/tmp`, so that check build's `capabilities.json` had the folder added for the check; `nsh.json` in the repository is unchanged. `nsh check`, the new `media-source` and `media-fit` unit tests, the screen tests `annotations-annotators`, `artifacts-files`, `job-view-board` and `plan-job`, the whole messenger suite (4045) and `port-messenger --check` pass.

## 补记 2026-11-11（三十）：web 包启动就 panic / Addendum 2026-11-11 (30): the web build panicked on start

给 README 截手机图时，`nsh package App.tsx --target web` 打出来的包在 Chromium 和 WebKit 里一打开就是「This app could not start」，控制台是 `nsh-platform-web/src/lib.rs:1138` 的 `RefCell already borrowed`。11-07 的手机 web 截图（0.4.0）还是好的。只有一行字的空应用用同一个 CLI 打包不崩，所以是应用触发的。把栈深开到 200 看到整条链：按需加载的 wasm 模块装好后，JS 调 `nsh_after_gate`，NSH 在 `send` 里拿着整个应用提交界面操作；其中一条是宿主调用（`op_cmd` → `hostCall`），结果当场经 `eventJson` → `nsh_event` 再进 `send`，第二次借用就 panic。给页面的 host.js 打日志，这条宿主调用是 `shell.keepers`（补记十九之后的「点别处让输入框失焦」那套，`src/platform/press.ts`）：web 上 `host.available()` 也为真，可页面的 `web/host.js` 只答 `runtime.endpoint`，不认识的名字当场 `throw`。

两处都改了：`press.ts` 用 `shell()`（`platform.os !== "web" && host.available()`）决定装不装按下转发、报不报 keepers，web 上没有外壳，这套整个不装；`web/host.js` 的 `call` 改成 async，以后不认识的名字也是稍后拒绝，不会在 NSH 拿着应用时同步回来。单换 host.js（应用照旧调 `shell.keepers`）已经不崩；两处一起重新打包，启动时页面不再收到任何宿主调用，手机宽度显示会话列表和底栏。NSH 在提交里同步投递宿主调用的结果会重入，这一点在上游（钉住的 7a2c11e0），这里只是不再触发它。

While taking the phone shots for the README, the package from `nsh package App.tsx --target web` opened at "This app could not start" in Chromium and WebKit, with `RefCell already borrowed` from `nsh-platform-web/src/lib.rs:1138` in the console. The phone web shots of 11-07 (on 0.4.0) were fine. A one-line app packaged with the same CLI did not panic, so the app set it off. With the stack limit raised to 200 the whole chain showed. When a lazily loaded wasm module is ready, JS calls `nsh_after_gate`, and NSH commits UI ops inside `send` while it holds the app. One of them is a host call (`op_cmd` → `hostCall`) whose answer came straight back through `eventJson` → `nsh_event` into `send` again, and the second borrow panicked. Logging the page's host.js named the call: `shell.keepers`, from the press-elsewhere-takes-the-keyboard work after addendum 19 (`src/platform/press.ts`). On the web `host.available()` is true too, but the page's `web/host.js` answers only `runtime.endpoint` and threw on any other name in the caller's turn.

Both sides are changed. `press.ts` decides with `shell()` (`platform.os !== "web" && host.available()`) whether to install the press forwarding and report keepers; the web has no shell, so none of it is installed there. `web/host.js`'s `call` is async, so a name it does not know is refused a moment later and never comes back while NSH holds the app. Swapping in the new host.js alone (the app still calling `shell.keepers`) already stopped the panic. With both, a fresh package asks the page for nothing at start and shows the conversation list and the tab bar at a phone's width. NSH re-entering when a host call's answer is delivered synchronously during a commit is upstream (the pinned 7a2c11e0); this only stops setting it off.

## 补记 2026-11-12（三十一）：窗口小了，分屏不再被挤出窗口 / Addendum 2026-11-12 (31): split panes stay in a small window

你截图说多分屏时窗口一小，右侧的分屏内容直接看不到了：对话、对话、用量三格并排，最右的用量格只剩左边一条，「刷新」和账号卡片都在窗口外。信使的工作台每个分屏是一条 `minmax(min(<下限>px, <按比例的份额>), <权重>fr)` 的网格轨道（`trackTemplate`），`allocate()` 在「所有下限加起来都放不下」时也是把每个下限按同一比例缩（"Shrink everyone in proportion … rather than letting the last track overflow the container"）。NSH 版把轨道换成 flex 子项时，`WorkbenchBranch` 给每格写的是 `minWidth = minSize(子项)`，硬下限，不随窗口缩；下限加起来比工作台宽，flex 缩不动，后面的格子就从右边溢出，被 `overflow-hidden` 裁掉。

现在 `WorkbenchBranch` 从工作台拿到每个分支的矩形（`Workbench` 本来就为分隔条算 `computeGeometry`，`branchRect` 传下去），用 `cell-floors.ts` 的 `cellFloors` 算各格的下限：都放得下时就是各自的最小值；放不下时都乘上 `可分的长度 / 下限之和`，和 `allocate()` 那一支逐个对得上（单测比对了两者），分隔条的把手也就还在格子的接缝上。嵌套的分支各按自己的长度再算一遍，和网格一层层嵌套时一样。

检查：后台检查副本、隔离运行时，侧栏 320、对话 | 对话 | 用量三格，窗口 1100×760。改之前两个对话各占 360，用量格只露出 55pt 左右（「用量」两个字也截了）；改之后三格约 280 / 280 / 218 都在窗口里，用量格的边框完整；窗口拉到 1600 时三格按原来的 0.36 / 0.36 / 0.28 分，各自的最小值又起作用。用量页本身在 218pt 宽时，「5 小时」这类窗口名被挤没、「已用 11%」超出卡片边一点：那是用量行自己的排法（信使 `UsageBar.svelte` 是 `minmax(0,1fr) auto auto`，窄了也一样溢出），这次没动。故事 `workbench` 加了「tight」开关（所有格子的下限 1000，单第一格就比窗口宽），界面测试 `workbench.test.tsx` 在 900×700 的窗口里打开它，要求右侧那列的标签都还看得见、按得到：退回旧的下限时这条失败，改后通过。`nsh check`（App 和 Story）、`cell-floors` 单测、`workbench`、`workbench-resize`、`workbench-drag`、`workbench-menus`、`shell` 界面测试、`bun test scripts` 和行数预算都通过。

You sent a screenshot: with several split panes, a small window left the right-hand pane's content out of sight. Three panes sat side by side (a conversation, a conversation and the usage page), and the usage pane showed only a strip at its left, with its Refresh button and account cards outside the window. In the messenger each pane is a grid track `minmax(min(<floor>px, <proportional share>), <weight>fr)` (`trackTemplate`), and `allocate()` shrinks every floor by the same ratio when they cannot all fit ("Shrink everyone in proportion … rather than letting the last track overflow the container"). When the NSH port turned the tracks into flex items, `WorkbenchBranch` gave each cell `minWidth = minSize(child)`, a hard floor that does not shrink with the window. Once the floors added up to more than the workbench, flex could not shrink them, and the last cells overflowed to the right and were cut off by `overflow-hidden`.

`WorkbenchBranch` now gets each branch's rect from the workbench, which already ran `computeGeometry` for the dividers; `branchRect` hands it down. `cellFloors` in `cell-floors.ts` works out the cells' floors: each child's own minimum while all of them fit, and otherwise each multiplied by `extent / sum of floors`. That matches `allocate()`'s branch for this case cell by cell (a unit test compares the two), so the dividers' handles still sit on the seams. Nested branches do the same with their own extent, as nested grids did.

Checked in a background check copy on an isolated runtime, with a 320 pt sidebar, three panes (conversation | conversation | usage) and a 1100×760 window. Before, the two conversations took 360 each and the usage pane showed about 55 pt, its title cut. After, the three are about 280 / 280 / 218, all inside the window, the usage pane's border whole. At 1600 wide the panes go back to 0.36 / 0.36 / 0.28 and their own minimums apply again. The usage page itself, at 218 pt, loses a window's name ("5 小时") and pushes "已用 11%" a little past its card's edge. That is the usage row's own layout (the messenger's `UsageBar.svelte` is `minmax(0,1fr) auto auto` and overflows the same way when narrow) and is left as it is. The `workbench` story gained a "tight" switch (every floor 1000, the first pane alone wider than the window), and `workbench.test.tsx` opens it in a 900×700 window and requires the right column's tabs to show and take a press. It fails with the old floors and passes with this. `nsh check` (App and Story), the `cell-floors` unit tests, the screen tests `workbench`, `workbench-resize`, `workbench-drag`, `workbench-menus` and `shell`, `bun test scripts` and the line budget pass.

## 补记 2026-11-12（三十二）：在消息上右键又能打开消息菜单 / Addendum 2026-11-12 (32): a right click on a message opens its menu again

你截图说在 Bot 输出的消息上右键打不开右键菜单，只弹出灰掉的「Copy」和「Select All」。这就是补记二十八顺带记下、当时没改的问题。NSH 的右键从按下的那一点往外找（`input.rs` 的 `context_menu`），先碰到选区就弹 NSH 自己的菜单，不再往外找。Bot 回复的 `msg-reply`、你的气泡 `msg-bubble`、系统卡 `system-card`、提问卡 `ask-bubble` 都是 `selectable` 视图，里面的 `MarkdownBody` 也各自是一个选区，而消息菜单的 `onContextMenu` 挂在外面的 `msg-…` 行上。所以只有回复底下那一行（用时和时间，在选区外）能打开消息菜单。

现在这四处的选区视图用 `transcript-parts.tsx` 的新函数 `lineSelection` 铺上 `selectable`、`onSelectionChange` 和 `onContextMenu`。同一视图上监听先于选区，右键打开这条消息的菜单。里面不再有自己的选区：回复和气泡的 `MarkdownBody` 写 `selectable={false}`，字照样并进外层选区；`WorkQuestionCard` 去掉了自己的 `selectable`，它只出现在系统卡里，嵌套的选区本来就并进外层。信使右键时读窗口的选区，选区在这条消息里时，菜单的「复制内容」只复制选中的部分；NSH 版以前不读（「NSH has no selection to ask for」）。现在 `onSelectionChange` 报来的字记在这一行里。它是普通变量，不是 signal：没有东西画它，选区的框一动就会再报一次。右键时这段字随位置交给 `openMenu`，桌面上去掉首尾空白后作为 `selectedText`；手指打开的菜单照旧不带，和信使在触摸时清掉选区一样，那时菜单里有「选择文字」。别的消息里有选区时，这一行报的是空字符串，复制的是整条，和信使只认「选区在这条消息里」一样。代价：在消息上右键不再有 NSH 的「Look Up」；拷贝用菜单里的「复制内容」或 ⌘C。

检查。无头测试的 `rightClick` 不看选区（补记二十八），`chat-stage` 的「a secondary press on a line opens its menu」修前修后都过，所以新加了 `scripts/line-menu.test.ts`。它读这四个行组件，和它们选区里画的组件（引用、附件、批注卡、提问表单、控制按钮、工作问题卡、编辑框）的源码，要求每一行用 `lineSelection`，没有 `<View>` 自己写 `selectable`，`MarkdownBody`、`TextView`、`TurnStepList` 都写 `selectable={false}`，也没有 `select-text` 的字。拿 main 的源码跑它报出 11 处，改后通过。

真窗口：后台检查副本（自己的 bundle id）加了一个临时请求 `rclick x y`，给第一个窗口发右键按下和抬起，并把 `+[NSMenu popUpContextMenu:withEvent:forView:]` 换成只记日志。`chat-stage` 故事打了修前、修后两个包，都在 `openMenu` 和选区上临时记日志。隔离运行时里，Writer 的私聊有你的一行、一条带行内代码、短行、列表和代码块的回复、一条应用的提示和一条提问。窗口 1100×1040，两次各右键同样 15 个点。修前，你的气泡、回复的字、行内代码、短行、短行旁的空白、列表、代码块的字和空白、系统卡、提问卡的问题和状态，这 12 处都是 `["Copy", "Select All"]`，只有你那行的时间和回复的底栏打开消息菜单。修后这 12 处都打开各自消息的菜单，消息 id 和坐标都对得上，底栏和时间照旧。输入框两次都没有记下这两种菜单：输入框留着 AppKit 自己的菜单，它不经过这个日志，所以那个菜单本身没有看到。气泡左边的空白两次都没有菜单。

拖选：从回复第一段拖到「短行。」，修前修后报出的字逐字相同。在选区里右键，修前是 Copy、Select All 和 Look Up，修后是带着这段字的消息菜单。回复里有选区时右键系统卡，菜单带的是空字符串，复制整条。在你的气泡里拖选后右键，菜单带着选中的「摘要，顺便说说 P」。另外两次看旧选区会不会跟着走：回复里有选区，在别处按一下再右键回复，菜单带空字符串；回复里有选区，再在你的气泡里拖选，然后右键回复，也是空字符串。选区一离开这一行，它就报来空字符串。截图里消息菜单在点击处打开，选区仍然高亮。

工作中的气泡和审批卡上右键仍是 NSH 的拷贝菜单。信使里它们本来就没有消息菜单：`TranscriptBotGroup.svelte` 只给 `item.type === 'message'` 的段落开菜单，`TranscriptApproval.svelte` 不接右键。还能回答的卡片里的输入框没有在真窗口里右键过（故事里的提问已经结束）。

其他平台只读了规则，没有运行：Windows、Linux 的 `context_menu` 和网页的 `nsh.js` 也是同一视图上先看监听、再看选区。网页上 `TextView` 写 `selectable={false}` 时不给字设值（`nsh-autoselect`，不是 `nsh-noselect`），所以字在选区里照样可选；手机上长按回复的字，以前是浏览器自己的选择，现在打开消息菜单的底部弹层，和信使一样。临时改动都在一次性的 worktree 里，已撤掉。`nsh check`（App、Story）、`bun test scripts`（28）、`port-messenger --check`、`theme-from-tokens --check`、行数预算都通过；界面测试全部 88 个文件 702 个测试通过，2 个终端测试因为 worktree 里没有 pty helper 照例跳过。

You sent a screenshot: a right click on a Bot's message did not open its menu, only a greyed "Copy" and "Select All". This is the fault addendum 28 noted on the way and left unchanged. NSH's right click looks outward from the point pressed (`context_menu` in `input.rs`), and the first selection it meets shows NSH's own menu and ends the search. A Bot reply's `msg-reply`, your bubble's `msg-bubble`, the system card `system-card` and the question card `ask-bubble` are all `selectable` views, and the `MarkdownBody` inside each is a selection of its own too, while the message menu's `onContextMenu` sits on the `msg-…` row outside them. So only the line under a reply (its time taken and its time, outside the selection) opened the message's menu.

These four selectable views now spread `selectable`, `onSelectionChange` and `onContextMenu` from a new function, `lineSelection` in `transcript-parts.tsx`. On the same view the listener comes before the selection, so a right click opens that message's menu. Nothing inside is a selection of its own. The reply's and the bubble's `MarkdownBody` say `selectable={false}`, and their words still join the outer selection. `WorkQuestionCard` lost its own `selectable`: it appears only in the system card, and a nested selection joins the outer one anyway. The messenger reads the window's selection on a right click, and when the selection is in that message the menu's 复制内容 copies only the selected part; the NSH build did not read it ("NSH has no selection to ask for"). Now the text `onSelectionChange` reports is kept per line. It is a plain variable, not a signal: nothing draws it, and it is reported again whenever the selection's box moves. A right click hands it to `openMenu` with the position, and on a desktop it becomes `selectedText`, trimmed. A menu opened by a finger still carries none, as the messenger clears the selection on touch; that menu offers 「选择文字」. With the selection in another message this line reports an empty string, and the whole message is copied, as the messenger only counts a selection inside the message. The cost: a right click on a message no longer offers NSH's Look Up. Copy with the menu's 复制内容 or ⌘C.

Checks. The headless `rightClick` ignores selections (addendum 28), and `chat-stage`'s "a secondary press on a line opens its menu" passed before and after, so `scripts/line-menu.test.ts` is new. It reads the source of the four row components and of what they draw inside their selection (the quote, attachments, annotation cards, the question's form, the control buttons, the work question card, the edit field). It requires each row to use `lineSelection`, no `<View>` to say `selectable` on its own, `MarkdownBody`, `TextView` and `TurnStepList` to say `selectable={false}`, and no `select-text` text. Run on main's source it reports 11 places; with the fix it passes.

In a real window: a background check copy with its own bundle id had a temporary `rclick x y` request that posts a right button down and up to its first window, and replaced `+[NSMenu popUpContextMenu:withEvent:forView:]` with a log line. The `chat-stage` story was built twice, before and after, each with temporary logs in `openMenu` and on the selection. On an isolated runtime, Writer's direct held a line of yours, a reply with inline code, a short line, a list and a code block, an app note and a question. In a 1100×1040 window the same 15 points were right-clicked in each build. Before, 12 of them gave `["Copy", "Select All"]`: your bubble, the reply's words, its inline code, the short line, the blank beside it, the list, the code block's text and its blank, the system card, and the question card's question and status. Only your line's time and the reply's foot opened the message menu. After, all 12 opened their own message's menu, with matching message ids and points, and the foot and the time were unchanged. The composer logged neither menu both times: a field keeps AppKit's own menu, which does not pass this log, so that menu itself was not seen. The blank left of your bubble gave no menu either time.

Selection: a drag from the reply's first paragraph to 「短行。」 reported the same text, character for character, before and after. A right click inside it gave Copy, Select All and Look Up before, and after it gave the message menu carrying that text. With the reply selected, a right click on the system card carried an empty string, so the whole message is copied. A drag inside your bubble and a right click there carried the selected 「摘要，顺便说说 P」. Two more probes checked that an old selection does not ride along. With the reply selected, a press elsewhere and then a right click on the reply carried an empty string. With the reply selected and then a drag in your bubble, a right click on the reply carried an empty string too. Once the selection leaves a line, the line reports an empty string. A capture shows the message menu open at the point clicked, with the selection still highlighted.

The working bubble and the approval cards still give NSH's Copy menu on a right click. In the messenger they have no message menu either: `TranscriptBotGroup.svelte` opens one only for a part whose `item.type === 'message'`, and `TranscriptApproval.svelte` takes no right click. A field in a card that can still be answered was not right-clicked in a real window (the story's question had ended).

Other platforms were read, not run. The Windows and Linux `context_menu` and the web's `nsh.js` also check the listener before the selection on the same view. On the web, `TextView` with `selectable={false}` sets nothing on its texts (`nsh-autoselect`, not `nsh-noselect`), so the words still select inside the area. A long press on a reply's words on a phone gave the browser's own selection before; now it opens the message menu's sheet, as the messenger does. The temporary changes lived in throwaway worktrees and are gone. `nsh check` (App and Story), `bun test scripts` (28), `port-messenger --check`, `theme-from-tokens --check`, and the line budget pass, and all 88 screen test files pass (702 tests; the 2 terminal tests skip as usual, with no pty helper in the worktree).

## 补记 2026-11-12（三十三）：项目会话的 `/` 列表是空的；命令后面接什么也列出来 / Addendum 2026-11-12 (33): a project session's `/` list was empty; what a command takes is offered too

你截图说项目会话里打 `/`，命令候选列表是空的；又说有些命令要紧接着给出后面的输入作为选项，比如 `/advisor` 要接着列出模型。

列表空的原因在应用里。守护进程这边没问题：这个会话的 `GET /v1/project-sessions/:id/commands` 答了 62 条命令。命令存在 `project-commands.svelte.ts` 的 `deep()` 仓库里，只打 `/` 时 `matchCommands` 原样交出仓库里那个数组，也就是深层代理，`ProjectCommandMenu` 的 `<Each>` 吃代理数组什么都画不出来（PORTING.md 早有这条）。键位提示只在有匹配时才画，所以菜单里只剩一行提示。多打一个字母时 `matchCommands` 返回新建的普通数组，列表就出来了。现在 `Each` 拿的是 `matches.slice()`。复现过程：后台检查副本里，同一个菜单放进 Overlay，一份给普通数组、一份从 `deep()` 仓库取，前者有 62 行，后者只剩提示，和截图一样；`chat-stage` 故事的项目会话里打 `/` 是空的，打 `/a` 有列表。PORTING.md 原来说 Overlay 里的 `Each` 画不出东西，这次 Overlay 里给普通数组的 `Each` 画得出来，那条改成先查代理数组。

后面的选项来自 Claude Code 自己写的参数提示：`/advisor` 是 `[fable|opus|sonnet|off]`，`/fast` 是 `[on|off]`，`/code-review` 开头是 `[low|medium|high|xhigh|max]`。新文件 `ui/chat/command-arguments.ts` 从提示的第一组里读出备选（`a|b|c` 的每一项取第一个词，`<tokens>` 这样的占位和词组不算），没有 `|` 的提示（`[name]`、`<file>`、`key=value`，Codex 的 `[instructions]`）不给选项。用这个会话的 62 条提示核对过：给出选项的是 `/advisor`、`/fast`、`/code-review`、`/mcp`（reconnect、enable、disable）、`/design`（consent、revoke）和 `/autocompact`（只有 auto）。这些都在单测里。输入框里光标在命令名后面第一个词里、命令在列表里（名字或别名）时，`CommandChoiceMenu` 列出以已打内容开头的选项，顶上是命令名和说明。几条规则：在命令列表里选这类命令（按、Tab 或回车）都先填进去、接着出选项，不直接发出；选项一开始不高亮，回车照样把命令原样发出（这些参数都是可选的），打了字才高亮第一个；↑↓ 移动，Tab 填进去，回车选高亮的那个并照「回车发送」的设置发出；打的词已经就是唯一对得上的选项时列表收起；没有选项以它开头时列表不出现，按键照常。直接打 `/advisor ` 而命令列表还没读过时，也会去读。没改守护进程和协议；文案加了一条 `commandChoicesLabel`（信使和 NSH 共用）。Tauri 版（信使）没有这个功能。

检查：`chat-stage` 故事替项目会话回答命令列表（四条：无参数、自由参数、两条带选项），像它回答 `send` 一样，稍后再答，所以走的是和实际一样的仓库路径。界面测试 `chat-stage-project` 加了两条：打 `/` 出命令、选 `/advisor` 后出四个选项、打 `o` 缩小、选 `off` 发出 `/advisor off`；回车选 `/fast` 是填进去、再回车发出原样的命令、`/review 12` 不出菜单照原样发出。修之前第一条在等 `/compact` 那一步超时（屏幕上只有键位提示），第二条因为回车直接发出而失败。真窗口（后台检查副本、隔离运行时、同一个故事）：只打 `/` 列出四条；`/adv` 加 Tab 后输入框是 `/advisor `，下面 fable、opus、sonnet、off，没有高亮；↓ 加回车发出 `/advisor fable`；`/fast o` 列出 on（高亮）和 off；Esc 收起、文字留着；`/compact` 回车照旧发出；`/review 12` 原样发出。`nsh check`（App、Story）、`command-arguments` 单测（5）、全部 88 个界面测试文件（704 个测试，2 个终端测试照例跳过）、`bun test scripts`、`port-messenger --check`、信使的文案测试都通过。

You sent a screenshot: typing `/` in a project session showed an empty command list. You also asked that some commands offer what follows them, for example `/advisor` listing the models next.

The empty list was the app's. The daemon was fine: `GET /v1/project-sessions/:id/commands` answered 62 commands for that session. The commands live in a `deep()` store in `project-commands.svelte.ts`, and for a bare `/` `matchCommands` hands back the store's own array, a deep proxy. `ProjectCommandMenu`'s `<Each>` draws nothing from a proxy array (PORTING.md already said so). The key hints are drawn only when something matches, so the menu showed just them. One letter more and `matchCommands` returns a new plain array, so the list appeared. `Each` now gets `matches.slice()`. Reproduced in a background check copy: the same menu in an Overlay, one fed a plain array and one fed from a `deep()` store; the first showed 62 rows and the second only the hints, as in your screenshot. In the `chat-stage` story's project session, `/` was empty and `/a` listed commands. PORTING.md said `Each` draws nothing inside an Overlay; here `Each` drew from a plain array in an Overlay, so that line now says to look for a proxy array first.

The choices come from the argument hints Claude Code writes: `/advisor` is `[fable|opus|sonnet|off]`, `/fast` is `[on|off]`, and `/code-review` starts with `[low|medium|high|xhigh|max]`. A new file, `ui/chat/command-arguments.ts`, reads the alternatives of a hint's first group, each by its first word; placeholders like `<tokens>` and phrases are not choices. A hint with no `|` (`[name]`, `<file>`, `key=value`, Codex's `[instructions]`) offers none. Checked against the 62 hints of this session: `/advisor`, `/fast`, `/code-review`, `/mcp` (reconnect, enable, disable), `/design` (consent, revoke) and `/autocompact` (auto only) offer choices, and the unit tests hold these. With the caret in the first word after a command's name, and the command in the list by its name or an alias, `CommandChoiceMenu` lists the choices that start with what is typed, under the command's name and description. The rules: picking such a command in the command list (press, Tab or Enter) fills it in and its choices follow, never sending it bare. No choice is highlighted at first, so Enter still sends the command as it stands (these arguments are optional); typing highlights the first fit. ↑↓ move, Tab fills in, and Enter takes the highlighted one and sends as the Enter-sends setting says. When the typed word already is the one fit, the list closes; when no choice starts with it, there is no list and the keys behave as usual. Typing `/advisor ` before the command list was read also reads it. The daemon and the protocol are unchanged; the copy gains `commandChoicesLabel` (shared by the messenger and the NSH app). The Tauri app (the messenger) does not get this.

Checks: the `chat-stage` story answers the project session's command list (four: one taking nothing, one free text, two with choices) a moment after it is asked, as it answers `send`, so the real store path runs. Two screen tests in `chat-stage-project`: `/` lists the commands, picking `/advisor` shows its four choices, typing `o` narrows them, picking `off` sends `/advisor off`; Enter on `/fast` fills it in, Enter again sends the bare command, and `/review 12` opens no menu and is sent as typed. Before the fix the first timed out waiting for `/compact` (only the key hints on screen), and the second failed because Enter sent the command. In a real window (background check copy, isolated runtime, the same story): a bare `/` listed the four; `/adv` and Tab left `/advisor ` in the box with fable, opus, sonnet and off under it, none highlighted; Down and Enter sent `/advisor fable`; `/fast o` listed on (highlighted) and off; Esc closed it and kept the text; `/compact` and Enter sent as before; `/review 12` went as typed. `nsh check` (App and Story), the `command-arguments` unit tests (5), all 88 screen test files (704 tests; the 2 terminal tests skip as usual), `bun test scripts`, `port-messenger --check` and the messenger's copy tests pass.

## 补记 2026-11-12（三十四）：⌘K 搜索卡死 / Addendum 2026-11-12 (34): ⌘K search froze the window

你说搜索功能会直接卡死。

原因在应用里。`GlobalSearch.tsx` 照 PORTING.md 对照表把 `$derived` 写成了普通函数。`available()` 每读一次就把所有结果过一遍 `canOpenSearchHit`（每次都扫会话列表），而每一行都读它，一次渲染就是结果数的平方次判断。所有结果又在同一个跟踪插入里一次建成行，这个插入还读 `active()` 和整个快照，所以方向键、悬停、别处的每个事件都会重建整个列表。守护进程的 `/v1/search` 不限条数，一个字母就能搜到长历史里的大半消息：真实数据库上「a」1687 条、「的」2315 条（1–1.5 MB），守护进程本身 70–300 ms。Svelte 版的 `$derived` 是记忆化的，`#each` 带 key，WebKit 里一次建两千行也不慢，所以只有 NSH 版卡死。

改法：
- 派生值都换成 `computed`。`available` 只算已经建出来的行，答案没变时保持原值（`equals`）。
- 行改成按结果对象带 key 的 `Each`，选中和能否打开在每一行里读。
- 一次只建 50 行；最后一行进入离底部一屏之内，或方向键走过最后一行时，再加 50 行。
- 重置到第一条只跟着结果走，不再跟着快照走（以前别处有 Bot 回复，选中项就跳回第一条）。
- 方向键走到的行照 Svelte 版的 `scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' })` 露出来（悬停不滚），并算上列表上方计数那一行的高度（以前下边缘会藏在底边下面）。
- 结果还没全部建成行时，在第一条上按 ↑ 停在原地，不绕到已建的最后一行。

检查：
- `misc` 故事加了两个按钮，往搜索里放 2000 条结果，其中一个的前 49 条所在的会话已不存在。修之前，500 条就让一次 JS 调用超过测试的 2 秒上限而被打断。
- 两条新的界面测试：只有 50 行，滚到底变成 100 行；方向键从第 50 行走到第 51 行（行数变成 100），↑ 停在第一条能打开的结果上。
- 真实规模在数据库副本上量：`sqlite3 .backup` 复制，删掉 `remote_host`、`live_procs`，起隔离运行时，在同一个故事里输入。修之前「a」「的」都被 2 秒上限打断；修之后从输入到第一行分别 126 ms、202 ms，只建 50 行。临时计数显示，方向键和悬停不再新建行；会话更新后选中项不变（修之前跳回第一条）。
- `tsc`、`bun test scripts`、`port-messenger --check` 和全部 88 个界面测试文件都通过。

还剩：
- 真实数据里约 35% 的消息结果所在的会话不在快照里（灰着显示，打不开），最长连续 308 条（「的」）。方向键穿过这样一段时，一次按键会连着加几页（约 300 行），会顿一下，没有量过。
- `/v1/search` 仍不限条数，每次按键都传 1–1.5 MB。要限条数得改守护进程，改后要在空闲时重启运行时。

You said the search froze the window.

The cause was the app's. `GlobalSearch.tsx` had turned `$derived` into plain functions, as the PORTING.md table allowed. Each read of `available()` ran `canOpenSearchHit` over every hit (each scanning the sessions), and every row read it, so one render made hits² checks. Every hit got a row at once in one tracked insert, which also read `active()` and the whole snapshot, so each arrow key, hover and event elsewhere rebuilt the whole list. The daemon's `/v1/search` has no cap, and one letter finds most of a long history: on the real database "a" gives 1687 hits and "的" 2315 (1–1.5 MB), with 70–300 ms in the daemon. The Svelte version's `$derived` is memoised and its `#each` keyed, and WebKit makes two thousand rows quickly, so only the NSH build froze.

The fix:
- Every derived value is a `computed`. `available` covers only the rows there are and keeps its value while no answer changed (`equals`).
- Rows are a keyed `Each` by hit, reading selection and availability per row.
- 50 rows are made at a time; 50 more come when the last row is within a screen of the bottom, or when the arrows go past it.
- The reset to the first hit follows the hits, not the snapshot (a Bot replying elsewhere used to send the selection back to the first hit).
- The arrows reveal the row they reach as the Svelte version's `scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' })` does (hover does not scroll), counting the count line above the rows (a row's lower edge used to stay under the bottom).
- While not every hit has a row, ↑ on the first stays there rather than going round to the last row made.

Checks:
- Two `misc` story buttons put 2000 hits in the search, one with the first 49 in a conversation that is gone. Before the fix, 500 hits already ran one JS call past the test's 2 s budget, and it was interrupted.
- Two new screen tests: 50 rows, and 100 after scrolling to the end; the arrows go from row 50 to row 51 (100 rows), and ↑ stays on the first hit that can be opened.
- At real size, on a copy of the database: copied with `sqlite3 .backup`, `remote_host` and `live_procs` emptied, an isolated runtime, typing in the same story. Before the fix both "a" and "的" were interrupted at the 2 s budget. After it, the first row shows 126 ms and 202 ms after typing, with 50 rows made. A temporary counter showed no new rows on arrows or hover, and the selection staying put after a session update (it went back to the first hit before).
- `tsc`, `bun test scripts`, `port-messenger --check` and all 88 screen test files pass.

Left:
- About 35% of the message hits on the real data are in conversations not in the snapshot (shown greyed, not openable), with runs of up to 308 in a row ("的"). Arrowing down across such a run adds several pages in one press (about 300 rows), a hitch that was not measured.
- `/v1/search` is still uncapped, 1–1.5 MB per keystroke. Capping it is a daemon change, and the runtime then needs a restart while idle.

## 补记 2026-11-13（三十五）：消息里写到的文件右键能在 Finder 中打开 / Addendum 2026-11-13 (35): a file a message names opens in Finder from a right click

你要求 Bot 或项目 agent 输出的行内文件都能右键在系统默认的文件管理器中打开。文件块自己不听右键，按 NSH 的右键规则，右键落到它外面：Bot 的回复里是消息菜单（补记三十二），项目会话的回复里是窗格菜单（补记二十八），本身就是选区的 Markdown（正在写的 Bot 回复、子代理弹窗）里是 NSH 的「Copy / Select All」。只有左键会对文件做点什么：打开预览。

现在 `MarkdownBody` 多了 `files`（`ui/file-on-disk.ts` 的 `FileOnDisk`：`locate` 说文件在这台电脑的哪里，`open` 默认是宿主的 `open_workspace_path`）。传了它，`ArtifactChip`（文件块和图片卡）就在块本身和它的每段字上挂 `onContextMenu`：右键从按下的那一点往外找，同一视图上监听先于选区，所以块上的右键先到块，不会被选区或行菜单接走。菜单是 `ui/FileChipMenu.tsx`，复用文件树的 `ArtifactTreeMenu`，只给这一个文件适用的三项：「用系统默认应用打开」「在 Finder 中打开」「复制绝对路径」。菜单状态放在 `MarkdownBody` 里，不在块上：流式回复每来一段字块都会重做。哪里的路径按哪里找：Bot 对话里的（回复、正在写的回复、你的气泡、委派结果、手机的「选择文字」页）用 `workspaceFiles()`，和预览一样按工作区拼（`sanitizeCitedPreviewPath` + `absWorkspacePath`，`~/` 先问一次运行时的家目录）；项目会话的（回复、正在写的回复、计划卡、子代理弹窗）用 `project-chat-state` 的 `files()`，和左键的 `openCited` 一样先问 `ProjectCitedPaths`，答不出、答出几处、或者答回原样的相对路径（查询失败时它这样答），都提示 `citedMissing` / `citedAmbiguous`，不去打开。系统打不开（文件已被移走、删掉，宿主的 `check_openable` 拒绝）时提示新文案 `artifactNotOnDisk`。复制绝对路径和文件树一样不提示。

只在 Mac 上的本机窗口里给 `files`（`onThisComputer()`：macOS、不是远程客户端、有宿主桥）。手机和浏览器的页面、远程窗口里文件在另一台电脑上；Windows、Linux 的外壳还不回答 `open_workspace_path`：它在只在 macOS 编的 `host_calls.rs`、`system.rs` 里，`launch.rs` 给这两个平台答的几条 Tauri 命令里没有它，文件树的「在 Finder 中打开」在那里也一样打不开。这些地方不传 `files`，右键照旧是消息菜单。

检查。无头测试的 `rightClick` 不看选区，而且测试宿主里 `onThisComputer()` 为假，所以分三层。`shared` 故事给 Markdown 传了一个假的 `files`（文件在 `/ws` 下，`open` 只记下做了什么）；新屏幕测试右键文件块，菜单只有那三项，点「在 Finder 中打开」得到 `reveal:/ws/notes/weekly.md`，图片卡右键点「用系统默认应用打开」得到 `open:/ws/charts/trend.png`，没传 `files` 的气泡右键不出这个菜单。`scripts/file-chip-menu.test.ts` 读源码：`ui/chat` 里每个 `MarkdownBody` 都传 `files`，`ArtifactChip` 的两种块和每段字都挂 `onContextMenu`；去掉委派结果的 `files` 它就报出那一行。真窗口用补记三十二的办法：后台检查副本（自己的 bundle id）加临时的 `rclick` 请求，`NSMenu` 的右键菜单只记日志，`shared` 故事临时加了一行模仿 Bot 回复（`<View selectable>` 自己挂行菜单，里面 `MarkdownBody selectable={false}`），并在打开文件菜单和 `open` 上记日志。九个点：这一行里文件块的字和图标、Markdown 卡片里文件块的字和图标、图片卡的名字和缩略图，都打开文件菜单，路径和坐标对得上；这一行的普通字打开行菜单，Markdown 的普通字和没传 `files` 的气泡里的文件块是 NSH 的 `["Copy", "Select All"]`，和以前一样。两份菜单（一份在可选的行里，一份不在）按指针下方的偏移逐点点过：「用系统默认应用打开」在 +12 到 +30，「在 Finder 中打开」在 +38 到 +62，分别记下 `open` 和 `reveal` 和对的路径，两份一样。再用整个应用（`App.tsx`，不是故事）跑一次，只把默认的 `open` 临时换成记日志，免得在你的屏幕上打开 Finder：隔离运行时的工作区里放了 `notes/周报.md`、`docs/方案 v2.md`，Writer 私聊里一条回复写到它们、一个绝对路径和一个不存在的文件；一个 Claude Code 项目会话里，agent 的回复写到只给了文件名的 `工具.py`（实际在 `src/` 下）和不存在的 `src/没有.py`。在对话列表的行里右键这些文件块：工作区的两个文件和绝对路径都解析成磁盘上确实存在的路径，`reveal` 和 `open` 各对；不存在的那个按工作区拼出路径（真宿主的 `check_openable` 会拒绝并提示，这里没走到）；项目会话里 `工具.py` 经 `locate` 找到 `src/工具.py`，`src/没有.py` 提示「在项目目录里没找到」、什么也不打开；回复里的普通字右键既不是文件菜单也不是 NSH 的拷贝菜单（消息菜单、窗格菜单照旧）。另外看到一处原有的行为：反引号里带空格的路径（`notes/周报 草稿.md`、`src/主程序 v1.py`）不会被认成文件块，只是行内代码，所以也没有这个菜单；写成 `[名字](<带 空格.md>)` 的链接是文件块。「复制绝对路径」没在真窗口里点，免得改掉你的剪贴板。真的 `open -R` 没跑：那会在你的屏幕上打开 Finder；它是文件树一直在用的同一个宿主调用。临时改动都在一次性的 worktree 和 `/tmp` 里，已撤掉。`nsh check`（App、Story）、`bun test scripts`、`port-messenger --check`、`theme-from-tokens --check`、行数预算、信使的 `svelte-check` 和文案测试都通过，全部屏幕测试通过（707 个，0 失败）。

You asked that every inline file a Bot or a project agent writes open in the system's file manager from a right click. The chip itself did not listen to a right click, so by NSH's rule the click went to what is around it: the message's menu in a Bot's reply (addendum 32), the pane's menu in a project session's reply (addendum 28), and NSH's "Copy / Select All" in Markdown that is a selection of its own (a Bot's reply still being written, the subagent popup). Only a left click did anything with the file, opening its preview.

`MarkdownBody` now takes `files` (`FileOnDisk` in `ui/file-on-disk.ts`: `locate` says where the file is on this computer, `open` defaults to the shell's `open_workspace_path`). Given it, `ArtifactChip` (the file chip and the picture card) listens to `onContextMenu` on the chip and on each of its texts. A right click looks outward from the point pressed and, on one view, the listener comes before the selection, so a right click on a chip reaches the chip before any selection or the line's menu. The menu is `ui/FileChipMenu.tsx`, which reuses the file tree's `ArtifactTreeMenu` with the three entries that apply to one file: "Open with default app", "Show in Finder", "Copy absolute path". Its state lives in `MarkdownBody`, not on the chip, since a streamed reply makes its chips again with every piece. Each place resolves paths its own way. A Bot conversation's (replies, the reply being written, your bubble, delegation results, the phone's text page) use `workspaceFiles()`, joined onto the workspace as the preview does (`sanitizeCitedPreviewPath` + `absWorkspacePath`; for `~/` the runtime's home is asked once). A project session's (replies, the reply being written, the plan card, the subagent popup) use `files()` from `project-chat-state`, which asks `ProjectCitedPaths` as the left click's `openCited` does. When it has no answer, several answers, or the relative path back as written (what it answers when the lookup failed), the app says `citedMissing` / `citedAmbiguous` and opens nothing. When the system cannot open the file (moved or deleted since, so the shell's `check_openable` refuses), the new string `artifactNotOnDisk` says so. Copying the absolute path says nothing, as in the file tree.

`files` is given only in a window on this Mac (`onThisComputer()`: macOS, not a remote client, with the shell's bridge). On the phone's and the browser's page and in a remote window the file is on another computer. The Windows and Linux shells do not answer `open_workspace_path` yet: it lives in `host_calls.rs` and `system.rs`, which build on macOS only, and the few Tauri commands `launch.rs` answers on those platforms do not include it, so the file tree's "Show in Finder" cannot open anything there either. There a right click still opens the message's menu.

Checks. The headless `rightClick` ignores selections, and `onThisComputer()` is false in the test host, so the checks come in three layers. The `shared` story hands its Markdown a fake `files` (files under `/ws`, `open` only noting what it was asked). A new screen test right-clicks a chip: the menu has just the three entries, "Show in Finder" gives `reveal:/ws/notes/weekly.md`, the picture card's "Open with default app" gives `open:/ws/charts/trend.png`, and a right click in the bubble without `files` shows no such menu. `scripts/file-chip-menu.test.ts` reads the source: every `MarkdownBody` under `ui/chat` passes `files`, and both kinds of chip and each of their texts listen to `onContextMenu`; with the delegation result's `files` removed it names that line. The real window used addendum 32's method: a background check copy with its own bundle id, a temporary `rclick` request, `NSMenu`'s context menu replaced by a log line. The `shared` story temporarily gained a line like a Bot's reply (a `<View selectable>` with its own line menu around a `MarkdownBody selectable={false}`) and log lines on opening the file menu and on `open`. Nine points were right-clicked. The chip's text and icon in that line, the chip's text and icon in the Markdown card, and the picture card's name and thumbnail all opened the file menu, with matching paths and points. The line's plain words opened the line menu. The Markdown's plain words and the chip in the bubble without `files` gave NSH's `["Copy", "Select All"]`, as before. Both menus (one inside a selectable line, one not) were clicked point by point below the pointer: "Open with default app" answers from +12 to +30 and "Show in Finder" from +38 to +62, logging `open` and `reveal` with the right path, the same in both. Then the whole app (`App.tsx`, not a story) ran once, with only the default `open` swapped for a log line so Finder would not open on your screen. The isolated runtime's workspace held `notes/周报.md` and `docs/方案 v2.md`. A reply in Writer's direct named them, an absolute path and a file that does not exist. In a Claude Code project session, the agent's reply named `工具.py` by its bare name (it is under `src/`) and the missing `src/没有.py`. Right clicks on these chips in the conversation's list rows went as follows. The two workspace files and the absolute path resolved to paths that exist on disk, with `reveal` and `open` each right. The missing one was joined onto the workspace (the real shell's `check_openable` refuses it with a notice; not reached here). In the project session, `locate` found `工具.py` at `src/工具.py`, and `src/没有.py` gave "not found in the project directory" and opened nothing. A right click on a reply's plain words gave neither the file menu nor NSH's Copy menu (the message and pane menus as before). One existing behaviour showed up too: a path with a space inside backticks (`notes/周报 草稿.md`, `src/主程序 v1.py`) is not made a chip, only inline code, so it has no such menu; a link written `[name](<with space.md>)` is a chip. "Copy absolute path" was not clicked in the real window, to leave your clipboard alone. The real `open -R` was not run, since it would open Finder on your screen; it is the same shell call the file tree has always used. The temporary changes lived in a throwaway worktree and `/tmp` and are gone. `nsh check` (App and Story), `bun test scripts`, `port-messenger --check`, `theme-from-tokens --check`, the line budget, the messenger's `svelte-check` and copy tests pass, and the whole screen test suite passes (707 tests, 0 failed).

## 补记 2026-11-13（三十六）：暗色下窗格标签栏和亮色一样放在毛玻璃上 / Addendum 2026-11-13 (36): in dark, a pane's tab strip lies on the glass as in light

你说暗色模式的 tab 页要参考亮色不带背景。我先理解成选中的标签页，只去掉了它在色带上的底（f79b9af2）；你纠正说是整个窗格头部，要和亮色主题一样不加颜色。

以前，窗口有材质（macOS）时，暗色把固定窗格的标签栏画成卡片的实色头部（`WorkbenchLeaf` 的 `head()`）：标签栏下面垫一条 `bg-ground` 色带（开着玻璃内容时是它的玻璃比例），离窗格边 4 pt，卡片只圆下角，`Shell` 的主区域顶上因此少留 4 pt。这是 2026-10-19 定下的：暗色玻璃透得多，会随窗口后面的东西变色，两个窗格的标签栏曾一蓝一橙。ADR 0106 那句「暗色下标签栏是卡片的头部，跟着变成玻璃」说的也是它。亮色一直没有这条带：标签栏放在窗口的地面（`bg-ground-glass`）上，选中的标签页是卡片色，和卡片连在一起。

现在：
- 去掉头部，暗色和亮色走同一套结构：标签栏在窗口地面上，选中的标签页是卡片色（开着玻璃内容时是它的玻璃比例加发丝边），卡片四角都圆，主区域顶上 6 pt。f79b9af2 的「选中标签页不画底」一起撤回，`darkGlass()` 删掉。NSH 版在这一点上不再照 ADR 0106 那句和信使的 `WorkbenchLeaf.svelte` 做；信使现在只在手机网页上用，那里没有材质，没改它。
- 代价：标签栏和侧栏、窗格之间的缝隙一样，随窗口后面的东西变色，两个窗格的标签栏可以不同色。
- 没选中的标签页名字落到暗色玻璃上，`tab-idle-ink` 只剩 2.9–3.5:1（窗口后面是浅灰、橙色时），亮色是 4.5:1。和侧栏的灰字一样提亮一级：固定窗格的标签栏（`LeafStrip` 给出 `InDockedStrip`）在暗色、有材质时用 `ink-muted-glass`（`glass.ts` 的 `idleTabInk()`，`PaneTabLabel`、`ChatTabLabel` 用它）。亮色、浮动窗格、手机仍是 `tab-idle-ink`。标签栏上的图标（`tint-ink-muted`）按它的颜色值和量到的三种地面算，在 3.6–5.4:1，图形够 3:1，没动。

检查：后台检查副本（自己的 bundle id）加隔离运行时，两个窗格（一个当前、一个变暗），窗口正下方垫一块自绘的背景窗口（左橙右蓝、浅灰、深色），用 `screencapture -R` 拍窗口后面真实透上来的玻璃（拍之前确认前面没有别的窗口、窗口位置没变）。
- 暗色，开和不开玻璃内容：标签栏的地面和窗格缝隙同色，随背景变（蓝 (32,41,67)、灰 (62,62,61)、深 (33,33,32)；以前固定在 (11,14,19) 上下）；卡片顶边（y 92/93，2x 像素）和标签行的位置与亮色逐行相同；没选中的名字 5.0–7.5:1，和侧栏的时间字一样；变暗窗格里 2.4–3.0:1（以前 2.45 左右，亮色 1.91）；选中的名字 13.8–14.5:1。
- 亮色：同样的背景下，改前改后整个窗口逐像素相同；两次改前之间本来就有三小块头像渲染的差别，不算在内。
- `nsh check`、`tsc`、`bun test scripts`（39 个）、`port-messenger --check`、`theme-from-tokens --check`、`nsh test`（194 个通过，1 个跳过）都通过，全部 88 个界面测试文件通过（711 个，0 失败）。

You said dark mode's tabs should follow light's and have no ground. I first took it to mean the active tab and only removed its ground on the band (f79b9af2); you corrected me: the whole pane head should have no colour of its own, as in the light theme.

Before, on a window with material (macOS), dark drew a docked pane's tab strip as the card's solid head (`head()` in `WorkbenchLeaf`): a `bg-ground` band under the strip (its glass share with glass content on), 4 pt in from the pane's edges, the card rounded at the bottom only, and `Shell`'s main column 4 pt less from the top to make room. That was decided on 2026-10-19: dark glass lets so much through that it takes the colour of whatever is behind the window, and two panes' strips had come out one blue and one orange. ADR 0106's sentence "in dark the tab strip, the card's head, turns glass with it" is about the same thing. Light never had the band: its strip lies on the window's ground (`bg-ground-glass`), and the active tab is the card's colour, joined to the card.

Now:
- The head is gone, and dark is built as light is: the strip lies on the window's ground, the active tab is the card's colour (its glass share and the hairline with glass content on), the card is rounded at all four corners, and the main column starts 6 pt from the top. f79b9af2's active tab with no ground goes with it, and so does `darkGlass()`. Here the NSH build no longer follows that sentence of ADR 0106 or the messenger's `WorkbenchLeaf.svelte`; the messenger is used only as the phone's web page now, where there is no material, and is unchanged.
- The cost: like the sidebar and the gaps between panes, the strip takes the colour of whatever is behind the window, so two panes' strips can differ in tint.
- On dark glass the names of the tabs not shown came out at only 2.9–3.5:1 in `tab-idle-ink` (with a light grey or an orange backdrop), where light reads about 4.5:1. They are raised a step as the sidebar's grey text is: in a docked pane's strip (`LeafStrip` provides `InDockedStrip`), dark on the material uses `ink-muted-glass` (`idleTabInk()` in `glass.ts`, used by `PaneTabLabel` and `ChatTabLabel`). Light, floating panes and the phone keep `tab-idle-ink`. The strip's icons (`tint-ink-muted`), worked out from their colour on the three grounds measured, come to 3.6–5.4:1, above the 3:1 a graphic needs, and are unchanged.

Checks: a background check copy (its own bundle id) on an isolated runtime, two panes (one current, one dimmed), with a backdrop window of our own right under the window (orange left and blue right, light grey, dark) and `screencapture -R` taking the real glass through the window (with nothing over it and the window unmoved, both checked first).
- Dark, with glass content on and off: the strip's ground is the gaps' and follows the backdrop (blue (32,41,67), grey (62,62,61), dark (33,33,32); it stayed near (11,14,19) before). The card's top edge (y 92/93 in 2x pixels) and the tab row sit on the same rows as in light. Names of tabs not shown read 5.0–7.5:1, the same as the sidebar's times; in the dimmed pane 2.4–3.0:1 (about 2.45 before, 1.91 in light). The active name reads 13.8–14.5:1.
- Light: over the same backdrops the whole window is pixel-identical before and after, apart from three small spots of avatar drawing that already differ between two runs of the old build.
- `nsh check`, `tsc`, `bun test scripts` (39), `port-messenger --check`, `theme-from-tokens --check` and `nsh test` (194 passed, 1 skipped) pass, and all 88 screen test files pass (711 tests, 0 failed).

## 补记 2026-11-16（三十七）：窗格的标签之间留出间距 / Addendum 2026-11-16 (37): a pane's tabs keep a gap between them

你说 tab 与 tab 之间要留一点间距。以前标签一个挨一个：鼠标停在一个标签上，它的底色直接贴着旁边选中的那个，看上去连成一块。信使的标签也是挨着的，中间画一条细分隔线；NSH 版没有这条线，固定窗格的标签又是标题栏地面上的圆角块，挨在一起更显眼。

现在（`leaf-strip.tsx`）：
- 每个标签放在一个格子里，格子左右各留半个间距（`TAB_GAP` 4 pt 的一半），两个标签之间就是 4 pt。桌面和手机（窄屏的标签带）都一样。
- 格子之间仍首尾相接，标签宽度量的是格子。`drop-zones.ts` 按宽度累加算拖放落点，这样分界正好在间距中间，落点和指示条不用改，滚动到可见、两端箭头的计算也不用改。
- 标签行起点和 + 前面的边距从 4 pt 改成 2 pt，第一个标签和 + 都不挪位置。
- 间距算标签行的，不算标题栏：`windowDrag: false` 从标签外框移到格子上。否则在平铺窗格里，从标签上滑出一点按在间距里，就会拖走整个窗口，双击还会缩放窗口（补记二十二）。右键在间距里照旧冒泡到标签栏，是窗格菜单。

检查：后台检查副本（自己的 bundle id，只回健康检查的假运行时）跑 `workbench` 故事。一个临时 bundle 让所有标签都画悬停底色，在 2x 截图上沿标签中线量：三个标签在 8–203、212–371、380–539 px，两两之间都是 8 px，即 4 pt。第一个标签离窗格左边 4 pt，和以前一样。窄到 600 pt（手机布局）时，选中的标签仍在 8–203 px，上圆角、和下面的卡片连着。间距里按下不拖窗口这一点没在实机上看：系统拖窗口跟的是真鼠标，检查不动你的鼠标。`nsh check App.tsx`、`bun test scripts`（39 个）通过；全部 88 个界面测试文件 706 个通过，`settings-routing-modal` 的「awaiting a retry」失败 1 次，单独重跑通过（它本来就偶尔失败）。

You said tabs should keep a little gap between them. They sat edge to edge: a tab under the pointer ran straight into the active one beside it, and the two read as one block. The messenger's tabs touch too, with a hairline between them; the NSH build has no hairline, and a docked pane's tabs are rounded blocks on the title bar's ground, where touching shows more.

Now (`leaf-strip.tsx`):
- Each tab sits in a slot with half a gap on either side (half of `TAB_GAP`, 4 pt), so two tabs are 4 pt apart, on the desktop and on a phone's narrow band alike.
- The slots still meet edge to edge, and a tab's measured width is its slot's. `drop-zones.ts` adds widths up to find where a drop lands, so the boundary falls in the middle of the gap: the drop target and its bar need no change, nor does scrolling a tab into view or the row's end arrows.
- The margin before the row and before the + goes from 4 pt to 2 pt, so the first tab and the + stay where they were.
- The gap belongs to the tab row, not the title bar: `windowDrag: false` moves from the tab's frame to its slot. Otherwise, in a tiled pane, a press that slipped off a tab into the gap would move the whole window, and a double press would zoom it (addendum 22). A right click in the gap still bubbles to the strip and opens the pane's menu.

Checks: a background check copy (its own bundle id, on a fake runtime that answers only the health probe) on the `workbench` story. A throwaway bundle painted every tab with its hover ground, and a scan along the tabs' middle line in the 2x snapshot found the three tabs at 8–203, 212–371 and 380–539 px: 8 px, or 4 pt, between each pair. The first tab is 4 pt from the pane's left edge, as before. At 600 pt wide (the phone layout) the active tab is still at 8–203 px, rounded at the top and joined to the card. That a press in the gap does not move the window was not seen on a real window: the system's window drag follows the real pointer, and the check never moves yours. `nsh check App.tsx` and `bun test scripts` (39) pass; all 88 screen test files ran with 706 passed and one failure, `settings-routing-modal`'s "awaiting a retry", which flakes now and then and passed when run again alone.

## 补记 2026-11-17（三十八）：0.5.0 起 Mac 和 Windows 上发的是原生版 / Addendum 2026-11-17 (38): from 0.5.0 the Mac and Windows releases are the native app

你让发新版本，发 NSH 版（Windows 那部分在后面，是你看过 Mac 的进展后说的）。在这之前 NSH 版只有开发版的打包（`package.ts`：「Deskfolk Dev」、`.nsh-dev` 的 bundle id，运行时是这台 Mac 上的 bun 跑源码），发布流水线打的仍是 Tauri 包。这次补上的是正式版：

- **身份在编译时定。** `DESKFOLK_RELEASE` 编出的宿主（`host/src/config.rs` 的 `RELEASE`）就是 Deskfolk：名字、`com.real-bot.desktop`、标准数据目录、17890、`com.real-bot.daemon`，和 Tauri 版一模一样，所以它顶替 Tauri 版，对话、设置、已配对的手机都接着用。`deskfolk-nsh --identity` 报出自己是哪一种，打包时核对。
- **守护进程在包里。** `package.ts --release` 先跑 Tauri 版的 `build-native.ts`（守护进程、pty、运行时和 WebRTC 助手、凭据库，各自签好），放进 `Contents/Resources/native/`，和 Tauri 版的位置一样（守护进程按自己的位置找助手）。宿主启动时没有运行时就起它，带上远程配对通道（`remote_setup::prepare_channel`，补记前面记的缺口之一）；之后每秒看一次，没了、也没有别的持有者时再起（Tauri 的 `supervisor.rs` 那一套，窗口总在监管，因为独立运行时仍然是关着的）；退出时停掉它，SIGTERM 或者认证过的 `/v1/runtime/quit`。起不来时弹窗给出 `daemon.log` 的位置。
- **令牌每次启动都换。** 守护进程每次启动发一个新令牌（`mintLocalToken`），宿主原来把窗口打开时的地址一直用下去，运行时一重启窗口就连不上（`--stable-instance` 下重启源码运行时也是这样）。现在 `tauri.local_api_endpoint`、`runtime.endpoint` 和通知循环每次都重读 `local-api.json`；信使的连接循环本来就每次连接前重问地址（`connection-loop` 的 `discoverEndpoint`）。
- **更新原地换。** 0.4.0 的更新检查只认 `Deskfolk_<版本>_<aarch64|x64>.dmg`，安装器只核对 bundle id 和版本，所以 dmg 用这个名字、`Info.plist` 用这两样，装着 Tauri 版的 Mac 就在「关于」里换成原生版。用 v0.4.0 标签里一字未改的 `installer.rs` 对本机打出的 dmg 走了一遍（挂载、找 app、核对、拷出、交换脚本）：换上的是 `deskfolk-nsh`，签名校验通过，临时文件清掉。原生版自己的 `installer.rs` 和它一样，应用内更新经 `terminate:` 退出，守护进程跟着停。
- **流水线。** `release.yml` 在原有的 macOS 全套验证之外，加上原生版自己的检查（`cargo test --workspace`、`nsh check`、`nsh test`；界面测试不在这里跑：试跑时有两个只在 macOS runner 上过不了——`misc-search` 几千条结果超过 30 秒的上限，`remote-screen` 触摸板的滑动被读成按下——它们在 Linux runner 和这台 Mac 上都过，`nsh-desktop.yml` 每次推 main 都在 Linux 上跑它们；打标签时 nsh 命令行要重编，再加上界面测试会超过任务的时限。`ui-tests.ts --retry` 让失败的文件再跑一次），再分别打 Apple 芯片和 Intel 两个 dmg：签名、公证、装订，Gatekeeper 必须认作 Notarized Developer ID（打包前先用同一组凭据问一次公证服务，账号有协议没接受时几秒内就失败）；两个都在 runner 上带着自己的守护进程真起一次（Intel 那个经 Rosetta）（`--smoke`：第一份快照，守护进程是包里那个且带配对通道，杀掉后被重新拉起、窗口换新令牌重连，退出时一起停）。两个都成了才由最后一个任务建 release。nsh 命令行按钉住的 NSH 提交在 macOS 上编一次（约 30 分钟）并缓存。
- **Windows。** 你说 Windows 版你测过、可以发。`package.ts --release` 在 Windows 上打 `Deskfolk_<版本>_x64-setup.exe`（`scripts/installer.nsi`，NSIS）：装进 Tauri 版的文件夹 `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Deskfolk`，写同一个卸载项（Tauri 模板的 `...\Uninstall\Deskfolk`），主程序用这个卸载项记下的 `MainBinaryName`（Tauri 版装过就沿用它的名字，否则 `Deskfolk.exe`），所以开始菜单和桌面的快捷方式、任务栏固定、开机启动都还指着它；装之前先请窗口关掉、再结束守护进程和助手；数据目录不动。0.4.0 在 Windows 上的更新检查本来就只打开浏览器去下载，所以 Windows 上的「更新」就是下载这个安装包、运行它。宿主在安装目录找 `app\bundle.nshc` 和 `native\real-bot-daemon.exe`，起守护进程不开控制台，退出时先经 API 请它退出；release 构建是窗口程序。Windows 上还没有配对通道（Tauri 版的 stdin/stdout 那一条没搬过来），`remote_local_setup` 一律答 `disabled`：设置里的配对设备、设备列表、中继都用不了，配对过的手机照常能连。安装包不签名。`--smoke` 在 Windows runner 上静默安装、起装好的应用、杀掉守护进程看它被重新拉起并重连、关窗口看守护进程跟着停、静默卸载，确认程序和卸载项没了、数据目录还在；而且先静默装上最新一版的安装包再装新的（0.5.0 发版时就是 Tauri 的 0.4.0，它的主程序叫 `real-bot-desktop.exe`）：新的主程序沿用这个名字，卸载项的版本换成新的，开始菜单的快捷方式还在，起来的是正式版。
- **限制。** NSH 的最低系统是 macOS 14，Tauri 版是 13：0.4.0 的更新器不看系统版本，macOS 13 上点了更新会换上一个打不开的应用，数据不受影响，重新装回 0.4.0 即可。更新日志的标题写明需要 macOS 14，「关于」卡片里也看得到。Linux 这一版不发包（原生版还是骨架），装着的停在 0.4.0，文档改成指向 0.4.0 的安装包。
- **没在真机上走过的。** 这台 Mac 的 17890 一直被你自己的运行时占着，正式版在这里只会连上它、起不了自己的守护进程，所以完整的启动只在 CI 的 runner 上验过；在新设备上配对手机、从 0.5.0 往后的应用内更新、正式版的系统通知，都还没有实际走过。

You asked for a new release, the NSH build (the Windows part came after, once you had seen the Mac's). Until now the NSH build packaged only as the dev build (`package.ts`: "Deskfolk Dev", a `.nsh-dev` bundle id, a runtime that is this Mac's bun running the source), and the release workflow still packaged Tauri. This adds the release:

- **Identity at build time.** A shell built with `DESKFOLK_RELEASE` (`RELEASE` in `host/src/config.rs`) is Deskfolk: its name, `com.real-bot.desktop`, the standard data directory, 17890 and `com.real-bot.daemon`, exactly the Tauri app's, so it takes the Tauri app's place and conversations, settings and paired phones carry on. `deskfolk-nsh --identity` says which build it is, and packaging checks it.
- **The daemon inside.** `package.ts --release` runs the Tauri app's `build-native.ts` first (the daemon, the pty, runtime and WebRTC helpers and the credentials library, each signed) and puts the result in `Contents/Resources/native/`, where the Tauri app kept it (the daemon finds its helpers beside itself). The shell starts it when no runtime answers, with the remote setup channel (`remote_setup::prepare_channel`, one of the gaps noted earlier); watches it once a second and starts it again when it is gone and nothing else holds the data directory (the Tauri app's `supervisor.rs`: the window always supervises, as the independent runtime stays off); and stops it on quit, by SIGTERM or the authenticated `/v1/runtime/quit`. When it cannot start, a dialog names `daemon.log`.
- **A new token each start.** The daemon mints a token every time it starts (`mintLocalToken`), and the shell kept the address its window opened with, so a restarted runtime left the window unable to connect (restarting the source runtime under `--stable-instance` did the same). `tauri.local_api_endpoint`, `runtime.endpoint` and the notification loop now read `local-api.json` on every ask; the messenger's connection loop already asks for the address before each connection (`discoverEndpoint` in `connection-loop`).
- **Updating in place.** 0.4.0's update check looks only for `Deskfolk_<version>_<aarch64|x64>.dmg`, and its installer checks only the bundle id and the version, so with that name and those two `Info.plist` keys a Mac on the Tauri app swaps to the native app from About. The v0.4.0 tag's `installer.rs`, unchanged, was run on a dmg packaged here (attach, find the app, verify, copy out, the swap script): the swapped app is `deskfolk-nsh`, its signature verifies and the staging is cleaned up. The native app's own `installer.rs` is the same file; an in-app update quits through `terminate:` and the daemon stops with it.
- **The workflow.** Beside the existing macOS verification, `release.yml` runs the native app's own checks (`cargo test --workspace`, `nsh check`, `nsh test`; not the screen tests: in the dry runs two missed on the macOS runner alone — `misc-search`'s thousands of hits ran past its 30 s limit and `remote-screen`'s trackpad slide read as a press — while they pass on the Linux runner and on this Mac, `nsh-desktop.yml` runs them on Linux on every push to main, and on a tag, which builds the nsh CLI afresh, they would take the job past its time limit; `ui-tests.ts --retry` runs a failing file once more), then packages an Apple silicon and an Intel dmg: signed, notarized and stapled, with Gatekeeper required to say Notarized Developer ID (the notary service is asked first with the same credentials, so an account with an agreement left to accept fails in seconds); each is launched once on the runner with its own daemon (the Intel one under Rosetta) (`--smoke`: the first snapshot, the daemon is the app's own with the setup channel, killed it is started again and the window reconnects on the new token, and a quit stops it). Only when both exist does the last job create the release. The nsh CLI is built once on macOS for the pinned NSH commit (about 30 minutes) and cached.
- **Windows.** You said you had tested the Windows build and it can ship. On Windows `package.ts --release` builds `Deskfolk_<version>_x64-setup.exe` (`scripts/installer.nsi`, NSIS): it installs into the Tauri app's folder, `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Deskfolk`, under the same uninstall entry (the Tauri template's `...\Uninstall\Deskfolk`), and names the main program after that entry's `MainBinaryName` (the Tauri app's name when it was installed, `Deskfolk.exe` otherwise), so the Start menu and desktop shortcuts, a taskbar pin and the launch at login still point at it; it asks the window to close and then ends the daemon and its helpers before copying; the data folder is left alone. 0.4.0's update check on Windows only ever opened the download in the browser, so an update there is this installer, downloaded and run. The shell finds `app\bundle.nshc` and `native\real-bot-daemon.exe` in its install folder, starts the daemon without a console and asks it to quit over its API on the way out; a release build is a windowed program. Windows has no setup channel yet (the Tauri app's stdin/stdout one was not carried over), and `remote_local_setup` answers `disabled` to everything: pairing a device, the device list and the relay in Settings are not available there, while phones paired before keep connecting. The installer is unsigned. `--smoke` on the Windows runner installs it silently, starts the installed app, kills the daemon and sees it started again and the window reconnect, closes the window and sees the daemon stop, and uninstalls silently, checking the program and its entry are gone and the data folder is not; and it first installs the latest release's installer silently and goes over it (for 0.5.0 the Tauri app's 0.4.0, whose main program is `real-bot-desktop.exe`): the new main program keeps that name, the entry's version is the new one, the Start menu shortcut stays, and what starts is the release.
- **Limits.** NSH needs macOS 14 and the Tauri app ran on 13: 0.4.0's updater does not check the system version, so on macOS 13 the update swaps in an app that will not open; the data is untouched, and installing 0.4.0 again brings it back. The changelog's heading says macOS 14, and so does the About card. Linux gets no package in this release (the native build is a skeleton); installs there stay on 0.4.0, and the docs point at 0.4.0's packages.
- **Not walked on a real machine.** This Mac's 17890 is always held by your own runtime, so a release here only connects to it and never starts its own daemon; the whole start-up was checked on the CI runner only. Pairing a phone on a fresh install, an in-app update from 0.5.0 on, and the release's system notifications have not been tried for real.

## 补记 2026-11-17（三十九）：0.5.1，Windows 的安装位置和开机启动 / Addendum 2026-11-17 (39): 0.5.1, Windows's install folder and launch at login

0.5.0 发出去以后复查到两处 Windows 的问题，你让修：

- **安装位置。** 0.5.0 的安装包总装进 `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Deskfolk`，而 Tauri 版的安装包有选文件夹的一页、重装时沿用上次的位置。装在别处的 0.4.0 会多出一份，旧快捷方式（安装包见到已有的就留着）还打开 0.4.0。现在 `.onInit` 读卸载项的 `InstallLocation`（两边都带引号写），那里有主程序就装到那里；命令行给了 `/D=` 时照 `/D=`。
- **开机启动。** Tauri 版的自启动在当前用户的 `Run` 键下写了 `Deskfolk`，带 `--hidden`；原生版在 Windows 上不看 `--hidden`，`set_launch_at_login` 也只答 `false`，所以从 Tauri 版升上来的每次登录都弹窗口，设置里也关不掉。现在 `--hidden` 让窗口隐藏启动（NSH 的 `window.defaultVisible: false`：窗口建好、应用在里面跑，等通知区域图标、开始菜单或第二次启动把它显示出来）；`set_launch_at_login` 写入或删掉 `Run` 值（`"<程序>" --hidden`，`win_login.rs`）；正式版启动时读设置 `launch_at_login`（默认开）把这个值对齐，所以升级上来的会改指向现在的程序、按设置开关；卸载时删掉。
- **单实例。** 隐藏启动以后，从开始菜单再打开一次是常见的事，原来会起第二份（两个通知区域图标，关掉其中一个还会停掉另一个在用的守护进程）。现在正式版打开 NSH 的 `single_instance`：后来的那次把参数交给正在运行的那份、让它显示窗口，然后退出。这时 `run_app` 会返回（平常它自己结束进程，不返回），宿主就只停自己起的守护进程，撤掉自己挂上的图标。
- **检查。** Windows 冒烟现在把两种升级都走一遍：先把最新发布的安装包（0.5.0）、再把 0.4.0 的（最后一个 Tauri 版）各自静默装到一个自选文件夹，然后装新的：必须装进那个文件夹、默认位置不能多出一份；以 `--hidden` 启动，拿到第一份快照时没有可见窗口；`Run` 值是 `"<程序>" --hidden`；第二次启动退出、正在运行的那份的窗口显示出来、守护进程还在；之后照旧杀掉守护进程看它被拉起和重连、关窗口看它停、卸载后 `Run` 值也没了。

After 0.5.0 went out, two Windows problems turned up on review, and you asked for them fixed:

- **The install folder.** 0.5.0's installer always went to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Deskfolk`, while the Tauri app's installer has a page to choose a folder and reuses the last one. A 0.4.0 installed elsewhere got a second copy, and its old shortcuts (the installer keeps ones it finds) still opened 0.4.0. `.onInit` now reads the uninstall entry's `InstallLocation` (both write it quoted) and installs there when the main program is there; a `/D=` on the command line wins.
- **Launch at login.** The Tauri app's autostart wrote `Deskfolk` under the current user's `Run` key, with `--hidden`; the native app ignored `--hidden` on Windows and its `set_launch_at_login` only answered `false`, so an install that came over the Tauri app opened its window at every login and Settings could not turn that off. Now `--hidden` starts the window hidden (NSH's `window.defaultVisible: false`: the window is made and the app runs in it until the notification area icon, the Start menu or a second launch shows it); `set_launch_at_login` writes or removes the `Run` value (`"<program>" --hidden`, `win_login.rs`); a release reads the `launch_at_login` setting (on by default) as it starts and brings the value in line, so an upgraded install points it at the program that is there and follows the setting; uninstalling removes it.
- **One instance.** With a hidden start, opening the app again from the Start menu becomes the usual thing, and it started a second copy (two icons in the notification area, and closing one stopped the daemon the other used). A release now turns on NSH's `single_instance`: the later launch hands its arguments to the running copy, which shows its window, and exits. `run_app` returns then (it otherwise ends the process itself), so the shell stops only a daemon it started itself and takes down the icon it put up.
- **Checks.** The Windows smoke now goes both ways up: the latest release's installer (0.5.0), then 0.4.0's (the last Tauri app), each installed silently into a folder of its own, then the new one, which must land in that folder with no second copy in the default place; a `--hidden` start whose first snapshot comes with no visible window; a `Run` value of `"<program>" --hidden`; a second launch that exits while the running copy's window shows and its daemon stays; then, as before, the daemon killed, started again and reconnected, the window closed with the daemon stopping, and an uninstall that also takes the `Run` value away.

## 补记 2026-11-17（四十）：手机版有了 `/sw.js`；web 包又能编了 / Addendum 2026-11-17 (40): the phone's page has its `/sw.js`; the web package builds again

接 11-10 列的切换前缺口第一条：NSH 的 web 包没有 `/sw.js`，而手机上的推送、点通知、应用角标和远程媒体都经它。

- **打包。** `scripts/phone-package.ts` 在 `nsh package Hosted.tsx --target web` 之后把信使 `static/` 里的 `sw.js`、它载入的 `media-stream.js`、通知用的 `icon-192.png`、`notification-badge.png` 和主屏幕图标拷进包里，manifest 补上图标，`index.html` 补上 `apple-touch-icon`（iPhone 只给加到主屏幕的网页推送），改过的两个文件重写它们的 `.br` / `.gz`。`phone-web-e2e.ts` 打包时自己做。
- **注册。** 应用跑在页面里一个隐藏的 `about:blank` 框架里（Phase 1c），worker 管的是页面：点通知时它把页面拉到前面、给页面发 `{ type: "inbox" }`。所以手机版启动时（`src/platform/page-service-worker.ts`，`hosted.ts` 调）把 realm 的 `navigator.serviceWorker` 换成页面的那个，并注册 `/sw.js`；信使的 `remote/push.ts`、`remote/media-source.ts` 和运行时的推送监听不改，注册、等 `ready`、订阅、收 worker 的消息都落在页面上，不管哪个浏览器怎么对待 `about:blank` 框架自己的容器。
- **web 包编不过。** 2adbf01b、f35a2906 让 Markdown 分块和 PDF 解析走 NSH 的 `blocking`，它在 WebAssembly 里没有，`deskfolk-app-native` 的 web 版编不过，手机版从那时起打不了包。`app/native` 在 wasm 上补一个就地执行的 `blocking`（`.await` 拿到的和桌面的 `JoinHandle` 一样是 `Ok`）。
- **检查。** 端到端在 Chromium 和 WebKit 里都过：重载以后页面归 `/sw.js` 管，应用的 realm 里 `ready` 解出来、带 `pushManager`，控制它的是同一个 worker，这几份文件都 200；两边控制台都干净。测试的 Chromium 要 `--ignore-certificate-errors`：边缘的证书是这一轮现做的，Chromium 只从它信任的来源取 worker 的脚本。没走的：真的收一条推送、点一条通知，要推送服务和真机。

Continuing the first gap listed before the switch on 11-10: NSH's web package had no `/sw.js`, which web push, a tapped notification, the app badge and remote media all go through on the phone.

- **Packaging.** `scripts/phone-package.ts` runs after `nsh package Hosted.tsx --target web`: it copies the messenger's `static/` `sw.js`, the `media-stream.js` it loads, the `icon-192.png` and `notification-badge.png` a notification shows and the home-screen icons into the package, gives the manifest its icons and `index.html` its `apple-touch-icon` (an iPhone gets web push only for a page added to the home screen), and writes the two changed files' `.br` / `.gz` again. `phone-web-e2e.ts` does it as it packages.
