- **Registration.** The app runs in a hidden `about:blank` frame of the page (Phase 1c), and the worker is the page's: a tapped notification brings the page forward and posts it `{ type: "inbox" }`. So as the phone build starts (`src/platform/page-service-worker.ts`, called by `hosted.ts`) the realm's `navigator.serviceWorker` becomes the page's and `/sw.js` is registered; the messenger's `remote/push.ts`, `remote/media-source.ts` and the runtime's push listener stay as they are, and registering, waiting for `ready`, subscribing and hearing the worker all happen on the page, whichever way a browser treats an `about:blank` frame's own container.
- **The web package did not build.** 2adbf01b and f35a2906 moved Markdown's blocks and PDF parsing onto NSH's `blocking`, which WebAssembly does not have, so `deskfolk-app-native` did not build for the web and the phone could not be packaged from then on. `app/native` gives wasm a `blocking` that runs in place (its `.await` gives `Ok`, as the desktop's `JoinHandle` does).
- **Checks.** The end-to-end run passes in Chromium and WebKit: after the reload the page is under `/sw.js`, the app's realm gets `ready` with a `pushManager` and the same worker as its controller, and each of the files answers 200; both consoles stay clean. The test's Chromium takes `--ignore-certificate-errors`: the edge's certificate is made for the run, and Chromium fetches a worker's script only from an origin it trusts. Not walked: a real push received or a notification tapped, which need a push service and a phone.

## 补记 2026-11-17（四十一）：Windows 上的通知由应用经 NSH 弹 / Addendum 2026-11-17 (41): on Windows the app shows the notifications, through NSH

Windows 原生版一条通知都不弹（Tauri 版在 Windows 上也没有，`FallbackNotificationAdapter`）。原来的打算是在 Windows 外壳里照 macOS 再写一套 toast；读 NSH 才知道它的 `Notifications` 在 Windows 上已经做全了：WinRT 的 toast，`run_app` 把应用 id 设成进程的 AppUserModelID，在 `HKCU\Software\Classes\AppUserModelId` 下登记名字、图标和听按下的 COM 类，正式版还写 `LocalServer32`，应用关了也能被按下拉起；角标是任务栏按钮上的覆盖图标。照「NSH 有的用 NSH 的」，不在外壳里另写。

- **投递循环在应用里。** `app/src/platform/app-banners.ts` 是 macOS 外壳 `notification_loop.rs` 那一轮的 TS 版，路由和请求体不变：每两秒读注意力计数放到角标上（设备偏好关了角标就是 0），清理版本变了就问运行时哪些横幅该撤、`close` 掉，再认领一条、复核、`Notifications.show`、回报。只在第一个窗口里跑，Windows 和 Linux 上才跑。横幅的 NSH id 是「运行时的 identifier｜click_ref」：同一会话的新横幅先关掉旧的（macOS 上 identifier 直接替换），按下时拿回 click_ref，应用被按下拉起时 NSH 先存着、等应用开始听。
- **命令由桥回答。** 这两个平台上 `tauri-bridge.ts` 先于外壳回答 `notification_permission_state`、`request_notification_permission`、`take_notification_intent`（权限是 NSH 的；`unavailable` 时照旧说不投递），`report_notification_view` 照旧问外壳要窗口的事实，再自己把在场报给运行时（那里没有 macOS 外壳的在场队列）。权限要是一直不答，五秒后算 `unavailable`：信使的状态轮询等着它，轮询停住的话会话就又一条都读不了（5b37220a 修过的那个）。`nsh.json` 加了 `notifications` 能力，这个只决定 JS 能不能调，macOS 上不调。
- **检查和没走的。** 九条单元测试（假运行时、假 `Notifications`）；macOS 上 `appShowsBanners` 为假，界面测试照旧。这台 Mac 编不了 Windows 宿主，也没在 Windows 上弹过一条、按过一条，所以先放在分支上，等 Windows 上看过再合。

The native Windows app showed no notifications at all (nor did the Tauri app on Windows: `FallbackNotificationAdapter`). The plan was another toast stack in the Windows shell, after the macOS one; reading NSH showed its `Notifications` already does it all on Windows: WinRT toasts, `run_app` making the app id the process's AppUserModelID and registering under `HKCU\Software\Classes\AppUserModelId` the name, the icon and the COM class that hears presses, a release also writing `LocalServer32` so a press starts the app once it has ended; the badge is the taskbar button's overlay icon. As with everything NSH has, the app uses NSH's rather than the shell growing its own.

- **The delivery loop is in the app.** `app/src/platform/app-banners.ts` is the macOS shell's `notification_loop.rs` pass in TS, the same routes and bodies: every two seconds the attention count goes on the badge (0 while the device's preference turns it off), stale banners are closed when the cleanup revision moves, and one notification is claimed, revalidated, shown with `Notifications.show` and reported. It runs in the first window only, and only on Windows and Linux. A banner's NSH id is "the runtime's identifier|click_ref": a newer banner for the same conversation closes the one before it (on macOS the identifier replaces it), a press hands back the click_ref, and a press that starts the app waits in NSH until the app listens.
- **The bridge answers the commands.** On these platforms `tauri-bridge.ts` answers `notification_permission_state`, `request_notification_permission` and `take_notification_intent` before the shell does (the permission is NSH's; while it is `unavailable` it says there is no delivery, as before); `report_notification_view` still asks the shell for the window's facts, then tells the runtime where the reader is itself (there is no macOS presence queue there). A permission that never answers counts as `unavailable` after five seconds: the messenger's state poll waits on it, and a poll that stopped would leave every conversation unread again (what 5b37220a fixed). `nsh.json` gains the `notifications` capability, which only lets the JS call it; macOS does not.
- **Checks, and what is not walked.** Nine unit tests (a fake runtime, a fake `Notifications`); on macOS `appShowsBanners` is false and the screen tests are as they were. This Mac cannot build the Windows host, and no toast has been shown or pressed on Windows yet, so this stays on a branch until it has been seen there.

## 补记 2026-11-17（四十二）：终端换成 NSH 自己的 `Terminal` / Addendum 2026-11-17 (42): the terminal is NSH's own `Terminal`

补记（十八）把 NSH 的 `Terminal` 接在 `--terminal nsh` 后面，默认仍是画布终端，等上游补齐四处：⌘ 键（longbridge/nsh#1633）、正好 80×24 时的第一次尺寸（#1638）、组字画在光标处（#1648）、宽字符对格（#1649）。四个都已合并，都在现在钉的 93df449 里。你说「终端换掉」，于是：

- **默认换成它。** 工作台的终端标签（`PaneTerminal`）和 `--pane terminal`（`Tools.tsx`）都用 `NshTerminal`。`--terminal nsh` 没了；`--terminal native`（Phase 0 的 NSView 终端）留作对照。
- **画布终端删掉。** 删的是 `app/src/Terminal.tsx`（`CanvasTerminal` 和它自己的键表）、`app/native/src/terminal.rs`（`TerminalGrid`、`Terminals`）和 `vt100` 依赖。`palette.rs` 只留 `RuntimeEmulator` 用的十六色和底色。`encode`、`terminalSender` 搬进 `NshTerminal.tsx`。
- **兜底也删掉。** 80×24 的兜底（#1630）不要了：有了 #1638，第一次布局总会报尺寸。`RuntimeTerminals` 改成具名导入：补记（十八）以后编的宿主都有它，正式版也有。
- **滚轮。** 画布终端靠外壳转来的滚轮（`onWheel`）翻历史。NSH 的 `Terminal` 是虚拟列表，历史和对话列表一样走原生滚动。Windows 的 `win_wheel` 不再为终端转发，看图、地图等还用它。
- **新得到的：** 拖选文字和复制（NSH 的选择区）。程序打开 bracketed paste 时，粘贴的内容整段包起来发。
- **打包仍不带 Ghostty。** 正式版用 NSH 的 `VtEmulator`，外加我们的配色和不答查询。它会丢掉零宽字符，也不重排。开发版有 Zig 0.15 和源码缓存时用 Ghostty。画布终端的 `vt100` 留着组合字符，所以正式版在这一点上少了一样。要不要把 Ghostty 带进正式版（Zig 进发版流程和 CI，Windows 上还没编过）另外决定。

**验证。**

- 自动测试：
  - `cargo test --workspace`，其中 `app/native` 83 过。
  - `nsh check`（App、Hosted、Story）。
  - `nsh test`：216 过、1 跳过。
  - desktop-nsh 的脚本测试 45 过。`press-cursor.test.ts` 的白名单去掉了画布那项。
  - `tsc -p apps/desktop-nsh`。
  - `nsh package Hosted.tsx --target web` 编过。
- 界面测试：`terminal-pane` 三项、`terminal-palette` 一项在真 shell 上过。退出码那项现在经 `label:Terminal` 打字，用键盘的回车提交。新加的一项粘贴 `exit 7` 再回车（测试的粘贴不碰本机剪贴板）。
- 真窗口：检查用的 .app 副本，隔离的运行时，后台窗口，只经宿主的请求文件驱动。
  - 两种宿主（VtEmulator、Ghostty）都跑过：
    - 打字回车；⌘⌫、⌥⌫、⌘←、⌥← 加 ⌘→、Ctrl+C。
    - 候选框在光标所在的格子（80,38）。上屏后马上回车没问题；取消组字什么都不发；组字时方向键归输入法。
    - 拉丁、纯中文、中英混排、反显四行的 `|` 都在第 40 格。
  - 只在 VtEmulator 上跑的：拼音画在光标处、带下划线（截图）；按 Esc 后焦点还在。
  - 只在 Ghostty 上跑的：拖选三行。
  - 程序的查询只答一次：VtEmulator 在你的 zsh 里测，Ghostty 在 `zsh -f` 里测。Ghostty 在你的 zsh 里多读到一段输入，`zsh -f` 里没有，所以多出来的东西来自 zsh 配置。两种模拟器都不回答查询（`RuntimeEmulator` 丢掉回答，`ghostty.rs` 不接 `on_pty_write`）。
- 没测到：
  - 滚轮翻历史：开发用的 `wheel` 请求只到 JS 的 `onWheel`，到不了 NSH 的原生滚动。
  - ⌘C 复制：没碰本机剪贴板。
  - 真鼠标的点击：检查用的 `drag` 请求绕过外壳的 `press.rs`。按代码看，终端 1pt 宽的隐藏输入算 sink，和画布终端透明的隐藏输入走的是同一条分支。
  - 手机的 web 页面，以及 Windows、Linux 上的真窗口。
- **钉住的 93df449 里还没有 longbridge/nsh#2095。** 没有它，iOS 和 web 上的输入框会把组字中的文字放进值里，NSH 的 `Terminal` 于是在确认前就把拼音发出去。画布终端是自己挡住的。macOS 把上屏的文字单独报，所以不受影响，上面的检查也是这么看到的。手机页面要等升到包含 #2095 的 NSH 之后再发版。

Addendum (18) put NSH's `Terminal` behind `--terminal nsh` and kept the canvas terminal as the default until upstream filled four gaps: the ⌘ keys (longbridge/nsh#1633), the first size when it is exactly 80 × 24 (#1638), the composition drawn at the cursor (#1648), and wide characters kept on their cells (#1649). All four are merged, and all are in the pinned 93df449. You said 「终端换掉」 (replace the terminal), so:

- **It is the default.** The workbench's terminal tabs (`PaneTerminal`) and `--pane terminal` (`Tools.tsx`) use `NshTerminal`. `--terminal nsh` is gone; `--terminal native` (Phase 0's NSView terminal) stays for comparison.
- **The canvas terminal is deleted.** That is `app/src/Terminal.tsx` (`CanvasTerminal` and its own key table), `app/native/src/terminal.rs` (`TerminalGrid`, `Terminals`) and the `vt100` dependency. `palette.rs` keeps only the sixteen colours and the ground that `RuntimeEmulator` uses. `encode` and `terminalSender` move into `NshTerminal.tsx`.
- **The fallbacks go too.** The 80 × 24 fallback (#1630) is gone: since #1638 the first layout always reports a size. `RuntimeTerminals` becomes a named import, because every shell built since addendum (18) has it, the released app included.
- **The wheel.** The canvas terminal moved through its history by the wheel the shell forwards (`onWheel`). NSH's `Terminal` is a virtual list, so its history scrolls natively, as the conversation lists do. Windows' `win_wheel` no longer forwards for the terminal; the picture viewer, the maps and the rest still use it.
- **New:** selecting text by dragging, and copying it (NSH's selection area). A paste reaches a program that turned on bracketed paste as one wrapped paste.
- **Packaging still leaves Ghostty out.** The released app runs NSH's `VtEmulator`, with our palette and no answers to queries. It drops zero-width characters and does not reflow. The source build uses Ghostty when Zig 0.15 and the source cache are there. The canvas terminal's `vt100` kept combining characters, so here the released app does one thing less. Whether Ghostty goes into releases (Zig in the release flow and CI, never built on Windows yet) is a separate decision.

**Checks.**

- Automated:
  - `cargo test --workspace`, with 83 passing in `app/native`.
  - `nsh check` (App, Hosted, Story).
  - `nsh test`: 216 passed, 1 skipped.
  - desktop-nsh's script tests: 45 passed. `press-cursor.test.ts`'s plain list loses the canvas entry.
  - `tsc -p apps/desktop-nsh`.
  - `nsh package Hosted.tsx --target web` builds.
- Screen tests: `terminal-pane` (3) and `terminal-palette` (1) pass on a real shell. The exit-code test now types through `label:Terminal` and submits with the keyboard's Return. A new one pastes `exit 7` and presses Return; a test's paste leaves the machine's clipboard alone.
- Real window: a check copy of the .app, an isolated runtime and a background window, driven only through the shell's request file.
  - On both shells (VtEmulator and Ghostty):
    - Typing and Return; ⌘⌫, ⌥⌫, ⌘←, ⌥← with ⌘→, and Ctrl+C.
    - The candidates open on the cursor's cell (80,38). Return right after a commit works. A cancelled composition sends nothing. Arrows during a composition go to the input method.
    - In the Latin, pure CJK, mixed and inverse lines, every `|` sits on cell 40.
  - On VtEmulator only: the pinyin drawn underlined at the cursor (a snapshot), and focus staying in the terminal after Esc.
  - On Ghostty only: a drag selecting three lines.
  - A query answered once: on VtEmulator in your zsh, on Ghostty in `zsh -f`. On Ghostty your zsh left an extra input that `zsh -f` does not, so the extra comes from the zsh configuration. Neither emulator answers queries (`RuntimeEmulator` drops replies; `ghostty.rs` wires no `on_pty_write`).
- Not checked:
  - The wheel through the history: the development `wheel` request reaches only JS `onWheel`, not NSH's native scrolling.
  - Copying with ⌘C: the machine's clipboard was left alone.
  - A real mouse click: the check's `drag` request goes around the shell's `press.rs`. By the code, the terminal's 1 pt hidden input counts as a sink, the same branch the canvas terminal's transparent hidden input took.
  - The phone's web page, and real windows on Windows and Linux.
- **The pinned 93df449 does not have longbridge/nsh#2095.** Without it, iOS and web fields put the composing text in their value, so NSH's `Terminal` sends the pinyin before it is confirmed. The canvas terminal held that back itself. macOS reports the committed text on its own, so it is not affected, which is what the checks above saw. The phone's page should not ship until the pin moves to an NSH with #2095.

## 补记 2026-11-18（四十三）：中继给手机的页面换成 NSH 版 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (43): the relay serves the NSH page to phones

用户 11-18 说「做一下」：中继给手机的页面从 Svelte 托管信使换成 NSH 的 web 包（`Hosted.tsx`）。挡着它的 longbridge/nsh#2095（补记四十二）已经在钉住的 48559f8 里。

- **严格 CSP 不放宽到内联。** NSH 打包时说明页面在什么策略下能跑。应用只要「整块」用到某个原生模块（命名空间、`import()`），运行时就得现做 import map 和 facade，策略要放开 `blob:` 和 `'unsafe-inline'`；而生产策略只认哈希，带哈希时 `'unsafe-inline'` 本来就被忽略。整块的来源只有一处：`ui/chat/composer-state.ts` 里 `await import("fs/promises")`，`fs/promises` 是 NSH 的原生模块。改成按名静态导入 `readFile` 以后，模块之间按文件 URL 互相导入（`linked`），页面没有内联脚本（配置和参数是 JSON 数据块），策略只要 `script-src 'self' 'wasm-unsafe-eval'`。`deploy/remote/csp.mjs` 一律加 `'wasm-unsafe-eval'`（它只许编译同源取来的 wasm，不许对文本 `eval`），JSON 数据块不再算哈希。
- **包。** `scripts/phone-package.ts --build` 用正式名字和 id（`Deskfolk`、`com.real-bot.desktop`）打包，再：清单沿用 Svelte 页的 `id: "./"`、名字和图标，加到主屏幕的手机还是同一个应用；去掉只有本机窗口导入的 `local-api`、`local-discovery` 两个块，任何文件还要 `__local-api` 就失败，保住「托管包里没有回环 bearer」；把整包的摘要作为版本写进页面参数（`#nsh-params` → `host.params.build`）和 `_app/version.json`。
- **新版本提示。** 已移植的 `BuildUpdateNotice` 以前没人挂。`platform/page-build.ts` 拿 `host.params.build` 和 `_app/version.json` 比，托管时 `Main.tsx` 挂上提示；刷新的是页面（`top`），不是应用所在的框架。文件名沿用 SvelteKit 的 `_app/version.json`：手机上还开着的旧 Svelte 页轮询的就是它，所以换版以后旧页面也会提示刷新，刷新就进了新页面。
- **边缘。** NSH 的文件名带内容摘要（`name.<10 位十六进制>.ext`、`browser.<…>/`、`facades/…`），Caddyfile 和线上 nginx 都改为按这个模式永久缓存，缺了回 404 而不是页面。Caddy 2.10.2 用 `precompressed` 发旁边的 `.br` 时一律回 206（官方镜像也一样），浏览器不会把 206 当整份缓存，所以 Caddy 用 `encode` 现压；nginx 的 `gzip_static` 回 200，线上用它。首次加载约 21 MB，压缩后约 7.8 MB（Svelte 页约 0.65 MB）。
- **检查。** `phone-web-e2e.ts` 现在打中继真正提供的那个包，边缘带生产的头和策略，查询规则同生产；任何策略违规、任何对本机接口代码的请求都判失败；中途在中继上放一个新版本，提示出现、刷新后仍在册。Chromium 和 WebKit 都过；`--edge caddy` 走真实 Caddy 跑生产 Caddyfile，也过；`--from-svelte` 先在 Svelte 页上配对，再把边缘换成 NSH 页：旧页面提示刷新，刷新后仍在册、同一个 worker、带推送（两个浏览器都过）。线上 nginx 的配置在 Docker 里的 nginx 1.24 上逐条查过（类型、压缩、缓存、404、400、浏览器里无违规）。
- **没换的。** 信使的 Svelte 应用和 CI 里的 `build:hosted` 留着：它是被移植逻辑的来源，也是回滚的路。真机（Android Chrome、iOS 主屏幕）上换版没看过。

On 11-18 the user said "do it": the page the relay serves to phones changes from the Svelte hosted messenger to NSH's web package (`Hosted.tsx`). What held it back, longbridge/nsh#2095 (addendum 42), is in the pinned 48559f8.

- **A strict policy, not widened to inline.** NSH's packaging says what policy the page runs under. When the app uses a native module whole (a namespace, an `import()`), the runtime makes the import map and facades as the app starts, and the policy must allow `blob:` and `'unsafe-inline'`; the production policy names hashes, and with a hash present `'unsafe-inline'` is ignored anyway. The one whole use was `await import("fs/promises")` in `ui/chat/composer-state.ts` (`fs/promises` is an NSH native module). Imported by name, statically, the modules import one another by file URL (`linked`), the page runs no inline script (its config and params are JSON data blocks), and the policy needs only `script-src 'self' 'wasm-unsafe-eval'`. `deploy/remote/csp.mjs` adds `'wasm-unsafe-eval'` always (it allows compiling wasm fetched from the origin, no `eval` of text) and hashes no JSON data block.
- **The package.** `scripts/phone-package.ts --build` packages under the released name and id (`Deskfolk`, `com.real-bot.desktop`), then: the manifest keeps the Svelte page's `id: "./"`, name and icons, so a phone that added the page to its home screen keeps the same app; the `local-api` and `local-discovery` chunks only a local window imports leave the package, and a package in which any file still asks for `__local-api` fails, which keeps "no loopback bearer in the hosted package" true; one digest of the whole package is the version, in the page's params (`#nsh-params` → `host.params.build`) and in `_app/version.json`.
- **The newer-build notice.** The ported `BuildUpdateNotice` was mounted nowhere. `platform/page-build.ts` compares `host.params.build` with `_app/version.json`, and `Main.tsx` mounts the notice when hosted; the reload is the page's (`top`), not the frame the app runs in. The file keeps SvelteKit's name, `_app/version.json`: a Svelte page still open on a phone polls it, so after the switch the old page offers the reload too, and the reload is into the new page.
- **The edge.** NSH names its files by their content (`name.<10 hex>.ext`, `browser.<…>/`, `facades/…`). The Caddyfile and the live nginx cache that pattern for good and send a missing one as a 404, not the page. Caddy 2.10.2 answers a precompressed `.br` sidecar with a 206 (the official image too), which a browser does not cache as the whole file, so Caddy compresses with `encode`; nginx's `gzip_static` answers 200, and the live box uses it. The first load is ~21 MB, ~7.8 MB compressed (the Svelte page was ~0.65 MB).
- **Checks.** `phone-web-e2e.ts` now packages what the relay serves and serves it with the production edge's headers, policy and query rule; any policy violation and any request for the local endpoint's code fail it; a newer build put up midway is offered, and after its reload the device is still enrolled. Chromium and WebKit pass; `--edge caddy`, real Caddy running the production Caddyfile, passes; `--from-svelte` pairs on the Svelte page, then swaps the edge to the NSH page: the old page offers the reload, and after it the device is still enrolled, under the same worker, with push (both browsers). The live nginx config was checked rule by rule on nginx 1.24 in Docker (types, compression, caching, 404, 400, no violation in a browser).
- **Not changed.** The messenger's Svelte app and CI's `build:hosted` stay: it is the source of the ported logic and the way back. The switch has not been seen on a real phone (Android Chrome, an iOS home screen).

## 补记 2026-11-18（四十四）：手机页面的网址和返回 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (44): the phone page's address and Back

补记四十三上线当天用户说「移动端路由体系乱了」，回滚到 Svelte 页。原因是 Svelte 页 `routes/+page.svelte` 和 `+layout.svelte` 里的胶水没随镜像过来（镜像只搬 `src/lib`）：网址不跟着打开的东西走，打开会话后按返回直接离开页面，刷新和链接回不到会话。逐行对照这两个文件，还缺两样：第二个标签页的等待和接管（`tab-standby`），以及断线后盖在外壳上的提示条。

- **`platform/page-route.ts`**，`+page.svelte` 那套搬到 NSH 的 `UrlScheme` 上：网址→打开什么（会话 `?s=`、设置和各页 `?o=`、附件 `?a=`，同 `session-url.ts`），打开什么→网址（`mobile-route.ts`：打开推一条，关闭退回打开它的那条，没有我们的条目就改写），返回先关网址不带的层（`backMobileLayer`），第一条下面垫一条 `#floor`（`armBackFloor` 的做法）：在那里返回就关掉当前这屏直到名单，再没东西可关才离开页面。刷新保留第一次加载的那条，NSH 把它作为 `open` 交回来。
- **先量 NSH 再写。** 48559f8 上 Chromium 和 WebKit 都量过：`setUrl` 推一条就是一条，返回是带下面那条网址的 `change`；应用自己的 `history.back()` 也是 `change`（所以要记一笔 `selfBack`）；同一轮里先 replace 再 push 只剩 push，地板的 push 要等 replace 落地；刷新后返回到上次加载的条目是 `open`。NSH 自己给底部菜单、对话框这类模态写条目，返回由它关；设置既是模态又在网址里，返回关掉它时页面已经在下面那条了，所以往哪走按页面实际所在（`href`）决定，不只看我们记的栈，否则会再退一步、退出页面。
- **另外两样。** `TabStandbyScreen`（另一个标签页拿着设备的链路时这一个等着，「接管」）；`OfflineBanner`（外壳出来以后断线：外壳变淡、不接点按，顶上一条「正在连接…」或「连不上」加「重试」）。后者先只在手机页面上：桌面窗口的遮罩也会盖住拖动区，还没在桌面上看过。
- **`k` 进了边缘的查询规则。** 流程页把它停在的那个任务写进网址（`session-url.ts` 的 `k`，2026-09-22 起），而 Caddyfile 和中继机上的 nginx 只放 `s|o|b|a`：在流程页刷新是 400，Svelte 页一直如此。现在四处规则（Caddyfile、端到端的边缘、`smoke:serve`、中继机的 `relay.conf`）都放 `k`；端到端记下页面去过的每个网址，有边缘不放行的就失败。
- **检查。** `page-route.test.ts` 用照实测写的假历史跑十种情形。`phone-web-e2e.ts` 加了：会话的网址和浏览器的返回/前进，页内返回不留条目，底部菜单先关、再关会话，流程页，设置一次返回就关且不离开，会话里刷新，链接加地板，第二个标签页接管再接回，中继断开时的提示条；Chromium、WebKit、`--edge caddy`、`--host localhost`、`--from-svelte` 都过。两件测试自己的事：NSH 写模态条目用 `setTimeout(0)`，无头浏览器能把它压后近一秒，测试先等条目写好再按返回；中继每个地址每分钟 10 次握手，这里页面和 Mac 共用回环一个地址、一次加载两次，连续加载会碰到，测试按「重试」并在输出里说明（真实里手机和 Mac 是两个地址）。

On the day of addendum 43 the user said the phone's routing was a mess, and the relay went back to the Svelte page. The glue in the Svelte page's `routes/+page.svelte` and `+layout.svelte` had never been carried over (the mirror carries `src/lib` only): the address did not follow what was open, Back from a conversation left the page, and a reload or a link did not land on the conversation. Read line by line against those two files, two more things were missing: a second tab's wait and takeover (`tab-standby`), and the banner over the shell once the connection is lost.

- **`platform/page-route.ts`** is `+page.svelte`'s routing on NSH's `UrlScheme`. The address names what is open (the conversation `?s=`, settings and the pages `?o=`, an attachment `?a=`, as `session-url.ts` has it). What opens follows the address, and the address follows what opens (`mobile-route.ts`: opening pushes, closing walks back to the entry that opened it or rewrites the entry when nothing of ours is under it). Back closes a layer the address does not carry first (`backMobileLayer`), and a floor sits under the first entry, `#floor`, as `armBackFloor` did: Back there closes the screen showing down to the roster, and only with nothing left to close leaves the page. A reload keeps the first load's floor, which NSH hands back as an `open`.
- **NSH measured first.** On 48559f8, in Chromium and WebKit:
  - A push through `setUrl` is one entry, and Back off it is a `change` with the address under it.
  - The app's own `history.back()` is a `change` too, hence `selfBack`.
  - A replace and a push in one turn are the push alone, so the floor's push waits for its replace to land.
  - Back onto an earlier load's entry after a reload is an `open`.
  - NSH writes entries for sheets and dialogs (modals) and closes them on Back. Settings is a modal the address carries too: when Back closes it the page is already on the entry under it, so where to go is decided from where the page is (`href`), not only from our stack. Otherwise it would step back once more, out of the page.
- **The other two.**
  - `TabStandbyScreen`: another tab holds the device's link, this one waits, "take over".
  - `OfflineBanner`: the connection is lost once the shell was up. The shell fades and takes no presses, and a pill at the top says "connecting…", or "can't reach" with Retry. It is on the phone's page only for now: on the desktop the veil would also cover the window's drag region, and nobody has looked at it there.
- **`k` joins the edge's query rule.** Since 2026-09-22 the flow page has put the job it settles on into the address (`session-url.ts`'s `k`), while the Caddyfile and the relay box's nginx let through only `s|o|b|a`. A reload on the flow page was a 400, on the Svelte page as well. All four copies of the rule now allow `k`: the Caddyfile, the e2e's edge, `smoke:serve` and the relay box's `relay.conf`. The e2e records every address the page goes to and fails on one the edge would refuse.
- **Checks.**
  - `page-route.test.ts` runs ten cases on a fake history written from the measurements.
  - `phone-web-e2e.ts` gains: a conversation's address with the browser's Back and Forward; the page's own Back leaving no entry; Back closing the sheet first, then the conversation; the flow page; settings closed by one Back without leaving; a reload inside a conversation; a link plus the floor; a second tab taking over and back; and the banner while the relay is out of reach.
  - It passes in Chromium, in WebKit, with `--edge caddy`, with `--host localhost` and with `--from-svelte`.
- **Two things that belong to the test, not the app.**
  - NSH writes a modal's entry on a `setTimeout(0)`, which a headless browser can hold back for most of a second, so the test waits for the entry before pressing Back.
  - The relay allows an address 10 handshakes a minute. Here the page and the Mac share loopback, at two handshakes a load, and back-to-back loads reach the limit. The test presses Retry and says so. On a phone the page and the Mac are two addresses.

## 补记 2026-11-18（四十五）：设置里按返回逐层退 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (45): Back steps out of settings one level at a time

用户说手机页上「进入设置页，再进入某一项设置，再进入某个细化页，应该是逐层返回」。实际是在设置的任何一页按一次手机或浏览器的返回，整个设置就关了；端点编辑器里从某个连接方式的表单按返回，整个编辑器就关了。页面自己的 ‹ 一直是逐层退的（`backWithinSettings`），坏的只是系统的返回。

- **原因。** NSH 把返回先交给最上面的对话框（`defaultBack` → `requestClose` → `onCancel`），`closeOnEscape` 为真时随后自己关掉它。设置的 `onCancel` 直接 `closeSettings()`；`Modal` 的 `onCancel` 调 `onClose`，端点编辑器在窄窗口里那是退一层，可对话框接着又自己关了。`page-route.ts` 原来以为返回会先到 `backMobileLayer`，那是 Svelte 页的路子。
- **设置。** 手机上在列表以下（`mobileDetail`），对话框的 `closeOnEscape` 为假，`onCancel` 退一层，和 ‹ 一样；在列表上照旧关。退一层要等一轮（`queueMicrotask`）：NSH 在 `onCancel` 返回后马上读 `closeOnEscape`，当场退到列表会让它变真，又关掉。
- **只退一次。** 设置在网址里，对话框条目下面那条是别的网址，NSH 在对话框没关时把它的条目重新推上（地址是下面那条的），随后还发一个 `change(back)`。外壳的 `backMobileLayer` 原来在这里再退一层，一次返回退两级；现在 `settings` 和 `provider-editor` 两种情形只答「已处理」，退的事归对话框。`page-route.ts` 这时把地址 replace 到 NSH 推回的那条上，不再 push：多推的一条会吃掉下一次返回。
- **端点编辑器。** `Modal` 加了 `stepsBack`：为真时返回和 Esc 只交给 `onClose`（同样等一轮），对话框不自己关。编辑器在窄窗口里打开它。
- **Bot 的设置** 量过，本来就逐层退，没改；端到端里留一步守着。
- **代价。** 宽度在 720 以下的窗口里，设置的某一页上按 Esc 也是退一层，不再直接关。
- **检查。** `phone-web-e2e.ts`：设置 › 模型 › 端点 › 添加端点 › 自定义，然后一路按返回：表单→连接方式→编辑器关→端点这一节→模型→列表→设置关，网址一直是 `/?o=settings`，历史条数不变；再加 Bot 的设置一节一节退。旧代码上 WebKit 第一次返回就回到了名单；改后 WebKit 和 Chromium 都过。设置从链接直接打开（`/?o=settings` 作第一屏）也照样逐层退，最后一次返回落在名单上。`.ui` 的 `settings-routing-modal` 加两条用无头驱动的 `app.back()` 的测试，旧代码上两条都失败；再加一条窄窗口里按 Esc 逐层退的。`page-route.test.ts` 加一条：设置当一步处理的返回，地址落在 NSH 推回的条目上，不多一条。

The user said that on the phone page, going into settings, then one of its items, then a page under that, Back should step out one level at a time. In fact one press of the phone's or the browser's Back on any page of settings closed all of it, and one from a connector's form in the endpoint editor closed the whole editor. The page's own ‹ had always stepped out one level (`backWithinSettings`); only the system's Back was wrong.

- **Why.** NSH hands Back to the topmost dialog first (`defaultBack` → `requestClose` → `onCancel`), and when `closeOnEscape` is true it then closes the dialog itself. Settings' `onCancel` called `closeSettings()`. `Modal`'s `onCancel` calls `onClose`, which for the endpoint editor in a narrow window steps out one level, and then the dialog closed itself anyway. `page-route.ts` assumed Back reached `backMobileLayer` first, which is how the Svelte page worked.
- **Settings.** On a phone, below the list (`mobileDetail`), the dialog's `closeOnEscape` is false and `onCancel` steps out one level, as ‹ does. On the list it closes, as before. The step waits a turn (`queueMicrotask`): NSH reads `closeOnEscape` right after `onCancel` returns, and stepping out to the list there and then would make it true and close the dialog after all.
- **One step, not two.** Settings is in the address, so the entry under the dialog's has another address. With the dialog still open, NSH pushes the dialog's entry again, at the address under it, and then also sends a `change(back)`. The shell's `backMobileLayer` used to step out once more there, so one Back went two levels. Its `settings` and `provider-editor` cases now only answer "handled"; the dialog does the stepping. `page-route.ts` then replaces the address onto the entry NSH pushed back, instead of pushing: an extra entry would swallow the next Back.
- **The endpoint editor.** `Modal` gains `stepsBack`. When it is true, Back and Escape only go to `onClose` (also a turn later), and the dialog does not close itself. The editor turns it on in a narrow window.
- **A Bot's settings** were measured and already stepped out one level at a time, so they are unchanged. The e2e keeps a step to guard them.
- **The cost.** In a window 720 wide or less, Escape on a page of settings steps out one level too, instead of closing settings.
- **Checks.**
  - `phone-web-e2e.ts` goes Settings › Models › Endpoints › Add endpoint › Custom, then Back all the way: the form, the connector tiles, the editor closed, the Endpoints section, Models, the list, settings closed. The address stays `/?o=settings` and the history keeps its length. Then a Bot's settings, a section at a time. On the old code, WebKit's first Back landed on the roster; with the fix it passes in WebKit and Chromium.
  - Settings opened by a link (`/?o=settings` as the first screen) step out the same way, and the last Back lands on the roster.
  - `.ui`'s `settings-routing-modal` gains two tests that use the headless driver's `app.back()`. Both fail on the old code. A third presses Escape in a narrow window and sees it step out one level at a time.
  - `page-route.test.ts` gains one: a Back settings took as a step leaves the address on the entry NSH pushed back, adding none.

## 补记 2026-11-18（四十六）：手机上设置的列表没有顶栏，底栏留在下面 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (46): on a phone settings' list has no head, and the bottom bar stays under it

用户说「手机端的设置页，不要顶栏，保留主底栏」。设置本来就是手机底栏的三个去处之一（`mobileDestination`），外壳的 `mobileNavigationVisible` 也说在设置列表上要显示底栏；可设置是铺满窗口的 NSH 对话框，把外壳的底栏盖住了，而且对话框下面的东西点不到（`inert`）。Svelte 页上设置列表也有顶栏（标题和 ✕）。

- **底栏画在对话框里。** 外壳把底栏要的三样（当前去处、更新的点、`navigateMobile`）作为 `mobileNavigation` 交给 `SettingsModal`，条件和外壳自己的底栏一样（窄窗口、`mobileNavigationVisible`）；设置开着时外壳自己的那条不画，同一时间只有一条。有它时列表没有顶栏（`SettingsNav` 的 `head={false}`，「设置未完成」挪到列表上面，列表顶上留出安全区），底栏在列表下面，对话框进出不淡入淡出（`animation="none"`），切过去和点底栏换页一样。某一项、某个编辑器还是带 ‹ 的页头，不显示底栏。没有底栏的窄窗口（681 到 720 宽）还是有顶栏和 ✕，否则没有出口。
- **`mobileNavigation` 不跟着 `settingsOpen`。** 跟着的话，点底栏离开时它先变空，对话框会淡出、顶栏冒出来一下。
- **顺带修的：应用自己关掉对话框时的网址。** 点底栏离开设置时网址停在 `/?o=settings`。量下来：`page-route` 的 `write` 自己 `history.back()` 了一次，正好用掉对话框的条目；popstate 到时对话框已经没了，NSH 当它是一次被应用拒绝的返回，用 `replaceState` 把 `/?o=settings` 写回了下面那条；我们的 `selfBack` 一直等不到 `change`。✕ 关设置走的是同一条路，线上一直如此。现在网址里带着的对话框（设置、会话的设置）由应用关掉时，`write` 不再后退，只 `setUrl(replace)`：NSH 自己 `history.go` 退过对话框的条目，再把这一轮设的地址放到落地的那条上。
- **检查。** `phone-web-e2e.ts`：从底栏进设置，列表没有顶栏、只有一条底栏，点「项目」离开、网址回到 `/`，之后打开会话再返回仍在页面里；Bot 的设置用它自己的 ✕ 关掉，网址回到会话。WebKit 和 Chromium 都过。`.ui` 的 `shell.test` 加一条 420 宽的：列表没有顶栏、底栏一条，某一项里没有底栏，返回列表又有，点「项目」离开设置。`page-route.test.ts` 加两条：设置由应用关掉、群设置里打开的 Bot 由应用关掉，网址都落在 NSH 退到的那条上。
- **没解释的一次。** 18 日 15 点多的两次 WebKit 运行里，页面对几个 `blob:` 地址的请求被 `connect-src` 拒绝；之后带改动的六次、不带改动的三次都没有；其中装了记录器的三次，一次 `blob:` 请求都没有。另一个会话在只有 87c90176 的代码上连跑四次，三次一样失败，看到的是主文档里已释放的 `blob:` 图片加载，没有脚本位置：这在这次改动之前就有，也未必是 `fetch`。端到端现在给每个 frame 装一个记录器，是 `fetch` 的话失败信息里带着调用栈。

The user said that on a phone, settings should have no head and keep the main bottom bar. Settings was already one of the phone bar's three places (`mobileDestination`), and the shell's `mobileNavigationVisible` already called for the bar on settings' list. But settings is an NSH dialog that fills the window: it covered the shell's bar, and what is under a dialog takes no presses (`inert`). The Svelte page's settings list had a head too, with the title and ✕.

- **The bar is drawn in the dialog.**
  - The shell hands `SettingsModal` what the bar needs as `mobileNavigation`: the place it is at, the update dot and `navigateMobile`. The conditions are the shell's own bar's: a narrow window and `mobileNavigationVisible`.
  - While settings is open the shell does not draw its own bar, so there is one bar at a time.
  - With it, the list has no head: `SettingsNav` gets `head={false}`. The "setup not done" badge moves above the list, and the list keeps clear of the top inset.
  - The bar sits under the list. The dialog comes and goes without a fade (`animation="none"`), as a press on the bar changes places.
  - A section or an editor keeps its head with ‹, and no bar.
  - A narrow window without the bar (681 to 720 wide) keeps the head and its ✕, or it would have no way out.
- **`mobileNavigation` does not follow `settingsOpen`.** If it did, it would go empty as the bar left settings, and the dialog would fade out with its head back for a moment.
- **Fixed on the way: the address when the app closes a dialog.**
  - Leaving settings through the bar left the address at `/?o=settings`. Measured: `page-route`'s `write` called `history.back()` itself, using up the dialog's entry.
  - When the popstate landed the dialog was gone, so NSH took it for a Back the app had refused and wrote `/?o=settings` back onto the entry under it with `replaceState`. Our `selfBack` waited for a `change` that never came.
  - Closing settings with ✕ takes the same path, and it always had this bug on the live page.
  - Now, when the app closes a dialog the address carries (settings, a conversation's settings), `write` no longer goes back. It only calls `setUrl(replace)`: NSH goes back over the dialog's entry itself with `history.go`, then puts the address set that turn on the entry it lands on.
- **Checks.**
  - `phone-web-e2e.ts`: settings from the bar shows a list with no head and one bar. Pressing Projects leaves settings and the address goes back to `/`; opening a conversation and pressing Back still stays on the page. A Bot's settings closed by their own ✕ put the address back on the conversation. It passes in WebKit and Chromium.
  - `.ui`'s `shell.test` gains a case at 420 wide: the list has no head and one bar, a tab has no bar, the list has it again, and Projects leaves settings.
  - `page-route.test.ts` gains two cases: settings closed by the app, and a Bot opened from a group's settings closed by the app. In both the address lands on the entry NSH went back to.
- **One thing left unexplained.**
  - In two WebKit runs around 15:30 on the 18th, `connect-src` refused the page's requests for a few `blob:` addresses. Six later runs with the change and three without showed none, and the three of them that ran the logger saw no `blob:` fetch at all.
  - Another session ran 87c90176 alone four times, and three failed the same way. It saw loads of released `blob:` images in the main document, with no script location. So this predates the change here, and may not be a `fetch` at all.
  - The e2e now puts a logger in every frame: if the request is a `fetch`, the failure carries the caller's stack.

## 补记 2026-11-18（四十七）：手机上的动画为什么卡 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (47): why animations stuttered on the phone

用户说远程手机端（安卓 Chrome，装到主屏幕的 PWA）的动画很卡，旧的 Svelte 页没有这种感觉，比如打开底部弹出的选项。

- **机制不同。**
  - Svelte 的 `BottomSheet` 是 240 ms 的 CSS keyframes（`cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1)`），浏览器的合成线程跑，主线程再忙也照走。
  - NSH 的 `Sheet`（`nsh-base` `SheetMotion`）是动画图里 280 ms 的 timing（`cubic-bezier(0,0,0.58,1)`）。在网页上，它每一帧由主线程的 `requestAnimationFrame` 走一步，再写成行内样式，主线程上的任何长任务都会让它丢帧。
  - 曲线本身也不同：100 ms 时 Svelte 进来了 95%，NSH 是 52%。
- **怎么量的。** 用 phone-web-e2e 的配对流程（本机中继和隔离的 Mac），在无头 Chromium 和 WebKit 里以 390×844@3x 打开手机页，Chromium 用 CDP 把 CPU 降到四分之一或六分之一。对话里的「更多」面板开五次，每帧用 `MutationObserver` 记面板行内的 `transform`，再看长帧（LoAF）和 CPU profile。对照是一个只有一个按钮和同样 `Sheet` 的 NSH 页面。脚本是临时的，不在仓库里。
- **NSH 自己的问题。**
  1. 动画的起点取第一个 rAF 回调拿到的帧时间，也就是帧开始的时刻。打开面板的那次按下如果跑得久，这个时间早于动画被要的时刻，于是第一步就走到了半路。对照页降到六分之一时首次打开，面板一帧从 224 跳到 26.6 px。上游的 issue 是 longbridge/nsh#2389，修复是 #2390：回调晚于帧时间 16 ms 以上时，按回调运行的时刻走，并且不早于上一帧；修后这次打开是 18 步，每步不超过 20 px。
  2. 每一帧把应用里所有绑着的动画值都发一遍，`op_a` 再逐个读 `getPropertyValue` 比较，所以成本跟着绑定的数量走，而不是跟着动的那些走。上游的 issue 是 #2391，修复是 #2407：帧、手势和滚动时只发变了的值，批次之后照旧全发。在同一个提交（dcf37282）的手机包上量，四分之一速下每帧的回调从约 7 ms 降到 1.4–2.8 ms。
  3. 首次打开时 NSH 的冷启动，对照页四分之一速时要 110 ms。第一次给代码着色时，`nsh-syntaxes.bin` 在主线程上同步解析，四分之一速时要 1.2 s。这两条没有报。
- **我们这边把它放大了。**
  - 每行消息的 `arriveMotion` 一直绑着，历史消息也绑。200 行时，带绑定的视图从 53 个变成 147 个，四分之一速下每帧的回调从 1.2 ms 变成 7 ms，面板滑入只画出 18 步里的 11 到 14 步。
  - `FolkAvatar` 空闲时也绑着约 10 个部件，眨眼又是无限循环，所以动画图一直在动，页面空闲时也每帧走一遍。打开 reduce motion 后，空闲帧变成零。
  - 手机上，会话列表一直挂在对话页下面。
  - `ChatHeader` 的脉冲（`pulseMotion(() => true)`）从挂载起就一直在播，`ChatTabLabel`、`PaneChatBar`、`BotDmEntry`、`ProjectSubagentEntry` 的也一样。空闲时没有视图绑定它，屏幕上什么都不动，动画图却每帧都在走：对话页开着时，空闲的页面 2 秒跑满 120 帧。这一条是落地后在 main 的构建上补测才发现的：把包里的 `iterations: "infinite"` 逐个改掉二分，才定位到它；强制 GC 停不下它，reduce motion 能。
  - Bot 回复时，中继消息的处理让主线程忙。四分之一速下一条要 88 到 343 ms，大部分在 NSH 的 JS↔wasm 桥里，这些帧也跟着丢。
- **改了什么。**
  - `arriveMotion` 只在刚到的行播放时给出动画值，播完就不给了，历史行一直不给。
  - `FolkAvatar` 的部件只在各自的动作播放时绑定。眨眼改成计时器触发的一次 208 ms 动画，两次之间什么都不动。
  - `LoopsHeld`（`ui/interface/motion.ts`）在手机会话列表被页面盖住时为真，头像的循环和 `SessionAvatar`、`AgentAvatar` 的工作脉冲都在它下面停。
  - 在网页上，对话列表带一个 `perspective` 变换，单独成一层。NSH 不认 `will-change`，恒等的变换又会被丢掉，所以用它。
  - `ChatHeader` 的脉冲只在会呼吸的小点显示时播；其余几处的脉冲做进小点自己，小点在时才播。所有循环（`pulseMotion`、头像的动作、工作脉冲、回复中的点、流式光标、历史的闪烁）都在 effect 的 cleanup 里停，组件没了它就停。
- **为什么要那一层。** 原先每行的 motion 变换里都有 `rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg)`，浏览器给每行一个合成层（229 个）。去掉行上的绑定以后，WebKit 每一步都重画整段对话，面板滑入从 20 步掉到 13 步。只给面板和背景加 `will-change` 没有用，给对话列表一层就好了。
- **结果。** 200 行的对话，热状态打开，五次里后四次。「改前」是 dcf37282 的手机包，「改后」是 30a08ffe 加上一节那条脉冲的修复；都在 Chromium 四分之一速和 WebKit 不降速下量：

| | 改前 | 改后 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Chromium：滑入步数，满分 18 | 10–12 | 14–15 |
| Chromium：每帧回调 | 6.8–7.4 ms | 1.2–1.4 ms |
| Chromium：首次打开 | 6 步，最大一步 148 px | 4 步，197 px |
| WebKit：滑入步数 | 20 | 21–22 |
| WebKit：最大一步 | 38–43 px | 17–26 px |
| 空闲、对话页开着：2 秒里的帧 | 120 | 0–14，只有眨眼 |

  - Chromium 的首次打开没有变好：那是动画起点的问题，要等 #2390 进了 pin。#2390 打进 f199fe7a 时期的包里量过，热状态 15–18 步。
  - Bot 回复时（Mac 每 120 ms 推一行）也没有变好，改前 7–17 步，改后 10–15 步，忽好忽坏，看消息落在哪一帧。那段停顿主要在 NSH 的 JS↔wasm 桥里处理消息，这次没碰。
  - 会话列表被盖住时，列表里的样式写入从 544 次（12 秒）变成 0；按返回后恢复眨眼。

- **没验证的。** 真机。用户的安卓手机还没看过，线上页面也没换。
- **检查。** 每一步落地前 `.ui` 全部 89 个文件都过了（最后一次 729 个），单测 1476 个也过了。`nsh check App.tsx` 只报 `page-route.test.ts` 的既有错误，那是另一处改动带进来的。`sidebar-rail` 的提示测试改成能看到 200 ms 的等待：之前头像无尽的眨眼让页面一直忙，无头驱动 hover 时顺带把手动时钟走过了这段等待。这个文件从 64 s 变成 11 s。脉冲那条修复是在 main 的构建上用 `MutationObserver` 和 rAF 计数核对的：眨眼照常，没有残留的行内 opacity，会话列表盖住时不写、返回后恢复。

The user said animations on the remote phone page (Chrome on Android, installed as a PWA) stuttered where the old Svelte page hadn't, opening a sheet of options for one.

- **Different mechanisms.**
  - Svelte's `BottomSheet` was 240 ms CSS keyframes (`cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1)`), which the browser's compositor runs however busy the main thread is.
  - NSH's `Sheet` (`nsh-base` `SheetMotion`) is a 280 ms timing in the animation graph (`cubic-bezier(0,0,0.58,1)`). On the web it steps once a frame from a `requestAnimationFrame` on the main thread and is written as inline style, so any long task on the main thread drops its frames.
  - The curves differ too: at 100 ms Svelte's is 95% in and NSH's 52%.
- **How it was measured.** The phone page ran through phone-web-e2e's pairing (a local relay and an isolated Mac) in headless Chromium and WebKit at 390×844@3x, with Chromium's CPU throttled 4× or 6× through CDP. A conversation's "more" sheet was opened five times. A `MutationObserver` recorded the panel's inline `transform` each frame, beside long animation frames (LoAF) and CPU profiles. The control was an NSH page with one button and the same `Sheet`. The scripts were throwaway and are not in the repo.
- **NSH's own problems.**
  1. An animation starts at the time the first rAF callback gets, which is when the frame began. When the press that opened the sheet ran long, that time is before the animation was asked for, so its first step is already halfway. The control's first open at 6× jumped from 224 to 26.6 px in one frame. Upstream this is longbridge/nsh#2389, with the fix in #2390: a callback more than 16 ms after its frame's time steps at the time it runs, never earlier than the frame before. With the fix that open draws 18 steps of 20 px or less.
  2. Every frame sends every bound animated value in the app, and `op_a` reads `getPropertyValue` for each, so the cost follows how many values are bound, not how many move. Upstream this is #2391, with the fix in #2407: frames, gestures and scrolls send only the values that changed, and a batch still sends them all. Measured on the phone package of one commit (dcf37282) at 4×, the frame callback fell from about 7 ms to 1.4–2.8 ms.
  3. Two cold costs were not filed: NSH's first-open start-up, 110 ms on the control at 4×, and the first code colouring, which parses `nsh-syntaxes.bin` on the main thread in 1.2 s at 4×.
- **What our side added.**
  - Every message row kept its `arriveMotion` bound, history included. With 200 lines, views with bindings went from 53 to 147, the frame callback at 4× from 1.2 to 7 ms, and the sheet's entrance drew 11 to 14 of its 18 steps.
  - `FolkAvatar` bound about ten parts while idle and blinked from an endless loop, so the graph never rested and an idle page stepped it every frame. With reduce motion the idle frames went to zero.
  - On the phone the roster stays mounted under the conversation page.
  - `ChatHeader`'s pulse (`pulseMotion(() => true)`) played from mount, and so did `ChatTabLabel`'s, `PaneChatBar`'s, `BotDmEntry`'s and `ProjectSubagentEntry`'s. At rest no view bound it, so nothing on screen moved, yet the animation graph stepped every frame: an idle page with a conversation open ran all 120 frames in 2 s. This was found only by measuring main's build after landing. Changing the bundle's `iterations: "infinite"` sites one group at a time pinned it; a forced GC didn't stop it, and reduce motion did.
  - While Bots reply, handling relay messages keeps the main thread busy. One message took 88 to 343 ms at 4×, most of it in NSH's JS↔wasm bridge, and those frames drop too.
- **What changed.**
  - `arriveMotion` gives its values only while a row that just arrived rises, then none. History rows never get them.
  - `FolkAvatar`'s parts bind their motions only while those play. A blink is one 208 ms animation started from a timer, with nothing moving in between.
  - `LoopsHeld` (`ui/interface/motion.ts`) is true in the phone's roster while a page covers it. The avatars' loops and `SessionAvatar`'s and `AgentAvatar`'s working pulses stop under it.
  - On the web the transcript list carries a `perspective` transform, which gives it a layer of its own. NSH takes no `will-change` and drops identity transforms, so this is the way to get one.
  - `ChatHeader`'s pulse plays only while a dot that breathes shows. The other pulses are made inside their dot, so each plays while it is there. Every loop (`pulseMotion`, a folk's work, the working pulses, the replying dots, the stream's cursor, the history shimmer) stops in its effect's cleanup, so it ends with its component.
- **Why the layer.** Each row's motion transform had included `rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg)`, which gave every row a compositing layer (229 of them). Once the rows lost their bindings, WebKit repainted the whole transcript on every step, and the sheet's entrance fell from 20 steps to 13. `will-change` on the panel and backdrop didn't help; a layer for the transcript did.
- **Results.** A 200-line conversation, the last four of five warm opens. "Before" is the phone package of dcf37282; "after" is 30a08ffe plus the pulse fix above. Measured in Chromium at 4× and in WebKit unthrottled:

| | Before | After |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Chromium: entrance steps, of 18 | 10–12 | 14–15 |
| Chromium: frame callback | 6.8–7.4 ms | 1.2–1.4 ms |
| Chromium: first open | 6 steps, largest 148 px | 4 steps, 197 px |
| WebKit: entrance steps | 20 | 21–22 |
| WebKit: largest step | 38–43 px | 17–26 px |
| Idle, a conversation open: frames in 2 s | 120 | 0–14, a blink's |

  - Chromium's first open is no better. That is the animation's start, which needs #2390 in the pin. Patched into a package of the f199fe7a days, #2390 gave 15–18 steps on warm opens.
  - Neither is a sheet opened while a Bot replies (the Mac posting a line every 120 ms): 7–17 steps before, 10–15 after, varying with where the messages land. That pause is mostly NSH's JS↔wasm bridge handling the messages, which this doesn't touch.
  - With the roster covered, style writes inside it went from 544 in 12 s to none, and its blinks came back after Back.

- **Not verified.** Real phones. The user's Android phone hasn't been checked, and the live page hasn't been replaced.
- **Checks.** Before each landing all 89 `.ui` files passed (729 tests the last time), and so did the 1476 unit tests. `nsh check App.tsx` reports only existing errors in `page-route.test.ts`, which another change brought in. `sidebar-rail`'s tip test now sees the tip's 200 ms wait. The folks' endless blinks had kept the page busy, so a headless hover ran the manual clock past that wait before it resolved, and that file went from 64 s to 11 s. The pulse fix was checked on main's build with a `MutationObserver` and rAF counts. Blinks still happen, no inline opacity is left behind, and the covered roster writes nothing until Back, after which it blinks again.

## 补记 2026-11-18（四十八）：在代码块和表格上上下滑，滚的是会话 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (48): a vertical swipe on a code block or a table scrolls the conversation

用户 11-18 说手机远程端「代码、表格这些区域会阻挡整个内容滚动，无法触摸滚动会话内容」。中继机的访问日志里，配对的手机是 Android Chrome。

- **原因在 NSH 的 web 运行时样式。** 每个滚动视图都是 `.nsh-scroll { overscroll-behavior: contain }`，两个方向都锁。回复里的代码块和表格是只左右滚动的视图（`.nsh-ox`：`overflow-x: auto; overflow-y: hidden`）。Chrome 在手势开始时挑接手的滚动容器：它在竖直方向滚不动，但竖直方向又不许往外传，于是手势停在它身上，谁也不滚。无头 Chromium 里用 DevTools 的触摸手势量过：在代码块、表格上竖划 200 px，会话一动不动；在旁边的文字上竖划，会话滚 200 px；只把横向视图的竖直方向改回 `auto`，在代码块、表格上竖划也滚会话，左右划照旧滚代码块自己。
- **修在包里。** `phone-package.ts` 在页面 `<head>` 末尾加一条 `<style id="deskfolk-page">.nsh-scroll.nsh-ox.nsh-ox { overscroll-behavior-y: auto; }</style>`（`PAGE_STYLE`），三个类压过运行时那条一个类的规则，和样式表的先后无关；策略里 `style-src` 本来就有 `'unsafe-inline'`。只改横向视图的竖直方向：纵向视图的横向还锁着，这次的问题用不到，放开会让每个纵向列表都把左右滑往外传。该在 NSH 里修：上游 main 已有 longbridge/nsh#2309（单轴视图只锁自己那一轴），内容装得下、没东西可滚的那一轴报了 longbridge/nsh#2417，修复是 PR longbridge/nsh#2418（`nsh-fits`，照 Android 把滑动交给外层）。钉住的版本带上这两个以后，`PAGE_STYLE` 可以去掉。
- **补：嵌套的纵向小块也一样（同日）。** 先只改了横向视图的竖直方向。再量：命令卡片和展开的命令输出是纵向的滚动视图，内容不满（113/113、143/143 px）时同样带着 `contain`，在上面竖划，会话不动；在卡片外面的那一行上竖划就动。规则改成 `.nsh-scroll .nsh-scroll { overscroll-behavior: auto; }`：滚动视图套在另一个里面时，自己滚不动的部分交给外面那个，最外层的会话列表仍是 `contain`，不会带动页面或浏览器的返回手势。代码块左右划照旧滚它自己。端到端加了一步：展开命令输出后，两者的 `overscroll-behavior-y` 是 `auto`，Chromium 里在命令输出和命令那一行上各竖划一次，会话要动过 100 px；旧规则下这一步失败（`contain, contain`），新规则两个浏览器都过。
- **检查。** `phone-runtime.ts` 给 Writer 的会话多放 24 行历史，回复里放一段带长行的代码和一张六列的表，都比手机宽。`phone-web-e2e.ts` 打开会话后：两个横向视图的 `overscroll-behavior-y` 都是 `auto`；Chromium 里用触摸手势在代码块和表格上各竖划一次，会话都要滚过 100 px，在代码块上左右划，代码块自己要滚过 100 px。WebKit 这里驱动不了触摸滚动，只查样式那一条。整个浏览器上下文开了 `hasTouch`：不开时 NSH 的列表按桌面浏览器的方式滚，没有原生滚动可量。旧包上这一步失败（`overscroll-behavior-y contain, contain`），新包 Chromium 和 WebKit 全程都过。iOS 上没量过。

On 11-18 the user said that on the phone, code blocks and tables blocked the whole page from scrolling: a touch could not scroll the conversation. The relay box's access log shows the paired phone runs Android Chrome.

- **The cause is the NSH web runtime's sheet.** Every scroll view gets `.nsh-scroll { overscroll-behavior: contain }`, both axes. A reply's code block and table are sideways-only scroll views (`.nsh-ox`: `overflow-x: auto; overflow-y: hidden`). Chrome picks the scroll container that takes a gesture as it starts: this one cannot move vertically, and it may not pass the vertical axis on, so the gesture stays on it and nothing scrolls. Measured in headless Chromium with DevTools' touch gestures: a 200 px vertical swipe on the code block or the table left the conversation where it was; on the text beside them it scrolled the conversation 200 px; with only the sideways views' vertical axis back to `auto`, a swipe on the code block or the table scrolled the conversation, and a sideways swipe still scrolled the code block itself.
- **Fixed in the package.** `phone-package.ts` puts `<style id="deskfolk-page">.nsh-scroll.nsh-ox.nsh-ox { overscroll-behavior-y: auto; }</style>` (`PAGE_STYLE`) last in the page's `<head>`. Three classes win over the runtime's one-class rule wherever its sheet sits; the policy's `style-src` already has `'unsafe-inline'`. Only the sideways views' vertical axis changes: the vertical views keep their horizontal axis locked. This problem does not need it, and opening it would let every vertical list pass sideways swipes on. The fix belongs in NSH: upstream main has longbridge/nsh#2309 (a one-axis view locks only its own axis), and the axis where the content fits and there is nothing to scroll is reported as longbridge/nsh#2417, fixed in PR longbridge/nsh#2418 (`nsh-fits`, handing the swipe on as Android does). Once the pin carries both, `PAGE_STYLE` can go.
- **Later the same day: the short vertical ones too.** The first rule only changed the sideways views' vertical axis. Measured again: a command's card and its opened output are vertical scroll views which, with too little in them to scroll (113/113 and 143/143 px), keep `contain` just the same; a vertical swipe on them left the conversation where it was, while one on the line outside the card moved it. The rule is now `.nsh-scroll .nsh-scroll { overscroll-behavior: auto; }`: a scroll view inside another hands what it cannot take to the one around it, and the outermost, the conversation's list, keeps `contain`, so neither the page nor the browser's Back gesture is reached. A sideways swipe on a code block still scrolls the block. The e2e gains a step: with the command's output open, both have `overscroll-behavior-y: auto`, and in Chromium a vertical swipe on the output and on the command's line each moves the conversation more than 100 px; with the old rule the step fails (`contain, contain`), with the new one it passes in both browsers.
- **Checks.** `phone-runtime.ts` gives the Writer's conversation 24 more lines of history, and its reply a block of code with a long line and a six-column table, both wider than the phone. Once the conversation is open, `phone-web-e2e.ts` checks that both sideways views have `overscroll-behavior-y: auto`; in Chromium it swipes vertically by touch on the code block and on the table, and the conversation must move more than 100 px each time, then sideways on the code block, which must itself move more than 100 px. WebKit here cannot drive touch scrolling, so there only the style is checked. The whole browser context has `hasTouch`: without it NSH's lists scroll the way a desktop browser's do, with no native scrolling to measure. On the old package the step fails (`overscroll-behavior-y contain, contain`); with the new one the whole run passes in Chromium and WebKit. Not measured on iOS.

## 补记 2026-11-18（四十九）：手机页面上的代码和 Mac 上同一套配色 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (49): code on the phone's page in the Mac's colours

用户 11-18 说手机远程端「看到的输出代码没有高亮」。量下来：回复里带语言的代码块在 web 上有颜色，但那是 NSH 自带的 syntect 配色（`nsh-syntaxes.bin`），不是 Mac 上的 GitHub 配色；命令的输出（`CommandActivity` 的 `OutputText`）、文件编辑器的着色在 web 上一点没有，因为 giallo 不在 wasm 里（它的语法和主题是 zstd 压缩的一份，zstd 是 C）。补记六说的「暂时只在桌面」就是这个缺口。

- **giallo 进 wasm。** zstd 的 C 用打包脚本本来就设的 wasm32 clang（`CC_wasm32_unknown_unknown`，CI 和 Docker 镜像里也有）编得过，`giallo` 移到所有 target 的依赖里。内置的那份（1.4 MB，`builtin.zst`）不进 wasm：web 上从不调 `Registry::builtin()`，链接时就丢了。`phone-package.ts` 把它按内容命名为 `nsh-giallo.<10 位十六进制>.bin` 放进包里（边缘、清理脚本按这个模式永久缓存和修剪），在页面的 `nsh-app` 配置里登记为运行时数据 `giallo`，NSH 的页面按需取（`nsh_core::runtime_data`，和 `nsh-syntaxes.bin` 同一条路）。它本来就是 zstd，不带 `.br`/`.gz`。`web-dev.ts` 也放一份。
- **要显示代码时才取，先显示，再上色。** `Highlight.ready()`（async）在 web 上等一帧、取文件、读好；桌面上立刻好。web 上谁要显示代码谁调 `wantCodeColors()`（只第一次真去取）：带围栏代码块的消息、命令输出、代码编辑器；从不显示代码的页面（名单、设置、没有代码的会话）既不下载这 1.4 MB，也不读它（读一次约 150 ms 长任务、wasm 内存涨几十 MB）。只有带围栏代码块的消息读配色，所以取到以后只有它们重画，别的消息（连同里面的芯片和图片）不动。`code-colors.tsx` 的 `codeColorsAvailable()` 成了信号，`ready` 成功时置真：`MarkdownBody` 的三元、命令输出和编辑器的 `computed` 都读它，于是取到以后重画一遍。在那之前 `CodeColors` 给一个什么都不上色的高亮器，代码块先是纯文字：要是让 NSH 自带的配色先上，它会去取自己的 `nsh-syntaxes.bin`、编那门语言的正则，4 倍降速下这一下就是 2.2–2.4 秒的长任务，换来的颜色片刻之后又被换掉。`highlightCode`、`codeSurface` 不调原生，免得把「还没有」缓存下来。取不到（文件缺、读不了）时 `ready` 拒绝，控制台说一句，`codeColorsComing()` 变假，代码块改用 NSH 的配色。
- **每个代码块只上一次色。** 回复流式输出时文本视图每来一个字就把整条回复的代码块重画一遍，web 上这是页面的主线程。`block_styles_kept` 按语言、主题和代码记住最近 32 块的结果（桌面也用，只省不费）。还在长的最后一块每次照旧重算。
- **量过的。** `nsh.<hash>.wasm` 12,414,796 → 12,839,359 字节（gzip 后多约 150 KB，brotli 多约 143 KB）；数据文件 1,417,837 字节，第一次显示代码时多下这么多。CSP 不变（`script-src 'self' 'wasm-unsafe-eval'`）。单独编的探针 wasm 在 Bun（JSC）里：读这份 34 ms，第一次给 14 KB 的 TSX 上色 55 ms（编语法的正则），之后 7 ms。端到端的页面里在 Chromium 4 倍 CPU 降速下、停在 Writer 的会话里重载（单个长任务）：回复的代码块一出来就去取，读它约 160 ms，带色重画 213–228 ms，重载后约 1.3 秒上色，全程没取 `nsh-syntaxes.bin`；文件故意晚到 3 秒时也一样，先是纯文字，读 172 ms、重画 239 ms。对照：同一个包拦掉 giallo、让 NSH 自带的配色上，第一块 TS 代码那一下是 2.2–2.4 秒（取它的语法、编正则）。真机上没量。giallo 链接语法时打五条「External grammar … not found」警告，桌面上也一样，不影响。
- **检查。** `phone-runtime.ts` 给 Writer 那一轮记一条 `cat src/summary.ts` 和它的输出。`phone-web-e2e.ts` 在会话里等回复代码块的 `const`、命令输出的 `export` 变成 GitHub 浅色的关键字红（`rgb(215, 58, 73)`）；旧包上这一步失败（`const` 是 NSH 的紫），去掉数据文件时也失败但页面照常、控制台说明原因，新包 Chromium 和 WebKit 全程都过。`phone-package.test.ts` 查文件名、内容、配置登记和换一份时只留一份；`highlight.rs` 的测试查记住的结果和现算的一样。`.ui` 的 `code-colors`、`chat-transcript`、`chat-cards-activity` 都过。
- **许可。** giallo 是 EUPL-1.2，补记里早说过待用户确认；现在它也随手机页面发出去，`THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` 已写上。
- **端到端在 WebKit 里偶发失败，不是这次的。** 在「通过链接打开设置……最后落到名单」那一步之后、打开 Editor 会话并重载时，页面对八个 `blob:` 地址发起加载，被 `connect-src` 拦下（违规事件在主文档里，没有脚本位置，包住页面的 `fetch` 也一次没抓到；是什么在加载没查清），于是「页面违反了生产策略」。不含这次改动的 87c90176 上单独顺序跑：开触摸 2 次都失败，不开触摸 2 次失败 1 次；并行跑时多半能过。这里没修。
- **没做的。** 深色外观没在端到端里断言（手工量过一次 NSH 配色的深色是对的，giallo 的深色走同一条 `AdaptiveColor`）；流式输出时手机上的主线程开销没量；iOS 没看过。

On 11-18 the user said the code they saw on the phone had no highlighting. Measured: a reply's code block with a language was coloured on the web, but in NSH's own syntect colours (`nsh-syntaxes.bin`), not the Mac's GitHub colours; a command's output (`CommandActivity`'s `OutputText`) and the file editor had none on the web, because giallo was not in the wasm (its grammars and themes are one zstd dump, and zstd is C). That was the "desktop only for now" gap of addendum 6.

- **giallo goes into the wasm.** zstd's C compiles with the wasm32 clang the packaging scripts already name (`CC_wasm32_unknown_unknown`; CI and the Docker image have it too), so `giallo` moves to every target's dependencies. The built-in dump (1.4 MB, `builtin.zst`) stays out of the wasm: the web never calls `Registry::builtin()`, so the linker drops it. `phone-package.ts` puts it in the package named by its content, `nsh-giallo.<10 hex>.bin` (the pattern the edges cache for good and the prune keeps to), and names it in the page's `nsh-app` config as the runtime's data `giallo`, which NSH's page fetches on demand (`nsh_core::runtime_data`, the way `nsh-syntaxes.bin` comes). It is zstd already, so no `.br`/`.gz`. `web-dev.ts` puts one beside its build too.
- **Fetched when code is about to show; shown first, coloured after.** `Highlight.ready()` (async) waits a frame on the web, fetches the file and reads it; on the desktops it is ready at once. On the web whatever is about to show code calls `wantCodeColors()` (only the first call fetches): a text with a fenced code block, a command's output, the code editor. A page that never shows code (the roster, settings, a conversation without code) neither downloads the 1.4 MB nor reads it (about a 150 ms long task, and tens of MB more wasm memory). Only a text with a fenced code block reads the colours, so once they come only such texts are drawn again; the others, with their chips and pictures, stay. `code-colors.tsx`'s `codeColorsAvailable()` is a signal now, set once `ready` resolves: `MarkdownBody`'s ternary and the `computed`s of a command's output and the editor read it, so they draw again once the colours are there. Before that `CodeColors` provides a highlighter that colours nothing, so code blocks are plain for a moment: NSH's own colours first would fetch their own `nsh-syntaxes.bin` and compile the language's regexes, a 2.2–2.4 s long task at 4× CPU throttling, for colours replaced a moment later. `highlightCode` and `codeSurface` do not call the native side, so "nothing yet" is not cached. Without the file, or with one that cannot be read, `ready` rejects, the console says so, `codeColorsComing()` turns false, and code blocks take NSH's colours.
- **Each block coloured once.** While a reply streams, its text view draws all its code blocks again for every word, and on the web that is the page's main thread. `block_styles_kept` keeps the last 32 blocks' styles by language, themes and code (the desktops use it too; it only saves work). The last block, still growing, is coloured again each time.
- **Measured.** `nsh.<hash>.wasm` 12,414,796 → 12,839,359 bytes (about 150 KB more gzipped, 143 KB more with brotli); the data file is 1,417,837 bytes, downloaded once, the first time code shows. The policy is unchanged (`script-src 'self' 'wasm-unsafe-eval'`). A probe wasm built on its own, in Bun (JSC), read the dump in 34 ms and coloured a 14 KB TSX file in 55 ms the first time (compiling the grammar's regexes) and 7 ms after. In the e2e's page, under Chromium's 4× CPU throttling, reloading on the Writer's conversation (single long tasks): the fetch starts as the reply's code block shows, reading takes about 160 ms and the coloured redraw 213–228 ms, the colours are on about 1.3 s after the reload, and `nsh-syntaxes.bin` is never fetched; with the file held back 3 s it is the same, plain first, then 172 ms reading and a 239 ms redraw. For comparison, the same package with giallo blocked and NSH's own colours on: the first TS block cost 2.2–2.4 s (fetching its syntaxes, compiling the regexes). Not measured on a real phone. Linking the grammars, giallo logs five "External grammar … not found" warnings, on the desktops too; harmless.
- **Checks.** `phone-runtime.ts` records a `cat src/summary.ts` and its output in the Writer's turn. `phone-web-e2e.ts` waits for the reply's code block's `const` and the command output's `export` to turn GitHub light's keyword red (`rgb(215, 58, 73)`). On the old package the step fails (`const` in NSH's purple); without the data file it fails too while the page carries on and the console says why; with the new package the whole run passes in Chromium and WebKit. `phone-package.test.ts` checks the file's name and content, the config entry and that another dump leaves one file; `highlight.rs`'s test checks kept styles match fresh ones. The `.ui` tests `code-colors`, `chat-transcript` and `chat-cards-activity` pass.
- **Licence.** giallo is EUPL-1.2, which earlier addenda left for the user to confirm; it now ships in the phone's page as well, and `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` says so.
- **The e2e fails now and then in WebKit, not from this change.** After the step "settings opened by a link … the last Back lands on the roster", opening the Editor's conversation and reloading, the page starts loads of eight `blob:` addresses, which `connect-src` refuses (the violation is the main document's, with no script location, and a wrapper round the page's `fetch` never saw them; what loads them is not known), so the run fails on "the page broke the production policy". On 87c90176, without this change, run alone one after another: with touch it failed 2 runs of 2, without touch 1 of 2; run side by side it mostly passes. Not fixed here.
- **Not done.** The dark appearance is not asserted end to end (NSH's colours in dark were measured by hand once; giallo's dark takes the same `AdaptiveColor`). The main thread's cost while a reply streams on a phone is not measured. Not seen on iOS.

## 补记 2026-11-18（五十）：手机上在设置里连按返回不再退出应用 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (50): Back after Back in settings no longer leaves the app on a phone

用户说手机远程端设置里「逐层进入跟逐层退出的逻辑，现在不对」，追问后是：在模型/端点、提示词、界面里按返回，退一层以后再按就退出了应用。手机是 Android Chrome。补记四十五的做法是让设置对话框拒绝关闭、在 `onCancel` 里退一层；拒绝以后 NSH 把对话框的条目重新推上（`syncHistory` 的 `pushEntry`），这一推前面没有点按。Chromium 的 history manipulation intervention 对这样的页面把它同一文档的所有条目标成可跳过（crbug 1248529：同文档的返回/前进之后，之前的点按不再算数，要等新的点按），浏览器自己的返回（Android 的返回键和手势、工具栏的 ←、扩展的 `chrome.tabs.goBack`）就跳过它们，全都可跳过时直接离开页面。端点编辑器的 `stepsBack` 也一样：从它的第一页按返回，拒绝后过一轮才关，NSH 在这之间把编辑器的条目推回去了一次（量到的：返回前后历史条数从 9 变成 8）。

- **为什么一直没测出来。** 端到端用 `page.goBack()`（CDP 的 `navigateToHistoryEntry`，不跳）；更要紧的是 Playwright 在 Chromium 里的 `evaluate` 和所有定位查询都带 `userGesture: true`，两次返回之间的任何检查都等于点了一下页面，把可跳过的标记清掉了。同一个包、同一段路，换成浏览器自己的返回、两次返回之间只用不带手势的 CDP `Runtime.evaluate` 读页面，走到半路就落到了页面前面那条 `data:` 页上。
- **做法：每一层一条历史，在点进去的那次点按里写。** 设置在手机上点进去的每一页（某一项、它的某一节，端点编辑器里连接方式的表单、某个模型的页）各有一条历史，地址是设置的地址加 `#l<层数>`（`page-route.ts` 的 `LEVEL`）。片段不发给服务器，边缘的查询规则不用改。`SettingsModal` 的 `onPageLevels` 按层报给 `page-route`（设置一层，编辑器一层；对话框自己的第一页不算，服务器和提示词的编辑器是 Back 直接关的对话框，也不算）：层数变多就 `setUrl(push)`，NSH 在同一深度上推一条；返回在这些同深度的条目之间走，NSH 发 `change(back)`，`page-route` 按落到的层数调 `stepOutOfDialog` 退几层，什么也不写。层数变少而不是返回造成的（‹、✕），`page-route` 自己 `history.go` 退过那几条；一次只走一步，走到了再看要不要接着走，两次很快的 ‹ 不会多退。对话框关了，它那几层的条目由 NSH 自己退掉（`entryAtDepth`），`page-route` 不动。只有层数真的变多才写：返回退层时层数往下数，不会触发写。
- **手机页面上对话框不再拒绝返回。** 设置的 `backStepsWithin` 和编辑器的 `stepsBack` 在 `runtime.hosted` 时为假：返回走到对话框自己的条目，就是它的第一页，直接关；万一层数没对上（一次点按同时打开设置和更深的页，NSH 把那次 push 并进了对话框的条目），也只是关掉，不再推。NSH 自己关编辑器时（返回落到它自己的条目、滑动），手机页面上从哪一页都直接关（`Modal` 新增 `onDismiss`，编辑器在手机页面上给 `closeProviderEditor`）：先退一页再被 NSH 关掉，会多退过一条。Esc 在 web 上先到应用的快捷键（外壳的 `escape()`），不经过对话框：编辑器里退一页（同 ‹，`page-route` 退过那一页的条目），在第一页上关掉；设置从哪一层都关。这和改之前一样，没变。应用自己的窄窗口照旧逐层退。
- **刷新。** 在某一层刷新，设置开在列表上，`armFloor` 把这条的片段换掉；之后返回落到上一次加载留下的某一层（NSH 当 `open` 交回来）时，接着往回退一条，上一次加载的设置只经过一次。
- **检查。** `phone-web-e2e.ts` 在 Chromium 里用浏览器自己的返回（一个只调 `chrome.tabs.goBack` 的扩展，持久上下文，要完整的 Chromium），页面前面垫一条 `data:` 页，离开页面就失败。原有的一路返回现在核对每一层的 `#l<n>` 和「返回不写条目」（历史条数在返回前后一样）。新加：Esc 从编辑器的表单退到连接方式（`#l2`），再按关掉编辑器（地址仍在那一节的 `#l2`），之后返回到 `#l1`，Esc 从某一项关掉设置；中间什么都不按的连按：模型（表单、编辑器、一节、一项、列表、名单）、‹ 之后再返回、提示词（编辑器、组、组列表、列表、名单）、界面（有这一项时）；在某一节刷新后一次返回关掉设置，再返回经过上一次加载的设置一次、然后是名单。改之前模型那段走到半路就离开了页面（日志没记是第几次）；改之后每次返回历史指针正好退一格、条数不变。`page-route.test.ts` 加了同深度条目的假历史和六种情形（逐层返回不写、两次 ‹、对话框关掉不动、前进到已关的页、刷新、上一次加载的层）。WebKit 照旧用 `page.goBack()`；最后一次全程都过，之前两次每一步都过、只在最后的策略检查上红，是补记四十六、四十九记过的 `blob:` 那个老毛病。Agents 没有单走一段；真机没看过；CI 的 Linux 上还没跑过持久上下文和扩展。
- **顺带看到的（没修）。** 从链接打开设置（`/?o=settings`），页面第一次连上要是晚了（量到约 10 秒），设置在连上的那一刻关掉、停在名单上：`page-route` 那段时间什么也没做，`SettingsModal` 也还没挂上，关的是启动和连接那条路。端到端里是那次刷新多用了中继的握手（每个地址每分钟 10 次，页面和 Mac 共用回环）造成的晚；现在刷新之后等满一分钟再打开那个链接。主线 d06515b3 上没有这次刷新，那一步照常过。
- **还留着的（这次没改，同一个毛病）。** 量过的：Bot 的设置抽屉（`closeOnEscape={false}`，在 `onCancel` 里退一层），从某一节返回到列表没事，再返回就离开了页面。看代码推断、没量的：`page-route.moved` 里 `backMobileLayer` 之后的 `setUrl(push)`（会话里先关搜索、话题这类网址不带的层）、地板上 `backFromFloor` 之后的 push、设置从链接或刷新直接打开时 NSH 在加载时写的对话框条目：都是返回之后、或没有点按时写条目。根子在 NSH：被拒绝的返回如果 `history.forward()` 回到刚离开的那条，而不是推一条新的，就不会被跳过（没试过）。

The user said that on the phone, going into and back out of settings' levels was wrong now. Asked what exactly happened, they said Back in Models and its endpoints, Prompts or Interface stepped out once and then left the app. The phone runs Android Chrome. Addendum 45 had the settings dialog refuse to close and step out one level in `onCancel`. After that refusal NSH pushed the dialog's entry again (`syncHistory`'s `pushEntry`) with nothing pressed. Chromium's history manipulation intervention then marks every entry of that document skippable: after a same-document Back or Forward, earlier presses no longer count until a new one (crbug 1248529). The browser's own Back skips such entries, and that Back is Android's Back button and gesture, the toolbar's ←, or an extension's `chrome.tabs.goBack`. With all of them skippable it leaves the page. The endpoint editor's `stepsBack` did the same: from its first page Back was refused and the editor closed a turn later, and in between NSH pushed the editor's entry back once. That was measured: history went from 9 entries to 8 over the walk.

- **Why nothing caught it.** The e2e used `page.goBack()`, which is CDP's `navigateToHistoryEntry` and does not skip. More importantly, Playwright's `evaluate` and every locator query run with `userGesture: true` in Chromium, so any check between two Backs counts as a press on the page and clears the marks. With the same package and the same walk, but the browser's own Back and the page read between Backs only through a raw CDP `Runtime.evaluate` with no gesture, the walk landed partway through on the `data:` page put before the app.
- **The way: one entry per level, written under the press that opens it.**
  - Each page of settings opened on a phone gets an entry of its own: a tab, a section of it, the endpoint editor's connector form, a model's page. Its address is settings' with `#l<depth>` (`page-route.ts`'s `LEVEL`). A fragment never reaches the server, so the edge's query rule stays as it is.
  - `SettingsModal`'s `onPageLevels` reports the depths to `page-route` per dialog, settings then the editor. A dialog's own first page does not count. Neither do a server's or a prompt's editor, dialogs that Back simply closes.
  - A depth that grows is a `setUrl(push)`: NSH adds an entry at the same depth. Back moves between those same-depth entries. NSH sends a `change(back)`, `page-route` calls `stepOutOfDialog` once for each level the landed entry is under, and nothing is written.
  - A depth that falls without a Back (‹, ✕) has `page-route` go back over those entries with `history.go`. It makes one move at a time and decides on the next once it lands, so two quick ‹ do not overshoot.
  - A dialog that closes takes its levels' entries with its own (NSH's `entryAtDepth`), and `page-route` leaves them alone.
  - Only a real increase writes. A Back that steps out counts down, so it never writes.
- **On the phone's page a dialog no longer refuses Back.**
  - Settings' `backStepsWithin` and the editor's `stepsBack` are false when `runtime.hosted`. A Back that reaches a dialog's own entry is on its first page and closes it.
  - If the depths are ever out of step, the dialog is simply closed and nothing is pushed. That happens when one press opens settings and a deeper page together, and NSH folds that push into the dialog's entry.
  - When NSH closes the endpoint editor itself (a Back onto its own entry, a swipe), the editor closes from any of its pages on the phone's page: `Modal` gains `onDismiss`, which the editor sets to `closeProviderEditor` there. Stepping out of a page first and then being closed by NSH would go back over one entry too many.
  - On the web Escape reaches the app's shortcuts first (the shell's `escape()`), not the dialog. In the endpoint editor it steps back a page as ‹ does, and `page-route` goes back over that page's entry; on the editor's first page it closes the editor. Settings close from any level. This is as it was before the change.
  - The app's own narrow window still steps.
- **Reload.**
  - A reload on a level opens settings on its list, and `armFloor` replaces that entry's fragment.
  - A later Back onto a level left by the earlier load, which NSH hands over as an `open`, goes on back one entry. The earlier load's settings are passed through once.
- **Checks.**
  - `phone-web-e2e.ts` uses the browser's own Back in Chromium: an extension that only calls `chrome.tabs.goBack`, in a persistent context, which needs the full Chromium. A `data:` page sits before the app, and leaving the page fails the run.
  - The existing walk now checks each level's `#l<n>`, and that Back writes no entry (history has the same length before and after the walk).
  - New: Escape on the editor's form steps back to the connector tiles (`#l2`), and a second Escape closes the editor, with the address staying on the section's `#l2`. Back then goes on to `#l1`, and Escape on a tab closes settings.
  - New walks press Back after Back with nothing pressed between:
    - Models: the form, the editor, a section, the tab, the list, the roster.
    - ‹ followed by Back.
    - Prompts: the editor, the group, the groups, the list, the roster.
    - Interface, where that tab shows.
    - A reload on a section, after which one Back closes settings, the next passes through the earlier load's settings once, and the one after reaches the roster.
  - Before the change the Models walk left the page partway; the log does not say at which Back. After it, each Back moves the history index by exactly one and the length stays the same.
  - `page-route.test.ts` gains same-depth entries in its fake history and six cases: Back steps without writing, two ‹, a dialog closing leaves entries alone, Forward onto a closed page, a reload, an earlier load's level.
  - WebKit keeps `page.goBack()`. The last run passed throughout. Two earlier ones passed every step but ended red on the final policy check, the old `blob:` refusal of addenda 46 and 49.
  - Not checked: Agents has no walk of its own, no real phone was tried, and the persistent context and the extension have not run on CI's Linux yet.
- **Seen on the way (not fixed).**
  - Settings opened by a link (`/?o=settings`) close, leaving the roster, when the page's first connection comes late (about 10 s measured). `page-route` does nothing in that time and `SettingsModal` is not mounted yet: the close is on the start-up and connection path.
  - In the e2e the lateness came from the section reload using up the relay's handshakes: 10 an address a minute, with the page and the Mac sharing loopback. The e2e now waits out the minute after that reload before the link. On main at d06515b3, without that reload, the step passes.
- **Left as they were (the same fault, not changed here).**
  - Measured: a Bot's settings drawer, which has `closeOnEscape={false}` and steps out in `onCancel`. Back from a section to its list was fine, and the next Back left the page.
  - Read from the code, not measured, each writing an entry after a Back or with no press:
    - the `setUrl(push)` after `backMobileLayer` in `page-route.moved`, for layers the address does not carry, such as a conversation's search or thread;
    - the push after `backFromFloor` on the floor;
    - the dialog entry NSH writes at load when settings open from a link or a reload.
  - The root is in NSH: a refused Back that went `history.forward()` to the entry it had just left, instead of pushing a new one, would not be skipped. This is untested.
## 补记 2026-11-18（五十一）：链路回来时，往回读的人留在原处 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (51): a reader scrolled back keeps their place when the link comes back

用户 11-18 说「手机端打开一个项目会话，向上滚动，一段时间后经常会自动瞬间滚动到最底部位置」。

- **链路回来时拿的是快照。** 中继机 11-18 下午的日志里，手机的设备链路三小时里断开又连上约 40 次，有时每 10–12 秒一次（中继只记 `socket_closed`，代码 1000）；很多条链路给手机送了 586–862 KB，正是一份完整快照的量（稳定版运行时的 `/v1/snapshot` 未压缩 1.04 MB，74 个会话），不是补事件。Mac 的事件环（2000 条或 16 MB）写满时给每个客户端发 `resnapshot`，走的也是这条路；项目会话的代理在跑时每条回复都发一次带最多 4000 字进度的事件。
- **原因。** `fromRuntimeSnapshot` 里每个会话只有它的最后一行。装快照时打开着的会话缩成那一行，直到 `selectSession` 重新读它（手机一页 20 行，`REMOTE_MESSAGE_PAGE`）；读回来的这一页又经 `mergeSessionRows` 换掉了这个会话的全部消息，往回翻读进来的几页就没了。倒置的列表保持离最新一端的距离：内容塌下去时 web 的滚动视图把位置夹到最新一端，内容回来后读者就停在最底部（原生上落到别处）。用 `stage-scroll` 故事打成 web 包在 Chromium 里复现过：条目 30 → 1 → 0.7 秒后回到 30，离最新一行的距离 1600 → 0。
- **修法，在数据这层（`src/m`），Bot 的会话一样受益。**
  - `installSnapshot`：选中的会话原样留着手里的消息（`keepSessionLines`），不混入快照带来的最后一行：那一行可能在一段空缺之后，放在一起中间就缺行，直到重读（`chat-stage` 的界面测试撞到过：事件里拿到 1–122 行，快照的最后一行是 130）。别的会话照旧只剩最后一行，它们留下的 `kept` 也丢掉。
  - `resetConnection`：读过历史的会话在 `messageNext` 清空前把 `{ next, newest }`（游标、当时最新一行的位置）存进 `SessionView.kept`；连着断两次，留第一次的。
  - `applySessionDetail` 经 `firstPageOver`：重新读到的第一页伸进了先前读过的历史（它最旧的一行在手里、且不晚于 `kept.newest`，后面还有更早的）时，比它更旧的已有消息留下，翻页从留下的游标接着走；否则这一页就是历史（断开期间来了一页以上，或历史被清空）。这一页覆盖范围内、它没有的已有消息丢掉。同一连接里的重读（点到某一行的链接）用会话自己的游标。续上的链路恢复游标时清掉 `kept`。快照带来的最后一行不算读过的历史：一页只有一行时它不能被当成接口，否则中间会缺行（测试里先撞到过）。
  - 守护进程的游标是 `created_at|id`，是会话里的位置，和连接无关。
  - 合并时仍先写视图（`detailLoaded`、`messageNext`，会同步触发副作用），再读 `snapshot` 合并，和原来的顺序一样。
- **检查。** `runtime-remote.test.ts` 新增两条：经假中继断开、Mac 换了事件实例拿快照，重读期间会话还留着往回翻的行，读完后游标是旧的那个；第一页够不着已有历史时整体换掉。main 上第一条失败（重读时会话里只剩 `["m7"]`）。`session-history.test.ts` 新增四条（接上、快照的最后一行不算、整段历史的一页、没读过的会话）。`phone-web-e2e.ts` 新增一步：`phone-runtime.ts` 给 Claude Code 项目会话放 60 行；Chromium 里触摸往上滑过第一页；中继暂时不可达（`bounceEdge`），其间 Mac 改 320 次 Bot（超过 `RESUME_MAX_EVENTS`），链路回来只能拿快照；要求离最新一行的距离不变、顶上仍是第二页的行。main 上 2427 → 0 px，失败；修好后 2134 → 2134 px，整轮在 Chromium 里通过。WebKit 驱动不了触摸，这一步跳过。`.ui/chat-stage.test.tsx` 的长会话那条原先断言「打开时第 1 行不在」，其实靠的是贴消息时链路断了一次、会话被快照缩成最后一行；`numbered` 读的是索引条里所有手里的行，不是屏幕上的。去掉这条，「停在最底部」由跳到底部的按钮不可用来查，后面的翻页断言不变。
- **还没解决的。**
  - 链路为什么断得这么勤、为什么续不上（补事件被拒），没查清。
  - 断开那一刻还有一次滑动，main 上一样：中继不可达时，视口以下没渲染的行变回估算高度（约 94 px，量到的是 211），读者往更早的方向挪几百 px（量到 330–656 px，两三行）。发生在重连之前，原因还没找到。试过一次：让打开的会话在断开和重读期间留着游标（「更早」那一行和索引条不再翻转），这一轮只挪了约 219 px（一行）；只一个样本，又多改了几条状态路径，没有采用。
  - Bot↔Bot 弹窗（`peekSession`）走同一个 `heldHistory`，没有专门的测试；同一连接里点到某一行的重读有一条。
  - 别的打开着的会话（桌面的其它窗格、Bot↔Bot 弹窗）重连时仍只剩最后一行，直到重新读。

On 11-18 the user said that on the phone, scrolled up in a project session, the view often jumped to the very bottom by itself after a while.

- **The link comes back to a snapshot.** On the relay box on the afternoon of 11-18, the phone's device link closed and reopened about 40 times in three hours, sometimes every 10–12 s (the relay logs only `socket_closed`, code 1000). Many links carried 586–862 KB to the phone, the size of a whole snapshot (the stable runtime's `/v1/snapshot` is 1.04 MB uncompressed, 74 sessions), not a catch-up. The Mac's event ring (2000 events or 16 MB) running out sends every client `resnapshot`, the same path; a project session's agent at work sends an event per reply with up to 4000 characters of progress.
- **Cause.** `fromRuntimeSnapshot` holds each session's last line only. Installing it shrank the open conversation to that line until `selectSession` read it again (20 lines a page on the phone, `REMOTE_MESSAGE_PAGE`), and the page read went through `mergeSessionRows`, which replaced all of the session's lines, so the pages read back were gone. The inverted list keeps its distance from the newest end: as its content collapsed, the web's scroll view clamped to that end, and once the lines were back the reader was at the bottom (on the desktops they landed somewhere else). Reproduced with the `stage-scroll` story packaged for the web, in Chromium: items 30 → 1 → 30 again after 0.7 s, distance from the newest line 1600 → 0.
- **The fix is in the data (`src/m`), so a Bot's conversation gets it too.**
  - `installSnapshot`: the selected conversation keeps the lines held as they were (`keepSessionLines`), without the snapshot's last line: that line may come after a gap, and beside them it leaves the lines in between missing until the read (the `chat-stage` UI test hit it: lines 1–122 held from events, the snapshot's last line 130). The others are cut to their last line as before, and drop what they kept.
  - `resetConnection`: a view whose history was read stores `{ next, newest }` (its cursor, and where its newest line was) in `SessionView.kept` before `messageNext` is cleared; a link that drops twice keeps the first one's.
  - `applySessionDetail`, through `firstPageOver`: when the first page read again reaches into the history read before (its oldest line is held, no newer than `kept.newest`, and there is more behind it), the held lines older than it stay and paging goes on from the kept cursor; otherwise the page is the history (more than a page came meanwhile, or the history was cleared). Held lines inside the page's span that it lacks are dropped. A read again within one connection (a link to a line) uses the view's own cursor. A link that resumes restores the cursor and drops `kept`. The snapshot's last line is no part of the history read before: with a one-line page it must not count as the join, or lines in between go missing (a test hit that first).
  - The daemon's cursor is `created_at|id`, a position in the session, not tied to a connection.
  - The merge still writes the view first (`detailLoaded`, `messageNext`, whose effects run at once), then reads `snapshot` to merge, in the old order.
- **Checks.** `runtime-remote.test.ts` gains two tests: over a fake relay, a link that comes back to a Mac with a new event instance takes the snapshot, and during the read again the conversation still holds the lines paged back, with the old cursor after; a first page that does not reach what is held replaces it. On main the first fails (only `["m7"]` held during the read). `session-history.test.ts` gains four (the join, the snapshot's last line not counting, a page that is the whole history, a conversation not read yet). `phone-web-e2e.ts` gains a step: `phone-runtime.ts` gives the Claude Code project session 60 lines; Chromium swipes up by touch past the first page; the relay goes out of reach (`bounceEdge`) while the Mac changes a Bot 320 times (more than `RESUME_MAX_EVENTS`), so the link comes back to the snapshot; the distance from the newest line must hold and the top of the view still be the second page. On main 2427 → 0 px, a failure; with the fix 2134 → 2134 px, and the whole run passes in Chromium. WebKit cannot drive touch here, so the step is skipped there. The long conversation in `.ui/chat-stage.test.tsx` asserted that line 1 was not there on opening, which only held because the link dropped while the lines were posted and the snapshot cut the conversation to its last line; `numbered` reads every line held, through the index strip, not what the view shows. That assertion is gone; being at the foot is checked by the jump-down button being off, and the paging checks after it stay.
- **Still open.**
  - Why the phone's link drops so often, and why it is not resumed (the catch-up refused), is not known.
  - A slide remains at the moment the link drops, on main as well: with the relay out of reach, the rows below the view that are not drawn take estimated heights (about 94 px against 211 measured), and the reader moves towards older lines by a few hundred px (330–656 px measured, two or three lines). It comes before the reconnect; its cause is not found yet. Tried once: with the open conversation keeping its cursor through the drop and the read (so the "earlier" row and the index strip do not flip), that run moved about 219 px (one line); one sample, and it changes more state paths, so it is not in.
  - The Bot↔Bot popup (`peekSession`) goes through the same `heldHistory` with no test of its own; a read again within one connection, for a link to a line, has one.
  - Other open views (another pane on the desktop, the Bot↔Bot popup) are still cut to their last line on a reconnect until read again.

## 补记 2026-11-18（五十二）：底部面板由浏览器自己滑上来 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (52): the bottom sheet slides on the browser's compositor

第四十七条的改动上线以后，用户说手机上的动画还是卡，也不是手机慢：nginx 日志里，用户那台 Android（Chrome 157）19:04 已经加载了含那些改动的构建。用户另做的一个单页底部面板，在同一台手机上很流畅。那个面板每帧用 JS 写 `translate3d`，但带 `will-change`，页面的主线程也几乎闲着。

- **卡在哪。** `Sheet`（nsh-base）的入场每一步都要等主线程。对照页面在 CPU 降到六分之一时，入场开始后用 400 ms 的死循环占住主线程，用 CDP screencast 数合成器画出的帧：`Sheet` 在那 400 ms 里只画出 0–1 帧，面板停住；同样的面板改成样式过渡（`transitionProperty: "transform"`，web 上就是 CSS transition）是 9 帧，一路滑完。手机页面上（四分之一速、200 行的对话）线上版本是 0–1 帧，改后 14 帧。
- **怎么改。** `BottomSheet` 不再用 `Sheet`，而用 `Dialog`（`animation="none"`），里面放我们自己的遮罩和面板。面板从 `translate: "0 100%"` 出发，遮罩从 opacity 0 出发，对话框 `onOpen` 之后（另有 400 ms 兜底）面板以 240 ms `cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1)` 滑到 0，遮罩用 180 ms 淡入，和 Svelte 的面板一样。减少动态效果时直接停在终点。
- **一个坑：** 视图一建出来就带着过渡属性时，浏览器会从样式的默认值（位移 0）过渡到出发点，于是面板先在终点露一帧，再跳到半路滑回来；百分比和像素一样。所以出发前不带任何过渡属性，整个对话框也先是 opacity 0，到 `onOpen` 才一起换上。改后两个浏览器逐帧量过：第一帧就在屏幕外 224 px，然后 141、86、52、32……到 0。
- **遮罩是我们自己的。** `Dialog` 的遮罩被点时也会先调 `onCancel`，分级的面板会把它当成返回上一级。现在返回和 Esc 走 `onCancel`：有 `onBack` 时 `closeOnEscape` 为假，在 microtask 里上一级；没有时由对话框自己关，`onOpenChange` 里调 `onClose`。点遮罩直接调 `onClose`。
- **没改的。** 带输入框的面板（日历的成员筛选 `RosterFilter`）加 `holdsText`，仍用 `Sheet`，因为 `Sheet` 会把面板抬到键盘上面。`ModelPicker` 自己的 `Sheet`、nsh/base 的 `Select` 和菜单在手机上弹出的面板，还是走动画图。面板不再能下滑关掉，Svelte 的面板也不能。
- **检查。** 手机页面的端到端测试（`phone-web-e2e`）在 Chromium 和 WebKit 里都过了，包括返回先关面板、再关对话。`.ui` 里用到面板的文件都过了。面板落在底部，满宽，高 224 pt，和 `Sheet` 时一样。

After addendum 47 went live, the user said animations on the phone were still janky, and that the phone is not slow. The relay's nginx log shows the user's Android (Chrome 157) loaded the build with those changes at 19:04. A one-page bottom sheet the user made separately runs smoothly on the same phone. It writes `translate3d` from JS every frame, but it has `will-change`, and its main thread is mostly idle.

- **Where it stuck.** Every step of `Sheet`'s (nsh-base) entrance waits for the main thread.
