  - On the control page at 6× CPU throttle, the main thread was blocked for 400 ms by a busy loop once the entrance started, and the compositor's frames were counted from a CDP screencast. `Sheet` drew 0–1 frames in those 400 ms: the panel stood still. The same panel with a style transition (`transitionProperty: "transform"`, a CSS transition on the web) drew 9 and slid all the way.
  - On the phone page at 4× with a 200-line conversation, the live build drew 0–1 frames and the new one 14.
- **What changed.**
  - `BottomSheet` no longer uses `Sheet`. It is a `Dialog` (`animation="none"`) holding a scrim and a panel of our own.
  - The panel starts at `translate: "0 100%"` and the scrim at opacity 0. After the dialog's `onOpen` (or a 400 ms fallback), the panel slides to 0 over 240 ms on `cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1)` and the scrim fades in over 180 ms, as the Svelte sheet did.
  - With reduced motion it is already where it ends.
- **A trap.**
  - A view made with transition properties already set transitions from the style's default (no offset) to where it starts. The panel then showed in place for a frame, jumped halfway and slid back. A percentage and a pixel offset both did this.
  - So nothing carries a transition before the start, and the dialog stays at opacity 0 too; both change at `onOpen`.
  - Measured frame by frame in both browsers after the change: the first frame is 224 px below the screen, then 141, 86, 52, 32 … 0.
- **The scrim is ours.** `Dialog`'s scrim calls `onCancel` on a press too, and a sheet with levels would take that for Back.
  - Back and Escape go through `onCancel`. With `onBack`, `closeOnEscape` is false and the sheet steps up a level in a microtask. Without it, the dialog closes itself and `onOpenChange` calls `onClose`.
  - A press on the scrim calls `onClose` directly.
- **Unchanged.**
  - A sheet with a text field (the calendar's member filter, `RosterFilter`) takes `holdsText` and stays on `Sheet`, which lifts it above the keyboard.
  - `ModelPicker`'s own `Sheet`, and the sheets that nsh/base's `Select` and menus open on a phone, still run on the animation graph.
  - A sheet no longer closes by swiping it down. The Svelte one didn't either.
- **Checks.**
  - `phone-web-e2e` passes in Chromium and WebKit, including Back closing the sheet first and then the conversation.
  - The `.ui` files that open sheets pass.
  - The panel sits at the bottom, full width, 224 pt tall, as it did on `Sheet`.

## 补记 2026-11-18（五十三）：项目会话的行不再一次次重新生成 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (53): a project session's rows are no longer made anew again and again

补记五十一部署后，用户说「问题还是存在，不像是重连引起的问题，没有看到任何重连的提示」。中继日志也对得上：跳动发生在一条一直连着的链路上。

- **复现。** 用手机端 e2e 那一套做了个临时脚本：真中继、隔离的 Mac、Hosted 页面、Chromium 触摸、手机宽度。项目会话里放用户一个真实项目会话的 10 行（两遍），往上翻，然后让 Mac 只做一件事：这个会话来一行；别的会话来一行，或者别的会话里有代理在跑；这个会话里代理在跑。三种都跳。离最新一行的距离从约 2720 px 变成约 1300 px，落点每次一样，和读者原来在哪无关。只要事先把整段都滚过一遍、所有行都量过，落点也还是那里。列表没有重新挂载。原生无头环境里同样的操作不跳。
- **机制，逐帧看 DOM。** 来一行的那一帧里，列表先 `scrollTop += -4496`，浏览器夹到 0（丢掉 1417 px），内容的撑高层变成 1988 px（21 行 ×（估计的 80 + 间距 14），也就是每一行的量好的高度都被丢了）；接着窗口落在顶上，把上面从没画过的行都量了，内容涨到 10118 px，再 `scrollTop += 7961`。核心本来想的是净移动为零、保持离最新一端的距离，夹掉的那一截就是读者被挪走的距离。约 40 ms 后又来一遍。
- **为什么会把所有行当新的。** `ProjectChat` 的 `inverted()` 是普通函数，每次读都重新生成每一行的对象：一条新消息之后约 200 ms 里它被求值了 18 次，列表的 `each` 也跟着变了好几次。至少有一次 NSH 的列表把所有行当成新的，把量好的高度全扔了（`keyExtractor` 一样，所以按理不该这样，NSH 里具体哪一步没有查清）。Bot 的会话（`ChatStage`）从 11-08 起就用 `computed` 加行保持器（`createRowKeeper`），不受影响。
- **修法。** `project-rows.ts`：`createProjectRowKeeper()` 在一行显示的内容没变时交回原来那个对象（同一行消息按对象相同，或者重新取来的、序列化后相同；日期分隔线相同）；`sameRows` 逐个比对象。`ProjectChat` 的 `inverted` 改成 `computed(() => keepRows(rows().slice().reverse()), { equals: sameRows })`：没有哪一行变时交给列表的还是同一个数组。修好后同样一条新消息只有一次 `scrollTop += 94`（新行的估计高度），内容没有塌下去，离最新一行的距离一点没变，`inverted` 求值从 18 次降到 3 次。
- **补记五十一留下的断开时的滑动也是它。** 断开时 `messageNext` 清空、运行状态变化，同样让行被重新生成；修好后 e2e 里中继断开再连回来拿快照，顶上那一行和它的位置都不变。
- **检查。** `project-rows.test.ts` 四条：同样的行还是同一批对象、同一个数组；重新取来的同样一行还是原对象；只有变了的行是新对象；加上日期分隔线的行是新对象。`phone-web-e2e.ts` 的项目会话那一步加了一个断言，并把重连那一项收紧：往上翻过第一页后，Mac 往 Writer 的会话里写一行（它那一轮在固定端点上马上失败）。顶上那一行要不变，位置差不超过 40 px，离最新一行的距离也差不超过 40 px；中继断开再连回来以后同样要求。旧代码上这一步失败（顶上从「项目第 20 问」变成「项目第 18 答」），修好后整轮 Chromium 通过。`nsh test` 全部通过（818 过，1 跳过），`nsh check App.tsx`、desktop-nsh 的 `tsc`、bun 两条线、行数预算、界面测试 chat-stage / chat-reading / conversations / phone-web 都过。
- **没做的。** NSH 里为什么键一样还会把所有行当新的、网页上中间那次负向滚动被夹掉，这两件事都该在 NSH 里修（核心应该只发净移动，或者先改内容高度再滚），还没去上游提。WebKit 上没有触摸可驱动，这一步只在 Chromium 跑。真机上还没确认。

After addendum 51 was deployed, the user said the jump was still there and did not look like a reconnect: no reconnecting notice showed. The relay's log agrees: the jumps came on a link that stayed up.

- **Reproduced.** A scratch copy of the phone e2e: real relay, isolated Mac, the Hosted page, Chromium with touch, a phone's width. The project session held the 10 lines of one of the user's real project sessions, twice over. The reader scrolled up, and then the Mac did one thing: a line in this session; a line in another session, or an agent at work in another one; an agent at work in this one. Each one jumped the reader: the distance from the newest line went from about 2720 px to about 1300 px. The landing place was the same every time, whatever the reader had been reading, and it stayed the same with every row measured beforehand by scrolling through it all. The list was not mounted again. Native headless does not jump on the same steps.
- **The mechanism, frame by frame in the DOM.** In the frame a line arrives, the list first does `scrollTop += -4496`, which the browser clamps at 0, losing 1417 px. The content's spacer becomes 1988 px: 21 rows × (the 80 estimate + 14 gap), every measured height dropped. The window, now at the top, then measures the rows above that were never drawn, the content grows to 10118 px, and `scrollTop += 7961` follows. The core meant no net move, keeping the distance from the newest end; the part the browser clamped away is how far the reader was moved. About 40 ms later it happens again.
- **Why every row counted as new.** `ProjectChat`'s `inverted()` was a plain function that made every row's object anew on each read: in the 200 ms after one new line it ran 18 times, and the list's `each` changed several times with it. At least once NSH's list took all the rows as new and dropped their measured heights. With the same `keyExtractor` keys it should not; which step in NSH does it is not pinned down. A Bot's conversation (`ChatStage`) has used `computed` with a row keeper (`createRowKeeper`) since 11-08 and does not do this.
- **The fix.** `project-rows.ts`: `createProjectRowKeeper()` hands back a row's previous object while what it shows is the same: the same line by identity, or one fetched again that serializes the same, and the same day line. `sameRows` compares object by object. `ProjectChat`'s `inverted` is now `computed(() => keepRows(rows().slice().reverse()), { equals: sameRows })`, so while no row changed the list is handed the same array. With it, the same new line makes one `scrollTop += 94` (the new row's estimate), the content does not collapse, the distance from the newest line does not change, and `inverted` runs 3 times instead of 18.
- **Addendum 51's slide at the drop was this too.** At a drop `messageNext` is cleared and the run's state changes, which made the rows anew the same way. With the fix, a relay that goes out of reach and a link that comes back to the snapshot leave the same line at the top, at the same place.
- **Checks.** `project-rows.test.ts` has four tests: the same rows come back as the same objects and the same list; a line fetched again that says the same keeps its row; only a row that changed is a new object; a line that gains its day line is a new row. In `phone-web-e2e.ts`'s project-session step, after swiping back past the first page, the Mac writes a line into the Writer's conversation (its turn fails at once against the fixture's endpoint). The line at the top must not change, its place must move by 40 px at most, and so must the distance from the newest line. The reconnect check now asks the same. On the old code the step fails (the top went from 「项目第 20 问」 to 「项目第 18 答」); with the fix the whole Chromium run passes. `nsh test` passes (818 passed, 1 skipped), as do `nsh check App.tsx`, desktop-nsh's `tsc`, both bun lanes, the line budget, and the UI tests chat-stage, chat-reading, conversations and phone-web.
- **Not done.** Two things belong in NSH and are not reported upstream yet: why rows with the same keys counted as new, and that the intermediate negative scroll gets clamped on the web (the core should send only the net move, or change the content's height before it scrolls). WebKit has no touch to drive here, so the step runs in Chromium only. Not confirmed on the real phone yet.

## 补记 2026-11-18（五十四）：手机上打开的文件盖在会话上面，工作区里点开的文件不再退回去 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (54): a file opened on the phone covers the conversation, and one opened in the workspace stays

用户说手机端「无法打开终端，也无法打开工作区，还有项目目录，以及任何产物面板」，接着补充「终端不是无法打开，是打开后一个空白页面，文件相关的都是无法打开点击没有反应」。在本机起了和线上同一个构建（`nsh-w/phone-deploy` 的 b99772dc 包、本地中继、隔离的 Mac，Chromium 手机尺寸、触摸点按），用 `elementFromPoint` 看手指落点最上层是什么。终端那一半（主栏没有终端页）另一个会话在做，这里只记文件这一半，和顺带查到、交给那边的几处。

- **文件盖在了会话下面。** 消息里点文件、项目右键菜单的「打开项目目录」、流程里点产物，预览其实都打开了（`artifact-preview` 在页面里，宽 195），最上层却还是会话。没有 workbench 的地方，`ShellPreview` 只是外壳那一行里的一个 flex 子项，压在盖住这一行的主栏（`shell-main`，absolute，z 10）和流程页（z 70）下面。Svelte 外壳这里是 `.shell.is-layered.is-preview .artifact-pane { position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 80 }`，移植时只带过来了 `sheet` 的头部。现在 `!wide` 时预览放进一个盖满窗口的 `Overlay`（`PreviewSheet`），和工作区页一样；手机、平板上的远程页、桌面的窄窗口都走这条。
- **工作区里点开的文件会自己退回去。** 工具里的工作区能打开，里面点开的文件也显示，但手机的地址不写工作区里的文件（远程页上 `sessionUrl` 去掉 `w`），而 `page-route` 应用地址的那个 effect 随快照的会话、Bot、连接变化重跑：拿地址里的 `{ workspace, selected: null }` 和当前比，不一样就 `applyOverlay`，`workspaceSelected` 被清成空，页面回到「从左侧打开一个文件」。测试的 Mac 很安静，本地点得开；用户的 Mac 上 Bot 一直在干活，快照不停地变。现在地址说「工作区开着」、工作区也开着时什么都不做。工作区页在手机上也不再在底部空出 60 点（给底栏留的，可工作区开着时底栏是藏起来的）。
- **返回。** 没有附件撑着的文件（消息里引用的路径、项目目录：替身行的 id 以 `virtual-` 开头，里面是路径）原来不进地址（`sanitizeBotId` 不收 `/`），没有自己的历史条目，返回直接拿走了它下面的会话，文件留在名单上。先试过让 `backMobileLayer` 先关这样的文件：关得掉，但 `page-route` 随后把会话的地址再推一条，这一推前面没有点按，正是补记五十记的那种写法；端到端里用浏览器自己的返回、中间什么都不按地再返回一次，就离开了页面。现在这样的文件在地址里是 `a=file`（`ATTACHMENT_UNNAMED`、`attachmentParam`）：不带路径（路径也不该进中继的日志），刷新打不开它，只是让它在打开它的那次点按里有一条自己的历史，和附件的 `?a=<id>` 一样，返回退过这一条、什么都不写；地址落到 `a=file` 时 `applyAttachment` 不动开着的东西，刷新也不按它恢复。
- **检查。** `.ui/shell.test.tsx` 一条：窄窗口里预览盖住会话、它的 ‹ 按得到（被盖住时 `press` 报 covered），主线的 `ShellPreview` 上失败。`page-route.test.ts` 两条（主线上都失败），`session-url.spec.ts` 一条。`phone-web-e2e.ts` 新增三段（Chromium 触摸点按，看手指落点）：消息里的文件盖住会话，返回只收起文件、会话还在，Chromium 里再中间什么都不按地返回一次，到名单、页面还在（只经一个不带手势的 CDP 会话读页面，同设置的连按）；工具里的工作区，点开的文件在 Mac 改了一个 Bot 的名字之后还在，返回收起；项目右键菜单打开项目目录，盖住项目会话，返回先收起目录再是会话（Chromium 里同样连按）。整轮在 Chromium 里通过。WebKit 里整轮也通过（WebKit 用 `page.goBack()` 返回，连按只在 Chromium 里测）。真机没看过，线上要重新部署才有。
- **查到的、交给终端那一边的。** 在带终端页的一版上量的（本地分支 `phone-terminal-page`）：
  - NSH 的 `Terminal` 把尺寸变化推迟到稳定以后（`resize_after`），这个定时器在终端销毁后还会跑，行重算时读到我们 `RuntimeEmulator` 里随 `RuntimeTerminals` 一起销毁的 `dark`，每帧 panic（`nsh_terminal.rs` 的 `line()`）。会话结束时终端刚变过大小就会触发；用 `try_get()` 读就不会。
  - web 上触摸点按不拦 `mousedown` 的默认动作，按键栏上的键会把终端隐藏输入框的焦点拿走，手机上键盘每按一次就收起。按下时（`onPressDown`）再 `focus()` 一次终端，NSH 记下 `pressFocus`，就拦下这次失焦。手指点终端本身不聚焦输入框（和 Svelte 手机页一致），键盘靠按键栏的键。
  - 一次读终端列表，守护进程答在某个终端事件之前、落地在它之后，会把事件撤销；读取期间来过事件就重读。
  - 守护进程 `Terminals.remove` 之后，挂断的 shell 退出落地，又把这一行记回存储并再发一次 `terminal.upsert`（那边也修了这处）。

The user said that on the phone they could not open the terminal, the workspace, a project's folder or any artifact panel, then added that the terminal did open but as a blank page, and that pressing anything file-related did nothing. The live build was run on this Mac (the `nsh-w/phone-deploy` package of b99772dc, a local relay, an isolated Mac, Chromium at a phone's size pressing by touch), and `elementFromPoint` told what was on top where a finger lands. The terminal half (the main column had no terminal page) is another session's work; this addendum records the file half, and what turned up on the way and went to that side.

- **Files lay under the conversation.** A file pressed in a message, a project's 打开项目目录 from its row's menu, an artifact in the flow: the preview did open (`artifact-preview` was in the page, 195 wide), yet the conversation stayed on top. Where there is no workbench, `ShellPreview` was a flex child of the shell's row, under the main column that covers that row (`shell-main`, absolute, z 10) and under the flow page (z 70). The Svelte shell had `.shell.is-layered.is-preview .artifact-pane { position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 80 }` there; the port brought over only the `sheet` head. Now with `!wide` the preview goes into an `Overlay` that fills the window (`PreviewSheet`), as the workspace page does: on a phone, on the hosted page at a tablet's width and in a narrow desktop window.
- **A file opened in the workspace went back by itself.** The workspace from the tools opened, and a file pressed in it showed. But the phone's address never names the workspace's file (`sessionUrl` drops `w` on a remote page), and `page-route`'s effect that applies the address runs again on every change of the snapshot's sessions, Bots and connection. It compared the address's `{ workspace, selected: null }` with what was open and, as they differed, called `applyOverlay`, which cleared `workspaceSelected`: the page went back to 从左侧打开一个文件. The test Mac was quiet, so the file stayed there; the user's Mac has Bots at work and its snapshot keeps changing. Now an address at the workspace while the workspace is open does nothing. On a phone the workspace page also no longer stops 60 points short of the foot, room kept for a bottom bar that is hidden while it is open.
- **Back.** A file no attachment backs (a path cited in a message, a project's folder: their stand-in rows' ids start `virtual-` and hold the path) was left out of the address (`sanitizeBotId` takes no `/`), so it had no history entry of its own, and Back took the conversation from under it, leaving the file over the roster. Having `backMobileLayer` close such a file first was tried: it closed, but `page-route` then pushed the conversation's address again with no press, the very write addendum 50 warns about; in the e2e, the browser's own Back and then another with nothing pressed between left the page. Now such a file is `a=file` in the address (`ATTACHMENT_UNNAMED`, `attachmentParam`). It holds no path (a path should not reach the relay's logs either) and a reload cannot open it again; it only gives the file an entry of its own under the press that opens it, as an attachment's `?a=<id>` has, and Back goes back over that entry writing nothing. An address at `a=file` leaves what is open alone (`applyAttachment`), and a reload does not seed it.
- **Checks.** `.ui/shell.test.tsx`, one: at a narrow width the preview covers the conversation and its ‹ can be pressed (`press` reports a covered view), failing with main's `ShellPreview`. `page-route.test.ts`, two, both failing on main, and `session-url.spec.ts`, one. `phone-web-e2e.ts` gains three parts, pressing by touch in Chromium and reading where a finger lands: a file in a message covers the conversation, and Back puts away only the file, then in Chromium another Back with nothing pressed between reaches the roster with the page staying (the page read only through a CDP session with no gesture, as in the settings walk); the workspace from the tools, where a file opened stays after the Mac renames a Bot, and Back puts it away; a project's folder from its row's menu covers its conversation, and Back puts away the folder, then the conversation (Back after Back too in Chromium). The whole run passes in Chromium. WebKit passes the whole run too (it goes back with `page.goBack()`, so Back after Back is checked in Chromium alone). No real phone was tried; the live page needs a redeploy.
- **Found on the way, handed to the terminal side.** Measured on a version with the terminal page (the local branch `phone-terminal-page`):
  - NSH's `Terminal` applies a size once it has settled (`resize_after`), and that timer still runs after the terminal is gone. The rows compute again and read `dark` in our `RuntimeEmulator`, disposed with `RuntimeTerminals`, and panic on every frame (`line()` in `nsh_terminal.rs`). A session ended just after its terminal changed size sets it off; reading it with `try_get()` does not.
  - On the web a touch tap leaves the browser's default `mousedown` alone, so a key of the bar takes the focus from the terminal's hidden input, and a phone's keyboard goes down at every key. Focusing the terminal again as the finger goes down (`onPressDown`) makes NSH record `pressFocus` and keep the field focused. A finger on the terminal itself does not focus its input (as on the Svelte phone page); the keyboard comes from the bar's key.
  - A terminal list read the daemon answered before a terminal event and that lands after it undoes the event; a read that an event overtook is read again.
  - After the daemon's `Terminals.remove`, the hung-up shell's exit lands, keeps the row in the store again and publishes another `terminal.upsert` (that side fixes this one too).

## 补记 2026-11-18（五十五）：桌面上关标签就结束终端，手机上的终端页接回已经开着的 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (55): closing a terminal tab ends it on the desktop; the phone's terminal page reattaches to the one already running

用户说：桌面上的终端不用显示「结束会话」，关掉就自动结束；远程手机端应当自动接回已经存在的终端。这推翻了 [ADR 0025](0025-desktop-pane-workbench.md) 里「关标签不杀进程」那一条。

- **桌面：标签就是 shell。** `PaneTerminal` 的头上只剩路径、状态和「停止」。结束放在布局提交的那一处（`ShellWorkbench.commitLayout`）：提交前后各数一遍标签指着的会话（`terminal-tabs.ts` 的 `closedTerminals`，平铺和浮动的都算），新布局里没有了的就结束（`runtime.endTerminal`，404 不算错）。所以不管是标签的 ×、关整块窗格、「关闭其他 / 右侧 / 全部」，还是同类替换，都一样；拖到别的窗格、浮起、停靠不改变那个集合，什么也不结束。两种提交不结束：自愈（它丢的本来就是守护进程已经没有的会话），和「重置布局」（那些会话照跑，在「＋」和空窗格的「接回」里）。会话列表已经读过时，只结束列表里还有的。关标签不再问：用户要的就是关掉即结束。Windows 和 Linux 上 Ctrl+W 原来不管焦点在哪都关标签（NSH 移植时丢了 `matchesCloseTab` 里「终端留着 Ctrl+W」那条），以前只是拿走，现在会结束 shell；所以当前窗格前面是终端时，Ctrl+W 交给它（readline 的删词），Ctrl+Shift+W 在哪都关标签，和 Windows Terminal 一样。键位表没有焦点范围，「键盘所在窗格前面那个标签」代替「有焦点」。
- **手机（以及没有工作台的窄窗）：终端页。** NSH 的手机页面一直没有终端页：「工具 → 终端」打开的是空白。新的 `TerminalPage` 放在 `MainPages` 里，排在对话前面（打开终端时选中的对话还在下面）。
  - 打开时接回（`pageTerminal`）：上次在这页看的那条还在就是它（它结束了也留着，看得到怎么结束的），不然是最新一条还在跑的。记在运行时的 `pageTerminalId` 上，所以离开再回来还是它；`openTerminalAt`（「在项目目录打开终端」）把新起的那条写进去。
  - 这一页打开后、在当前链路上读到一次列表（`refreshTerminals` 现在返回读没读到）之前，空列表不算「没有」，免得通过地址打开或者重连时在已有的旁边又起一个。读到了、一条都没在跑、有工作区，才起一个，每次打开只起一次；看过一条以后再把最后一条结束掉，页面留空，给「新建」，不自己再起。
  - 头上：返回、会话名和目录（点开是会话列表：切换，或「结束会话」，还在跑的要按第二下）、「＋」。下面是 `TERMINAL_KEY_ROWS` 两排键。Ctrl 锁到下一个键，软键盘敲的字也算（`NshTerminal` 的 `typed`）。方向键和粘贴要知道程序开没开应用光标键（模式 1）和括号粘贴（模式 2004），NSH 的 `Terminal` 不给，所以 `NshTerminal` 写屏时自己扫一遍（`privateMode`）。键盘键在键盘弹着时收起，不然把焦点给终端。
  - 终端只在链路连着时挂上：断了收起，连回来重新读屏幕再 watch，漏掉的字节不会把屏幕弄乱。第一次读列表失败而链路还在时，两秒后再读。手机浏览器在软键盘弹起时只缩视觉视口，不缩布局，所以整页按 `keyboardFrame()` 往上让，按键栏留在键盘上面；顶部按安全区留白。按会话做 key（一项的 `Each`），切到另一条就是另一个终端，从它自己的屏幕接上。
- **离开就不再收字节。** `NshTerminal` 卸下时 `unwatchTerminal`，和 Svelte 的 `detach()` 一样；不然离开这一页后，跑着的 `pnpm dev` 的输出还一直经中继发到手机上。守护进程按客户端记 watcher，不计数，所以一个客户端同一条会话同时只该有一个视图，桌面一条会话一个标签，手机一页一个，成立。
- **一个早就有的竞态，这次每次都撞上。** `remove()` 先发 `terminal.removed`，被挂断的 shell 退出后，退出回调又 `emit` 了一次 upsert（退出码 1），客户端就把刚关掉的会话当成「已结束」加回了列表，空窗格的「接回」里和手机的列表里都有它，再结束就 404。原来的「结束会话」也会，只是靠刷新抢在前面，大多数时候看不出来；关标签即结束以后，两个客户端同时连着时每次都出。修在两头：守护进程的退出回调先看这条还是不是它自己的（`this.entries.get(id) !== entry` 就什么都不做），加了测试，去掉修复时测试变红；客户端记下被移除的 id，之后再来的 upsert 不收（`TerminalList.upsertTerminal` / `dropTerminal`），因为用户正在跑的守护进程要重启后才有修复。
- **检查。** `.ui/terminal-pane.test.tsx`（4 条：关标签就结束、两个关一个、退出后关标签、粘贴）和新的 `.ui/terminal-page.test.tsx`（4 条：接回桌面开着的那条且不另起、没有时起一条并接回它、「＋」和切换、按键栏和 Ctrl）在真 shell 上过了；`terminal-tabs.spec.ts`、`terminals.spec.ts`、`terminal-keys.spec.ts`、`runtime-terminal.test.ts`、守护进程的 `terminals.test.ts` 过了。Windows 上 Ctrl+W 删词、Ctrl+Shift+W 关标签也有一条 `.ui`，去掉修复时它变红。新的客户端配旧的守护进程（用户的 17890 重启前就是这样）把这 10 条终端 `.ui` 再跑了一遍，全过：客户端那道防线够用。`phone-web-e2e` 加了一步：Mac 上先开一个 shell 跑一行，手机页面打开终端页，经中继读到那一行，守护进程里还是一条，按键栏的回车把手机上打的一行送到 Mac，按地址重新加载后还是那一条、没有另起，返回之后那条还在跑。
- **没做的。** 键按住不连发；粘贴读剪贴板，iOS 上会弹系统的粘贴提示；软键盘弹起时页面不另外让位（和页面上别的地方一样，交给浏览器）。几个客户端看同一条会话时 pty 的行列跟最后一个调整大小的走，手机接回桌面开着的那条，会把它变窄，桌面那边下次调整大小才变回来，这和以前一样。变窄时 zsh 重画的两行提示符在手机上画得不对：那两行比网格高出约半行，和上下行叠在一起，`╰─` 不见了，下面的行又回到网格上；还没查是 NSH web 的行高还是变窄后的重画。WebKit 没跑这次的 e2e。钉住的 NSH（48559f8）已经带着 nsh#2095（组字完才发），手机页面上用输入法打字，不会先把拼音发给 shell。

The user said the desktop terminal should not show 结束会话 (End session): closing it should end the session; and the phone should reattach to the terminals that already exist. This reverses "closing a tab does not kill the process" in [ADR 0025](0025-desktop-pane-workbench.md).

- **Desktop: a tab is its shell.**
  - `PaneTerminal`'s head keeps the path, the status and Stop.
  - The end happens where the layout is committed (`ShellWorkbench.commitLayout`). The sessions the tabs name, tiled and floating, are counted before and after (`closedTerminals` in `terminal-tabs.ts`), and any the new layout no longer shows is ended (`runtime.endTerminal`; a 404 is not an error). The tab's ×, closing a pane, Close Others / to the Right / All, and a same-kind replace all go through it. Moving, floating and docking leave the set as it was and end nothing.
  - Two commits end nothing: healing, which drops only sessions the daemon no longer has, and Reset Layout, whose shells keep running under 接回 (Reattach) in + and the empty pane. Once the list has been read, only sessions still in it are ended.
  - Closing asks nothing: ending on close is what the user asked for.
  - On Windows and Linux, Ctrl+W closed the tab wherever the focus was (the NSH port lost `matchesCloseTab`'s "a terminal keeps Ctrl+W"). That used to put the shell away; now it would end it. So with a terminal in front of the focused pane, Ctrl+W goes on to it (readline's delete a word back), and Ctrl+Shift+W closes the tab from anywhere, as in Windows Terminal. The keymap has no focus scope, so "in front of the pane the keyboard is in" stands for "has the keyboard".
- **Phone (and a narrow window, which has no workbench): the terminal page.** The NSH phone page had none: Tools → Terminal opened a blank page. `TerminalPage` now sits in `MainPages`, ahead of the conversation, which stays selected under it.
  - It reattaches (`pageTerminal`): the session it showed last while the daemon still has it (one that finished there stays, so you see how it ended), else the newest one running. That is `pageTerminalId` on the runtime, so leaving and coming back finds the same one; `openTerminalAt` (Open terminal in project folder) writes the shell it started there.
  - Until a list has been read on the current link since the page opened (`refreshTerminals` now says whether it was), an empty list is not "none": a page opened by its address, or over a link just back, must not start a shell beside the one it is about to find. Read, nothing running, a workspace set: it starts one, once per opening. Once it has shown a shell, ending the last one leaves the page empty with New, not a shell started behind your back.
  - The head has Back, the session's name and folder (a press lists the sessions, to switch or to end one, a running one on a second press), and +. Under the terminal are the two rows of `TERMINAL_KEY_ROWS`. Ctrl latches for the next key, keyboard letters included (`NshTerminal`'s `typed`). The arrows and Paste need application cursor keys (mode 1) and bracketed paste (mode 2004), which NSH's `Terminal` keeps to itself, so `NshTerminal` scans what it writes (`privateMode`). The keyboard key hides the keyboard when it is up and focuses the terminal otherwise.
  - The terminal is mounted only while the link is up: down, it goes; back, it reads the screen and watches again, so bytes missed meanwhile can't garble it. It is keyed by session (a one-item `Each`), so another session is another terminal, attached from its own screen. A first list read that fails while the link is up is tried again two seconds later.
  - A phone's browser shrinks only the visual viewport under the soft keyboard, not the layout, so the page moves up by `keyboardFrame()` and the key bar stays above the keyboard; the head keeps clear of the safe area at the top.
- **Leaving stops the bytes.** `NshTerminal` calls `unwatchTerminal` when it unmounts, as Svelte's `detach()` did; otherwise a running `pnpm dev` kept sending its output over the relay to a phone that had left the page. The daemon keeps one watcher per client, not a count, so a client should show a session in one view at a time: one tab per session on the desktop, one page on the phone.
- **An old race, now hit every time.** `remove()` publishes `terminal.removed`; when the hung-up shell exits, the exit handler `emit`s one more upsert (exit code 1), and clients put the session just closed back in their list as finished: under Reattach in the empty pane and in the phone's list, and ending it again is a 404. The old 结束会话 did this too, but the refresh usually got there first; with closing ending the session, and two clients connected, it showed every time.
  - Daemon: the exit handler first checks that the entry is still its own (`this.entries.get(id) !== entry` → nothing). A test covers it and goes red without the fix.
  - Client: ids removed are remembered and later upserts for them are dropped (`TerminalList.upsertTerminal` / `dropTerminal`), because the user's running daemon gets the fix only when it restarts.
- **Checks.** On real shells: `.ui/terminal-pane.test.tsx` (4: closing a tab ends it, one of two, after an exit, paste) and the new `.ui/terminal-page.test.tsx` (4: reattaches to the desktop's shell and starts none beside it, starts one when none runs and reattaches to it, + and switching, the key bar and Ctrl). `terminal-tabs.spec.ts`, `terminals.spec.ts`, `terminal-keys.spec.ts`, `runtime-terminal.test.ts` and the daemon's `terminals.test.ts` pass. A `.ui` test covers Ctrl+W deleting a word and Ctrl+Shift+W closing the tab on Windows; it goes red without the fix. The 10 terminal `.ui` tests also ran with the new client on the old daemon, which is what the user's 17890 runs until it restarts: all passed, so the client's guard holds. `phone-web-e2e` has a new step: the Mac opens a shell and runs a line, the phone opens the terminal page and reads that line over the relay, the daemon still has one session, the bar's Return sends a line typed on the phone, a reload on the page's address finds the same shell and starts none, and after Back that shell is still running.
- **Not done.** Keys do not repeat when held. Paste reads the clipboard, which shows the system's paste prompt on iOS. The page does not make room for the soft keyboard itself (as elsewhere on the page, the browser does). When several clients show one session, the pty takes the size of the last one to resize: the phone reattaching to the desktop's shell narrows it until the desktop resizes, as before. The two-line zsh prompt that zsh redraws when the pty narrows is drawn wrong on the phone: those rows sit about half a row above the grid, overlapping their neighbours, and the `╰─` is missing; the rows after it are on the grid again. Whether that is NSH web's row height or the redraw after narrowing is not known yet. WebKit was not run for this e2e. The pinned NSH (48559f8) already carries nsh#2095 (text sent only once composition ends), so an input method on the phone's page does not send its pinyin to the shell first.

## 补记 2026-11-18（五十六）：桌面「工具」里的终端也能接回 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (56): the desktop's Tools reattach to a terminal too

用户说：桌面端工具里的终端始终是新开的终端，也要展示可接回的终端，同时可接回的终端要把已经打开的排除在外。

- **「工具」菜单在「新建终端」下面列出没有标签的会话**，每条一行「接回 · 名字」（名字和标签、「＋」里一样，结束了的带上怎么结束的）。列表就是「＋」和空窗格用的那一份 `ShellWorkbench.untabbedTerminals`：守护进程的会话去掉平铺和浮动标签指着的，所以已经开在标签里的从不出现。点一行走 `ShellWorkbench.reattachTerminal`，和 `openNewTerminal(null)` 同一条放置路径，只是不起新 shell。接回后它有了标签，下次打开菜单就不在了。
- **只在桌面的工作台上。** 手机、窄窗和远程连过来的设备（`pages`）打开的是终端页，它自己接回，所以那里不列。
- **列表只在菜单开着时读。** `untabbedTerminals` 每次布局提交都会变，Shell 把它作为属性往下传，`ToolsMenu` 先看 `open` 再读，合着的菜单不跟着布局重算。方向键走得到这些行（它们的键排在「新建终端」后面）。
- **检查。** `sidebar-rail` 故事：工作台上列出、点一行回调并从列表里消失、没有工作台时不列；手机底部面板也不列。`.ui/terminal-pane.test.tsx` 新的一条在真 shell 上走：手机终端页起一个 shell 跑一行，桌面自己开一个终端标签，桌面「工具」里只列手机那一条，点它开出第二个标签、读回手机那一行，再开菜单两条都不列，关掉两个标签两条都结束。没测：方向键（无头测试里按键不会触发可按的视图）。

The user said the terminal in the desktop's Tools was always a new one: it should also show the terminals that can be reattached, leaving out those already open.

- **Tools lists the sessions no tab shows under New terminal**, one "Reattach · name" row each (named as their tabs and the + menu name them, a finished one with how it ended). The list is the one the + menu and the empty pane use, `ShellWorkbench.untabbedTerminals`: the daemon's sessions less the ones a tiled or floating tab names, so one already in a tab never shows. A row goes through `ShellWorkbench.reattachTerminal`, the same placement as `openNewTerminal(null)` without starting a shell. Reattached, it has a tab, so the menu no longer lists it.
- **The desktop's workbench only.** A phone, a narrow window and a remote device (`pages`) open the terminal page, which reattaches by itself, so they list none.
- **Read only while the menu is open.** `untabbedTerminals` changes with every layout commit; Shell passes it down as a prop and `ToolsMenu` reads it only after checking `open`, so a closed menu does not follow the layout. The arrow keys reach the rows (their keys come after New terminal).
- **Checks.** The `sidebar-rail` story: listed on the workbench, a press calls back and the row leaves the list, none without a workbench; none on the phone's sheet either. A new `.ui/terminal-pane.test.tsx` test on real shells: the phone's terminal page starts a shell and runs a line, the desktop opens a terminal tab of its own, the desktop's Tools list only the phone's, pressing it opens a second tab with the phone's line read back, the menu then lists neither, and closing both tabs ends both. Not tested: the arrow keys (headless tests do not activate a pressable from the keyboard).

## 补记 2026-11-18（五十七）：手机上工作区的文件列表用手指滑得动，面板里只有一个滚动视图 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (57): the workspace's file list scrolls under a finger on the phone, and is the sheet's one scroll view

用户发来手机（安卓 Chrome PWA）的截图：文件预览顶上「工作区」下拉弹出的底部面板里，文件列表滑不动，右边并排两条滚动条。两件事，各有原因。

- **行把手指当成了拖拽。** 文件树每一行带着 `pressWorkspacePaths` 的 PanResponder（把行拖到会话的输入框）。手指一动过 6 点，`onMoveShouldSetPanResponder` 就答应，行成了响应者；NSH web 在响应者要拦原生滚动时（`setJsResponder`）对之后的 `touchmove` 调 `preventDefault`，浏览器就不滚了。Svelte 的 `pressWorkspacePaths` 开头是 `event.pointerType === "touch"` 就返回（「手指滚树，树反正盖着会话」），移植时丢了。现在手指不拖（`e.pointerType !== "touch"`），鼠标和笔照旧。可在手机页面上光这一条不够：NSH 的浏览器运行时给触摸事件不带 `pointerType`（`nsh-platform-web/src/browser/events.rs` 的 Touch 负载只写 id、目标、路径、位置、`changed`、时间戳，`browser/gesture.js` 的 `point()` 也只拷这些），JS 读到的是 `undefined`；QuickJS 那条路（macOS 等原生宿主、无头测试，`nsh-js/src/host.rs`）是带着的。所以手机页（`HOSTED_MESSENGER`，手机和平板都是）上行一律不接拖拽：那里任何宽度都没有工作台，输入框从不和文件树同屏，拖了也无处可放。`hosted` 是 `pressWorkspacePaths` 的第二个参数，默认 `HOSTED_MESSENGER`，和 `isWorkbenchSurface(narrow, hosted)` 一样，好测。平板上工作区页里那一栏文件树也是同一个原因，同一个条件也管到它（没有单独量）。
- **两层滚动。** `BottomSheet` 把内容放进自己的 `Scroll`，而 `Tree` 自己就是滚动视图（虚拟列表，只做看得见的行），所以给了它窗口 60% 的高度；加上外层 `Scroll` 的上下内边距，比面板给得出的多 12 点，外层 534/522 也能滚，于是两条滚动条，外面那条还能拖动几个点。`BottomSheet` 新增 `scrollsItself`：内容自己会滚的，面板不再包 `Scroll`，换成同样内边距、可收缩的 `View`。只有工作区／文件夹的列表用它。`RosterFilter` 的面板里也套着一个 `Scroll`，但那块面板要随软键盘上移，被挤小时还靠外层滚，没动。
- **检查。**
  - `phone-web-e2e.ts` 新一段：工作区里放一个 60 个文件的文件夹 `many`，在工作区里打开文件后再点上面的下拉，在面板里展开它。面板里只能有一个滚动视图（文件树的），且不套在别的滚动视图里；Chromium 里用 CDP `Input.dispatchTouchEvent` 每 16 ms 移 3 点地从一行上往上滑 300 点，列表要动至少 100 点，且全程没出现拖拽的影子（`workspace-drag-ghost`，用 MutationObserver 记）。先用的 DevTools `Input.synthesizeScrollGesture` 第一步跨得大，浏览器先接管了滚动：在没修的包上列表照样滚，只是同时起了拖拽，测不出用户看到的现象，所以改成逐帧的手指。没修的包上这一段报：外层 `534/522` 加上套在里面的树，滑动 0 点、行当成了拖拽。只加了 `pointerType` 判断的包上原样失败（web 上是 `undefined`），由此查到上面那处 NSH 的缺口。两处都改后这一段通过，Chromium 整轮通过（终端页那段因为这次没给 pty helper 跳过）；WebKit 每一段都走过了，到平板那段为止，包括这一段的「只有一个滚动视图」，最后只红在早就有的 `blob:` 内容安全策略那一处（补记五十一、五十三也是）。
  - `src/ui/workspace-drag.test.ts`（nsh 单元测试，4 条），用自己构造的事件直接驱动行的响应者：鼠标过了阈值才拖，拖到目标上松开交出路径；笔和鼠标一样；手指往哪个方向都不拖；手机页上谁都不拖，包括不带 `pointerType` 的事件。去掉两项判断，后两条变红。
  - `.ui/artifacts-files.test.tsx`：无头驱动的 `dragAndHold` 是手指（`PointerType::Touch`，桌面环境下也是，而 macOS 宿主的鼠标拖动报 `Mouse`），原来那条「拖行出影子、松开交出路径」现在拖不起来，改成「手指拖行什么都不带」；鼠标那条链路挪到上面的单元测试里。
- **部署和上游。** 用户点头后手机页重新部署（构建 `8c8f90ba81152313`，来自 `0052928d`）；部署前在这个提交上带 pty helper 跑了 Chromium 的手机 e2e，整轮通过，终端页两段也在内。真手机没验证。WebKit 这里驱动不了触摸滑动，只查了面板里只有一个滚动视图。NSH 上游：web 的触摸事件不带 `pointerType`/`pressure`/`tilt` 这一处，上游 main 已经修了（longbridge/nsh#2399，在我们钉住的版本之后），钉住的版本带上它以后，`hosted` 这一条可以去掉，只留 `pointerType`；无头 `drag` 在桌面和 web 上也报 `touch`、也拖动滚动视图这一处，提了 longbridge/nsh#2454 和 PR #2455（只改 `drag`/`dragAndHold`，按下和长按仍是手指）。它进了钉住的版本以后，桌面环境的 `.ui` 拖动就是鼠标，「把行拖到输入框」那条可以回到界面测试里。

The user sent a screenshot from their phone (Android Chrome, installed as an app): in the bottom sheet the 工作区 (workspace) picker above a file preview opens, the file list would not scroll, and two scroll bars stood side by side. Two things, each with its own cause.

- **The rows took a finger for a drag.** Every row of the file tree carries `pressWorkspacePaths`' PanResponder (drag the row onto a conversation's message box). Once a finger moved 6 pt, `onMoveShouldSetPanResponder` said yes and the row became the responder; NSH web then calls `preventDefault` on the following `touchmove`s for a responder that blocks native scrolling (`setJsResponder`), and the browser does not scroll. Svelte's `pressWorkspacePaths` returned at once on `event.pointerType === "touch"` ("the finger scrolls the tree, and the tree covers the conversation anyway"); the port lost that. A finger no longer drags (`e.pointerType !== "touch"`); the mouse and a pen still do. On the phone's page that alone is not enough: NSH's browser runtime gives touch events no `pointerType` (the Touch payload in `nsh-platform-web/src/browser/events.rs` writes the id, target, path, positions, `changed` and time only, and `point()` in `browser/gesture.js` copies only those), so JS reads `undefined`; the QuickJS side (macOS and the other native hosts, headless tests, `nsh-js/src/host.rs`) carries it. So on the phone's page (`HOSTED_MESSENGER`, phones and tablets alike) no row takes a drag: there is no workbench there at any width, a message box is never on screen beside a file tree, and a drag would have nowhere to go. `hosted` is `pressWorkspacePaths`' second parameter, defaulting to `HOSTED_MESSENGER`, as in `isWorkbenchSurface(narrow, hosted)`, so it can be tested. The tree column of the workspace page on a tablet has the same cause, and the same condition covers it (not measured on its own).
- **Two scroll views, one in the other.** `BottomSheet` puts what it holds in a `Scroll` of its own, while `Tree` is a scroll view itself (a virtual list that makes only the rows in view) and so is given 60% of the window's height. With the outer `Scroll`'s top and bottom padding that is 12 pt more than the sheet has room for, so the outer one scrolled as well (534/522): two bars, and the outer one moved a few points. `BottomSheet` gains `scrollsItself`: for content that scrolls itself, the sheet puts no `Scroll` around it, only a `View` with the same padding that can shrink. Only the workspace's and a folder's list use it. `RosterFilter`'s sheet also holds a `Scroll`, but that sheet rises above the soft keyboard and relies on the outer one when squeezed, so it is left as it is.
- **Checks.**
  - `phone-web-e2e.ts` gains a part: the workspace gets a folder `many` of 60 files; with a file open in the workspace, the picker above it is pressed again and the folder opened in the sheet. The sheet must hold one scroll view, the tree's, not inside another; in Chromium a finger moves up 300 pt from a row, 3 pt every 16 ms through CDP `Input.dispatchTouchEvent`, and the list must move at least 100 pt with no drag ghost (`workspace-drag-ghost`, noted by a MutationObserver) at any point. DevTools' `Input.synthesizeScrollGesture`, tried first, takes a long first step and the browser takes the scroll before the row can: on the unfixed package the list scrolled anyway and only a drag started too, which is not what the user saw, hence the finger by frames. On the unfixed package the part reports the outer `534/522` with the tree inside it, and a swipe that moved the list 0 pt, taken for a drag. A package with only the `pointerType` check failed the same way (it is `undefined` on the web), which is how the NSH gap above was found. With both changes the part passes, and the whole run passes in Chromium (the terminal page part skipped, no pty helper given this time); in WebKit every part passed through the tablet one, this part's one-scroll-view check included, and the run went red only on the old `blob:` content security policy flake (as in addenda 51 and 53).
  - `src/ui/workspace-drag.test.ts` (nsh unit tests, 4) drives a row's responder directly with events of its own: the mouse drags only past the threshold, and letting go over a target hands the paths over; a pen drags as the mouse does; a finger drags in no direction; on the phone's page nothing drags, events without `pointerType` included. Without the two checks the last two go red.
  - `.ui/artifacts-files.test.tsx`: the headless driver's `dragAndHold` is a finger (`PointerType::Touch`, in a desktop environment too, while the macOS host reports a mouse drag as `Mouse`), so "dragging a row shows a ghost and letting go hands the path over" can no longer start a drag. It becomes "a finger dragging a row carries nothing"; the mouse's chain moves to the unit tests above.
- **Deployed, and upstream.** On the user's OK the phone's page went out again (build `8c8f90ba81152313`, from `0052928d`); before it, the phone e2e ran in Chromium on that commit with the pty helper and passed whole, the terminal page's parts included. The real phone is not checked. WebKit cannot be driven by touch here, so there only the one-scroll-view check ran. Upstream NSH: web touch events without `pointerType`/`pressure`/`tilt` are already fixed on its main (longbridge/nsh#2399, after our pin); once the pin carries it, the `hosted` condition can go and `pointerType` alone remain. The headless `drag` that is a finger on the desktops and the web, scroll views included, is longbridge/nsh#2454 with PR #2455 (`drag`/`dragAndHold` only; presses and long presses stay a finger). Once the pin carries that, desktop `.ui` drags are the mouse and "drag a row onto a message box" can go back into the screen tests.

## 补记 2026-11-18（五十八）：「工具」里的终端是两级 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (58): Terminal in Tools is a level of its own

用户说：工具里的那个终端可以做成多级的。（五十六）把可接回的终端平铺在「新建终端」下面，最多 8 条会把菜单撑高，而菜单不能滚动；这次改成第二级，取代（五十六）的平铺。

- **桌面工作台上，「工具」里是「终端 ›」。** 它不再直接新起 shell，而是在旁边展开一级（`ToolsTerminalMenu`）：先是「新建终端」，分隔线下「可接回」列出没有标签的会话（还是 `ShellWorkbench.untabbedTerminals`，标签里的不列），按名字一行，指着显示目录；一条都没有时写「没有可接回的终端」。只有这一级的行会做事，和应用里别的级联一样（项目会话的账号 / 模型 / 思考，[ADR 0095](0095-project-sessions.md) 决定 12）。
- **怎么打开、怎么收起。** 指着「终端」停 90 ms 展开，指着别的行停同样久收起，菜单还在；点「终端」也展开。斜着从「终端」移进第二级时经过别的行，只要没停够就不收；进了第二级就取消待收。键盘：在「终端」上按 → 进去，焦点落在第一行，↑↓ 在这一级里走（两级各有一个 `rovingMenu`，只有拿着键盘的那一级绑方向键）；← 或 Esc 退回「终端」那一行，再按 Esc 关掉整个菜单；Tab 关掉整个菜单。
- **放在哪。** 第二级是「工具」菜单 Overlay 里面的一个 Overlay，锚在「终端」那一行的右边，第一行和它齐平（减去自己的内边距）；右边放不下时 NSH 放到左边。因为在外层 Overlay 里面，点它不算点在「工具」菜单外面。
- **手机、窄窗、远程设备不变。** 它们的「终端」打开终端页，终端页自己接回。
- **检查。** `sidebar-rail` 故事：指着展开、指着别的行收起、Esc 先收一级、点一条回调后它从列表里消失、没有时的提示、点「新建终端」才新起；没有工作台时没有第二级；手机底部面板也没有。真 shell 上的 `.ui/terminal-pane.test.tsx`、`terminal-page.test.tsx` 改成经过第二级，「接回」那条照旧：手机起的那条在第二级里、桌面自己的不在，接回后两条都不列。没测：方向键（无头测试里按键不会触发可按的视图）。

The user said the terminal in Tools could be multi-level. Addendum 56 laid the terminals to reattach out under New terminal, up to 8 of them, in a menu that does not scroll; now they are a second level, which replaces 56's flat rows.

- **On the desktop's workbench, Tools has Terminal ›.** It no longer starts a shell itself; it opens a level beside it (`ToolsTerminalMenu`): New terminal, then under a line and Reattach the sessions no tab shows (still `ShellWorkbench.untabbedTerminals`, so none a tab shows), one row per name with its folder on pointing; with none, a line saying so. Only that level's rows act, as in the app's other cascade (a project session's account / model / thinking, [ADR 0095](0095-project-sessions.md) decision 12).
- **Opening and closing.** Resting on Terminal for 90 ms opens it; resting as long on another row closes it, the menu staying. A press on Terminal opens it too. Crossing another row on the way from Terminal into the level closes nothing unless the pointer rests there; entering the level cancels a pending close. Keyboard: → on Terminal goes in, the focus on its first row, and ↑↓ walk that level (each level has its own `rovingMenu`, and only the one holding the keyboard binds the arrows); ← or Esc goes back to the Terminal row, and Esc again closes the menu; Tab closes the menu.
- **Placement.** The level is an Overlay inside the Tools menu's Overlay, anchored on the Terminal row's right with its first row level with it (less its own padding); NSH puts it on the left when the right has no room. Being inside the outer Overlay, a press on it is not a press outside the Tools menu.
- **Phone, narrow window and remote devices: unchanged.** Their Terminal opens the terminal page, which reattaches by itself.
- **Checks.** The `sidebar-rail` story: opens when pointed at, closes on pointing at another row, Esc closes a level first, a row calls back and leaves the list, the line for none, and only New terminal starts one; no level without a workbench, nor on the phone's sheet. On real shells, `.ui/terminal-pane.test.tsx` and `terminal-page.test.tsx` now go through the level, and the reattach case holds: the phone's shell is in it, the desktop's own is not, and after reattaching neither is listed. Not tested: the arrow keys (headless tests do not activate a pressable from the keyboard).

## 补记 2026-11-18（五十九）：手机上项目目录的「＋」是底部面板 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (59): a project directory's + is a bottom sheet on the phone

用户说：远程手机上点项目的「＋」应该用底部面板弹出菜单，而不是下拉。手机「项目」页里，分区标题的「＋」本来就没有（新建走右下角的浮动按钮，那是底部面板），剩下的就是每个项目目录那一行的「＋」：它开的 `ProjectAddMenu` 不分宽窄，一律是挂在「＋」下面的 `Overlay` 下拉。

- **窄窗口是底部面板。** `ProjectAddMenu` 按 `narrowWindow()` 分两支，和会话行的 `SessionContextMenu` 一样：窄的是 `BottomSheet`，标题是目录名（新的 `title` 属性，侧栏传目录的 `label`；不给时用 `label`），行是 `SheetItem`，代理那几行把 logo 放在 `leading`，组之间 `SheetSeparator`，`testID` 和桌面的一样；宽的还是原来的下拉。选一项时先做事再收面板，和长按菜单的面板同一顺序（下拉是先收再做，没动）。
- **方向键只在下拉里。** 下拉用 `keymap.bind(…, { when: "always" })` 绑了方向键、Enter、Tab、Esc；面板里不高亮哪一行，这些键还绑着的话，方向键和 Enter 走的是看不见的高亮，Tab 会把面板关掉；Esc 和返回本来就归面板自己的 `Dialog`。所以只在下拉那一支绑。
- **`point` 在面板里不起作用。** 右键目录行带来的 `point` 只给下拉定位；面板总在底部。
- **没改的。** 侧栏、列表行、`BotAddMenu` 都没动；桌面窗口的分区「＋」和目录「＋」还是下拉，右键还是挂在指针处。目录行在手机上没有长按（只有 `onContextMenu`），这次没加。
- **检查。** `.ui/sidebar-main-phone.test.tsx` 新一条（390 宽，真侧栏）：建一个项目会话，到「项目」页按目录的「＋」，菜单在 `bottom-sheet-panel` 里，标题是目录名，有 ✕；三种代理、打开目录、打开终端都在，没有导入，「新开」不带省略号；点打开目录回调了这个目录、面板收起；再开一次点 ✕ 也收起。桌面的 `sidebar-main`、`sidebar-rows-more` 里下拉的老测试一条没改，全过。`phone-web-e2e.ts` 新一段（Chromium，浏览器自己的返回）：按目录的「＋」，面板贴着屏幕底边；等它的历史条目写好后按返回，面板收起、列表还在；再开、点打开目录，目录盖在会话上、面板已经收起；按返回收起目录，页面还在。
- **顺带查出的老毛病（没修）。** 从面板里打开目录后，第一次返回把目录和会话一起收掉了，而行菜单那一段是先收目录、再收会话。量了历史：行菜单是 `/`、`?s=`、`?s=…&a=file` 三条，「＋」面板只有 `/`、`?s=…&a=file` 两条。原因在 `handleMenuOpenProjectDir`：它 `await selectSession(id)` 再开目录；这个会话在这次连接里刚读过（`current`），Mac 支持 `bounded_read_v1` 时这一支什么都不等，`selectSession` 立刻返回，会话和目录在同一轮里交给写地址的 effect，只写成一条。行菜单那段过，是因为那个项目之前没打开过，取历史要走中继，把两次写隔开了。证据：在同一次 e2e 里把行菜单那段原样再走一遍，也是两条，一次返回全收。所以跟面板无关，旧的下拉也一样；复现：手机上打开一个项目会话、返回到列表、再从行菜单或目录「＋」打开它的目录，按返回。e2e 这一段因此只断言到「返回收起目录、页面还在」，注释写了原因。要修得改 `page-route` 的写法或 `handleMenuOpenProjectDir` 的顺序，单独做。
- **部署。** 用户点头后手机页重新部署（构建 `9fb56ed7a4793f85`，来自 `c3454062`，也带上了这之前 main 上的文件树拖动移动和「工具」里的终端两级）。部署前在主检出里、同一份代码上带 pty helper 跑了 Chromium 的手机 e2e，整轮通过；没在部署工作树里重跑，WebKit 没跑。线上的 `Main.c86fcaba21.js` 和构建逐字节一致；`/`、`?o=settings` 200，`?x=1` 400。真手机没验证。

The user said that on the remote phone, the project's + should open its menu as a bottom sheet, not a dropdown. On the phone's Projects page the section heading has no + (creating is the floating button, already a sheet), so what is left is the + on each project directory's row: the `ProjectAddMenu` it opens was an `Overlay` dropdown hanging off the + at every width.

- **A narrow window gets a bottom sheet.** `ProjectAddMenu` branches on `narrowWindow()`, as a row's `SessionContextMenu` does: narrow is a `BottomSheet` headed with the directory's name (a new `title` prop, which the sidebar gives the directory's `label`; `label` when left out), rows are `SheetItem`s with an agent's logo as `leading`, `SheetSeparator` between groups, the same `testID`s as the desktop's. Wide is the dropdown as it was. A choice runs and then the sheet goes, in the long-press sheet's order (the dropdown closes first and then runs, unchanged).
- **The arrow keys only in the dropdown.** The dropdown binds the arrows, Enter, Tab and Escape with `keymap.bind(…, { when: "always" })`. The sheet highlights no row: bound there, the arrows and Enter would walk a highlight nobody sees and Tab would close the sheet, while Escape and Back already belong to the sheet's own `Dialog`. So only the dropdown binds them.
- **`point` means nothing in the sheet.** A right-click's `point` places only the dropdown; the sheet stands at the bottom.
- **Unchanged.** The sidebar, the list rows and `BotAddMenu`; a desktop window's section + and directory + are still dropdowns, and a right-click still hangs at the pointer. A directory row has no long press on the phone (only `onContextMenu`), and none was added.
- **Checks.** `.ui/sidebar-main-phone.test.tsx` gains a case (390 wide, the real sidebar): make a project session, press its directory's + on the Projects page; the menu is inside `bottom-sheet-panel`, headed with the directory's name, with ✕; the three agents, open directory and open terminal are there, import is not, and "new" has no ellipsis; open directory calls back with the directory and the sheet goes; opened again, ✕ puts it away too. The desktop's dropdown tests in `sidebar-main` and `sidebar-rows-more` are unchanged and pass. `phone-web-e2e.ts` gains a part (Chromium, the browser's own Back): the directory's + gives a sheet whose panel sits on the screen's bottom edge; once its history entry is written, Back puts it away and the list stays; opened again, open directory covers the conversation and the sheet is gone; Back puts the folder away and the page stays.
- **An older fault found on the way (not fixed).** After opening the folder from the sheet, the first Back put away both the folder and the conversation, where the row menu's part put away the folder and then the conversation. Measured history: the row menu leaves `/`, `?s=`, `?s=…&a=file`; the +'s sheet only `/`, `?s=…&a=file`. The cause is `handleMenuOpenProjectDir`: it does `await selectSession(id)` and then opens the folder; with the conversation read in this connection already (`current`), that branch awaits nothing when the Mac has `bounded_read_v1`, so `selectSession` returns at once, so the conversation and the folder reach the effect that writes the address in one turn and become one entry. The row menu's part passes because that project was never open before, and fetching its history over the relay kept the two writes apart. Evidence: the row menu's part walked a second time, unchanged, also leaves two entries, and one Back puts both away. So it is not the sheet's, and the old dropdown had it too. To reproduce: on the phone open a project session, Back to the list, open its folder from the row's menu or the directory's +, and press Back. The e2e part therefore asserts no more than "Back puts the folder away and the page stays", with a comment saying why. Fixing it means changing how `page-route` writes or the order in `handleMenuOpenProjectDir`, as a change of its own.
- **Deployed.** On the user's OK the phone's page went out again (build `9fb56ed7a4793f85`, from `c3454062`, which also carries main's earlier file-tree drag-to-move and the Tools terminal level). Before it, the phone e2e ran whole in Chromium with the pty helper on the same code in the main checkout; it was not rerun in the deploy worktree, and WebKit was not run. The live `Main.c86fcaba21.js` is byte-identical to the build; `/` and `?o=settings` answer 200, `?x=1` 400. The real phone is not checked.

## 补记 2026-11-18（六十）：手机页首屏——中继发 brotli，service worker 存引擎 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (60): the phone page's first load — the relay sends brotli, the service worker keeps the engine

用户问首屏要加载的文件跟 Svelte 版比是不是差太多，然后说按顺序都做一下。量法：无头 Chromium，390×844 触屏，本地静态服务器，两边都按 gzip -9，到配对屏为止。Svelte（1794421836324，线上原来那版）80 个文件、原始 2.68 MB、gzip 0.81 MB；NSH（8c8f90ba）39 个文件、原始 19.6 MB、gzip 7.07 MB，到首屏以后不再加载别的。NSH 的构成：主 wasm 4.66 MB，`Main.js` 0.99 MB，11 个副模块约 1.4 MB（都标了按需，但整个应用是一个 `Main.js` 块，`chunkSides["Main.js"]` 列了除公式以外的全部，所以开头就都下了）。线上 11-18 约 13 次部署里 `Main.*.js` 每次都换（每次约 1 MB gzip，差不多是 Svelte 整个首屏），主 wasm 和副模块只换过一次。提的四步依次是：中继发 brotli、service worker 也存 NSH 的文件、拆 `Main.js` 让副模块真按需、把应用自己的 Rust 拆成按需模块。这条记前两步。

- **中继发 brotli（服务器，已生效）。** 包里本来就有每个文件的 `.br`，盒子上的 nginx（Ubuntu 24.04 的 1.24）只开了 `gzip_static`。装了 `libnginx-mod-http-brotli-static`（只多这一个包；postinst 建 `modules-enabled` 软链、触发一次 reload，不是 restart，中继的 WebSocket 不断），`relay.conf` 在按内容命名的文件和页面两处 `gzip_static` 旁边加 `brotli_static on`，照 `deploy-phone.sh` 的办法换上（旧的留作 `.prev`，`nginx -t` 过了才 reload）。线上量：主 wasm 带 `Accept-Encoding: br` 拿到 `br`、3.60 MB（gzip 4.77 MB），`Main.js` 0.82 MB（gzip 1.02 MB），类型 `application/wasm`、`immutable` 不变；只说 gzip 的照旧拿 gzip，不带的拿原文件；没有 `.br` 的（`nsh-prefetch.*.js`）回落到 gzip；`?x=1` 400、`?o=settings` 200、缺的按内容命名文件 404，策略不变。经 ssh 隧道用无头 Chromium 打开线上页，配对屏出来，31 个响应是 br，控制台无错。首屏按 gzip 算的 7.07 MB 按 br 算约 5.5 MB。`.scratch` 里的 `nginx-local-check.sh` 改成用装了这个模块的 Ubuntu 24.04 nginx 镜像（官方 `nginx:1.24` 没有 brotli 模块，`brotli_static` 会让 `nginx -t` 失败），并加了 br 的两项检查；Docker 当时没开，这个脚本这次没跑过。`deploy-phone.sh` 末尾多报一行引擎发给 brotli 浏览器的编码。
- **service worker 存 NSH 的文件。** `assets/phone/sw.js` 的 `isImmutable` 还是 Svelte 的 `/_app/immutable/`，NSH 的文件都在根下，一个也没进它的缓存，只靠 HTTP 缓存。改成和 vhost 同一条规则（路径里有 `.<10 位十六进制>.` 或 `.<10 位>/`），缓存名升到 `real-bot-immutable-v2`（激活时删掉 v1 里 Svelte 的旧文件）。存的副本去掉 `content-encoding` 和 `content-length`（`fetch` 交来的是解压后的内容），另记 `x-rb-size` 为实际字节数，修剪按它算（以前按 `Content-Length`，br 发来的引擎只记了三分之一，nginx 现压的没有长度就按 50 KB 估）。
- **量出来的。** 三份同样的构建只换 `sw.js`（旧的、新的但不存 `.wasm`、新的全存），各在独立配置里冷启动一次再重复打开六次：重复打开到配对屏都在 74–112 ms，引擎从 worker 来和从 HTTP 缓存来没有可见差别，所以 `.wasm` 也交给 worker。但只有页面自己发的请求经过 worker：引擎、副模块、`nsh.js`；应用跑在页面里的 `about:blank` 框架，那里用脚本加载的 `app.js`、`Main.js`、facades 在 Chromium 里不经过 worker，还是只靠 HTTP 缓存。全存那份的缓存是 25 个文件、16.4 MB。
- **检查。** `isolation.spec.ts` 从查源码里有没有 `/_app/immutable/` 改成在 vm 里跑 `sw.js`：按内容命名的引擎、副模块、`Main.js`、`browser.<摘要>/dom.js`、facades、giallo 数据、页面样式都存；页面、带查询的深链、`sw.js`、`media-stream.js`、清单、`_app/version.json`、中继接口、远程媒体、9 位或大写的摘要、别的源都不存；存下的副本状态和类型不变，没有 `content-encoding`/`content-length`，大小是解压后的。desktop-nsh 的 `bun test scripts`、spec 一道（1492 个）、主题检查、`tsc` 都过。手机 e2e 这次没跑；部署要用户点头，没部署。

The user asked whether the first screen loads much more than the Svelte version did, then asked for all the steps in order. Method: headless Chromium, 390×844 with touch, a local static server, gzip -9 for both, up to the pairing screen. Svelte (1794421836324, the page that was live) loads 80 files, 2.68 MB raw, 0.81 MB gzip; NSH (8c8f90ba) 39 files, 19.6 MB raw, 7.07 MB gzip, and nothing more after the first screen. NSH's share: the main wasm 4.66 MB, `Main.js` 0.99 MB, 11 side modules about 1.4 MB (all marked on demand, but the whole app is one `Main.js` chunk and `chunkSides["Main.js"]` lists every one but the formulas', so all load at start). Over about 13 deploys on 11-18, `Main.*.js` changed every time (about 1 MB gzip each, nearly the whole Svelte first load), the main wasm and the side modules once. The four steps, in order: brotli from the relay, the service worker keeping NSH's files, splitting `Main.js` so the side modules really load on demand, and splitting the app's own Rust into modules loaded on demand. This records the first two.

- **Brotli from the relay (server, live).** The package already carries a `.br` beside each file; the box's nginx (Ubuntu 24.04's 1.24) had only `gzip_static`. Installed `libnginx-mod-http-brotli-static` (the one package; its postinst links `modules-enabled` and triggers one reload, not a restart, so the relay's WebSockets stay up). `relay.conf` gains `brotli_static on` beside `gzip_static` for the files named by their content and for the page, put in the way `deploy-phone.sh` does (the old one kept as `.prev`, reloaded only once `nginx -t` passes). Measured live: the main wasm with `Accept-Encoding: br` comes as `br`, 3.60 MB (gzip 4.77 MB), `Main.js` 0.82 MB (gzip 1.02 MB), still `application/wasm` and `immutable`; a gzip-only client still gets gzip, one asking for neither the file itself; a file with no `.br` (`nsh-prefetch.*.js`) falls back to gzip; `?x=1` 400, `?o=settings` 200, a missing content-named file 404, the policy unchanged. Headless Chromium through an ssh tunnel opened the live page to the pairing screen, 31 responses as br, a clean console. The first load's 7.07 MB as gzip is about 5.5 MB as br. In `.scratch`, `nginx-local-check.sh` now runs an Ubuntu 24.04 nginx image with that module (the official `nginx:1.24` has no brotli module, and `brotli_static` would fail its `nginx -t`) and checks br twice more; Docker was not running, so the script was not run this time. `deploy-phone.sh` reports at its end what encoding the engine goes out in to a brotli browser.
- **The service worker keeps NSH's files.** `assets/phone/sw.js`'s `isImmutable` was still Svelte's `/_app/immutable/`; NSH's files sit at the root, so none ever entered its cache and only the HTTP cache held them. It now takes the vhost's rule (`.<10 hex>.` or `.<10 hex>/` in the path), and the cache is `real-bot-immutable-v2` (activating deletes v1 with the Svelte page's old files). A kept copy drops `content-encoding` and `content-length` (`fetch` hands over the body decoded) and records its real size as `x-rb-size`, which the pruning now counts (it went by `Content-Length`: a third of the engine when it came as br, and a 50 KB guess when nginx compressed as it went).
- **Measured.** Three copies of one build with only `sw.js` changed (the old one, the new one without `.wasm`, the new one with everything), each in a profile of its own, opened cold and then six more times: every repeat reached the pairing screen in 74–112 ms, with no visible difference between the engine coming from the worker or from the HTTP cache, so the worker keeps `.wasm` too. Only what the page itself requests reaches the worker, though: the engine, the side modules, `nsh.js`. The app runs in the page's `about:blank` frame, and the scripts loaded there (`app.js`, `Main.js`, the facades) do not pass through the worker in Chromium and stay on the HTTP cache alone. The copy that kept everything held 25 files, 16.4 MB.
- **Checks.** `isolation.spec.ts` no longer looks for `/_app/immutable/` in the source but runs `sw.js` in a vm: the content-named engine, side modules, `Main.js`, `browser.<digest>/dom.js`, facades, giallo's data and the page's style are kept; the page, a deep link with a query, `sw.js`, `media-stream.js`, the manifest, `_app/version.json`, the relay's routes, remote media, a 9-digit or upper-case digest and another origin are not; a kept copy has its status and type, no `content-encoding`/`content-length`, and the decoded size. desktop-nsh's `bun test scripts`, the spec lane (1492 tests), the theme check and `tsc` pass. The phone e2e was not run this time; deploying waits for the user's OK, and nothing was deployed.

## 补记 2026-11-18（六十一）：手机上用量、分工、已归档会话页下面没有底栏 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (61): no bottom bar under the phone's usage, roles and archived pages

用户说：手机端的用量、分工、已归档会话页不应该有底部的 Bot / 项目 / 设置栏。这三个页都是从「工具」打开的 `runtime.listPage`（`usage` / `roles` / `archived`），在侧栏里替掉名单，各有自己的「‹」，地址是 `?o=<kind>`。底栏的三个地方哪个都不是它们，按底栏上当前那一格（Bot 或项目）也什么都不做（`navigateMobile` 遇到同一个地方就返回）。

- **原因。** `shell-state.ts` 的 `mobileNavigationVisible` 排除了日程、花费、终端、远程屏幕、新建、会话设置、Bot 资料、危险确认，非设置时再排除工作区、选中的会话和文件预览，没看 `listPage`。Svelte 版 `Shell.svelte` 的同名条件也一样，是照搬过来的。
- **改法。** 设置关着的那一支加上 `!runtime.listPage`，和工作区、会话并列。不放在最外层：`topLayer` 的测试里有 `listPageOpen` 和 `settingsOpen` 同时为真的情形，`openSettings` 现在会清掉 `listPage`，但条件放在最外层的话，万一两个同时在，设置列表就会丢掉底栏、头又回来（补记四十六）。Shell 自己的底栏和设置对话框里画的那条都读 `mobileNavigationVisible`，所以一处就够。
- **没改的。** 三个页底部不另垫安全区，和花费、日程图这些本来就没有底栏的页一样。底栏在的时候它的 `60 + safeArea().bottom` 把下边的安全区占了；NSH 的网页是 `viewport-fit=cover`，所以在下边有安全区的手机上（iPhone 的横条、边到边的 Android），分工页和已归档列表的最后一行现在可能落到那一块里。用户手机的下边安全区没量过（中继日志里的「Android 10; K」是 Chrome 冻结的 UA，看不出系统版本），e2e 的 Chromium 是 0。`UsagePage` 有 `pb-[76px]`，不受影响。要垫的话是侧栏 `side-body` 在列表页时加 `paddingBottom: safeArea().bottom`，没做。
- **检查。** `.ui/shell.test.tsx` 新一条（420 宽）：依次从「工具」打开用量、分工、已归档会话，页出来后底栏没了，按页自己的「‹」收起、底栏回来；改之前这一条红（用量页上还看得到「Bot」「项目」「设置」），改之后 shell 全过。sidebar 的全部 `.ui` 文件、`usage`、`settings-routing` 全过；`nsh check App.tsx`、`tsc`、desktop-nsh 的 `bun test scripts`、spec 通道（1490 条）和主题检查都过。`phone-web-e2e.ts` 在设置底栏那一步后面新加一步：三个页依次从「工具」打开，地址是 `/?o=<kind>`，底栏没了，用浏览器自己的返回收起，回到 `/`、底栏和名单都在；带 pty helper 的 Chromium 整轮在工作树里通过（截图里用量页一直铺到屏幕底边）。WebKit 没跑，真手机没验证，没部署（要用户点头）。
- **部署。** 用户点头后手机页重新部署：构建 `f13ae0cb4f018241`，来自 `9c946d54`。那是这次修复 rebase 之前的提交，等于 `4d5147a7`（线上 `9fb56ed7` 那份代码加文档）加上修复，也就是上面 Chromium e2e 整轮通过的那棵树，所以没在部署工作树里重跑。没用 main 的最新：main 上还有没部署的首屏改动（补记六十、六十二）和要重启运行时的 Grok 改动，它们等各自的确认。线上 `Main.226763a50b.js` 和构建逐字节一致，里面是 `!J.listPage&&!J.workspaceOpen&&…`；`/`、`?o=settings`、`?o=usage` 200，`?x=1` 400，nginx 和中继都在跑，引擎对 brotli 浏览器发 br。真手机没验证。

The user said that on the phone the usage, roles and archived sessions pages should have no Bot / Projects / Settings bar at the bottom. All three are `runtime.listPage` (`usage` / `roles` / `archived`), opened from Tools in place of the roster in the sidebar, each with its own ‹, at `?o=<kind>`. None of the bar's three places is where they are, and pressing the bar's current place (Bots or Projects) did nothing there (`navigateMobile` returns on the same place).

- **Cause.** `mobileNavigationVisible` in `shell-state.ts` leaves out routines, spend, the terminal, the remote screen, the create dialogs, a conversation's settings, a Bot's profile and the danger confirm, and, while settings is closed, the workspace, a selected conversation and a file's preview, but never looked at `listPage`. The Svelte `Shell.svelte` condition of the same name had the same gap; it was ported as it was.
- **The change.** `!runtime.listPage` joins the workspace and the conversation in the arm for when settings is closed. Not at the top: `topLayer`'s tests have `listPageOpen` and `settingsOpen` both true, and although `openSettings` now clears `listPage`, a top-level condition would, should both ever be on, take the bar from settings' list and bring its head back (addendum 46). The shell's own bar and the one the settings dialog draws both read `mobileNavigationVisible`, so the one place is enough.
- **Unchanged.** The three pages get no bottom inset of their own, like spend and the routine calendar, which never had the bar. While the bar was there its `60 + safeArea().bottom` took the bottom inset; NSH's web page is `viewport-fit=cover`, so on a phone with a bottom inset (an iPhone's home bar, an edge-to-edge Android) the last row of the roles page and the archived list may now sit in it. The user's phone's bottom inset is not measured (the relay log's "Android 10; K" is Chrome's frozen UA and says nothing about the OS version); the e2e's Chromium has 0. `UsagePage` has `pb-[76px]` and is not affected. Padding them would be `paddingBottom: safeArea().bottom` on the sidebar's `side-body` while a list page is up; not done.
- **Checks.** `.ui/shell.test.tsx` gains a case (420 wide): usage, roles and the archived sessions opened from Tools in turn; with the page up the bar is gone, and the page's own ‹ puts it away and brings the bar back. Before the change it was red (the usage page still showed Bot, Projects and Settings); after it, shell passes whole. Every sidebar `.ui` file, `usage` and `settings-routing` pass; `nsh check App.tsx`, `tsc`, desktop-nsh's `bun test scripts`, the spec lane (1490 tests) and the theme check pass. `phone-web-e2e.ts` gains a step after the settings bar one: each page opened from Tools in turn, at `/?o=<kind>`, the bar gone, put away with the browser's own Back, back at `/` with the bar and the roster. The whole Chromium run with the pty helper passed in the worktree (in the screenshot the usage page reaches the screen's bottom edge). WebKit was not run, the real phone was not checked, and nothing was deployed (that waits for the user's OK).
- **Deployed.** On the user's OK the phone's page went out again: build `f13ae0cb4f018241`, from `9c946d54`. That is this fix's commit before its rebase, i.e. `4d5147a7` (the code of the live `9fb56ed7` plus docs) with the fix: the very tree the whole Chromium e2e above passed on, so it was not rerun in the deploy worktree. Not main's tip: main also carries first-load work not yet deployed (addenda 60, 62) and Grok changes that need a runtime restart, each waiting for its own OK. The live `Main.226763a50b.js` is byte-identical to the build and carries `!J.listPage&&!J.workspaceOpen&&…`; `/`, `?o=settings` and `?o=usage` answer 200, `?x=1` 400, nginx and the relay are active, and the engine goes out as br to a brotli browser. The real phone is not checked.

## 补记 2026-11-18（六十二）：手机页首屏只下引擎和几块小部件，重的视图和应用自己的 Rust 用到才下 / Addendum 2026-11-18 (62): the phone page's first screen downloads the engine and a few small parts; heavy views and the app's own Rust when first used

接补记 60 的第三步（拆 `Main.js`）和第四步的前一半（应用自己的 Rust 按需）。

- **NSH 怎么分。** `nsh package --target web` 把入口（`Hosted.tsx` → `App.tsx` 和它们静态引的模块）用到的部件链进引擎 `nsh.<摘要>.wasm`；之后每个 `import()` 出来的块，按它从 `nsh/base` 按名引了什么、引了哪些应用自己的 UI 集（`#[native_module]`），把对应的 gate 列进 `chunkSides`，那个块运行前先下这些副模块（`nsh-cli` 的 `web.rs` `GatePlan`）。整个应用是一个块 `Main.js`，所以富文本、代码编辑器、`TextView`、`DataTable`、日期选择、`Tree`、`Terminal` 全在首屏前下。应用自己的 Rust 更糟：入口经 `platform/shell-bridge.ts` → `desktop-commands.ts` 静态引了 `deskfolk/native` 的 `Files`，整个 `deskfolk/native`（PDF、Office、远程屏幕、代码配色、Markdown）就链进了引擎。
- **改法。** `desktop-commands.ts` 只在命令来时 `import("./desktop-files")` 取 `Files`（只有 Windows 和 Linux 的外壳会发这两个命令）；只这一处，引擎从 12.8 MB 降到 6.28 MB，`deskfolk/native` 成了 4.92 MB 的副模块。新的 `ui/lazy-view.tsx` 的 `lazyView(load)` 返回一个组件：第一次显示时 `import()` 真模块，之前什么都不画（或给的 `pending`），之后用同一个 props 对象调真组件（`untracked`），所以每个 prop 照旧是活的。加载失败时在它的位置说「这部分没能加载出来。」，带一个按钮：网页上是「重新载入页面」，桌面上是「重试」。（落地当天更正：原来写的是隔 0.5、2、8 秒自动重试，可量下来浏览器记住了失败的 `import()`，同一个地址再导入不发请求就失败，三次重试一次请求都没有；只有重新载入页面会重新取，它保留地址和设备。）`ui/lazy-views.ts` 用原名导出这些包装：`ComposerEditor`、`MarkdownBody`、`ArtifactCodeEditor`、`ArtifactTree`、`SheetView`、`WordView`、`NotificationQuietHours`、`SpendHeader`、`NshTerminal`、`TerminalPane`/`PdfPane`、`PdfViewer`、`OfficeViewer`、`MarkdownAnnotator`、`RemoteScreenView`；引的地方只改路径，类型改成 `import type`（留下一个只有类型的 `import { type X }` 会剩一句副作用导入，把模块拉回来）。`code-colors.tsx` 经 `code-colors-native.ts` 按需取 `Highlight`：桌面启动时就取并 warm（之前也是），网页上第一次要显示代码时才取（wasm 上 `warm` 本来什么都不做）。`pdfSourceOf`、`officeSourceOf` 搬进没有原生导入的 `pdf-source.ts`、`office-source.ts`（原模块再导出），因为文件预览对每个文件都问它们。`Select`、`MultiSelect`、菜单、浮层留在 `Main.js`：小，而且多数页面都用。
- **预取。** 外壳第一次连上后 1 秒，`preloadLazyViews()` 依次取标了 `preload` 的：输入框和消息正文，第一次打开会话不等；其余第一次显示时才下。
- **守卫。** `phone-package.ts --build` 打完包先跑 `checkFirstScreen`：`chunkSides["Main.js"]` 超出 core、menu、overlays、select、combobox，或 `deskfolk/native`（`nsh_ui_set_deskfolk_native`）不是单独的副模块（被链进了引擎），就失败并说出是哪个。拿这次之前的包（8c8f90ba）去跑，它说首屏会下 data_table、pickers、editor、rich_editor、term、text_view、tree。
- **量出来的。** 同样的方法（无头 Chromium，到配对屏，gzip -9）：改之前 39 个文件、7.07 MB；只挪 `Files` 以后总量不变（`Main.js` 还挂着一切）；拆完视图 5.83 MB；`deskfolk/native` 也挪出去以后 45 个文件、原始 10.0 MB、**3.57 MB**。剩下的大头：引擎 2.30 MB、`Main.js` 0.65 MB、`runtime.js` 0.20 MB、core 0.11 MB。`Main.js` 原始从 3.1 MB 到 2.1 MB。`deskfolk/native` 副模块 4.92 MB，br 1.44 MB，现在随消息正文在首屏后预取。CSP 不变：新旧两页用 `deploy/remote/csp.mjs` 生成的完全一样（打包时多印的 `img-src 'self' blob: data:` 只挪 `Files` 那次就有，不是这次带来的）。
- **试出来的下一步。** 在 wasm 上把 `pdf` 换成同接口的桩打包一次：`deskfolk/native` 从 4.92 MB 掉到 0.43 MB（br 1.44 MB → 0.14 MB）。九成是 PDF：hayro 连带 `hayro-cmap` 要的整个 `brotli` 8（含编码器的表）和内嵌字体。所以下一步把 PDF 拆成单独的 crate（`deskfolk/pdf`），打开会话就只下 0.14 MB 的这一块。
- **检查。** `isolation`、phone-package（新加 5 条：放过小部件、说出重部件、说出应用的 Rust 在首屏、说出它被链进引擎、没有 `Main.js`）、desktop-nsh 的 `bun test scripts`、spec 一道、主题检查、`tsc`（`app/tsconfig.json` 只有 main 上就有的 `Composer.tsx` 4 个错）、`nsh check App.tsx` 都过；`.ui` 的 `chat-composer*` 三个文件 43 条都过；新包的配对屏在无头 Chromium 里出来、控制台无错。没部署。

Continuing addendum 60's third step (splitting `Main.js`) and the first half of the fourth (the app's own Rust on demand).

- **How NSH splits.** `nsh package --target web` links into the engine `nsh.<digest>.wasm` the parts the entry uses (`Hosted.tsx` → `App.tsx` and what they import statically); every later chunk made by an `import()` gets the gates of what it imports by name from `nsh/base` and of the app's own UI sets (`#[native_module]`) in `chunkSides`, and those side modules load before the chunk runs (`nsh-cli`'s `web.rs`, `GatePlan`). The whole app was one chunk, `Main.js`, so the rich editor, the code editor, `TextView`, `DataTable`, the date pickers, `Tree` and `Terminal` all came before the first screen. The app's own Rust was worse off: the entry imported `deskfolk/native`'s `Files` statically through `platform/shell-bridge.ts` → `desktop-commands.ts`, so all of `deskfolk/native` (PDF, Office, remote screen, code colours, Markdown) was linked into the engine.
- **The change.** `desktop-commands.ts` takes `Files` by `import("./desktop-files")` when a command comes (only the Windows and Linux shells send those two); that alone took the engine from 12.8 MB to 6.28 MB and made `deskfolk/native` a 4.92 MB side module. The new `ui/lazy-view.tsx`'s `lazyView(load)` returns a component that `import()`s the real module the first time it shows, draws nothing until then (or the given `pending`), and then calls the real component with the same props object (`untracked`), so every prop stays live. A failed load says so where the view would be, "This part didn't load.", with a button: "Reload the page" on the web, "Try again" on the desktops. (Corrected the day it landed: it said a failed load was tried again after 0.5, 2 and 8 s, but measured, the browser keeps a failed `import()`: importing that URL again fails at once with no request, and the three retries made none. Only reloading the page fetches it again, and that keeps the address and the device.) `ui/lazy-views.ts` exports those wrappers under the views' own names: `ComposerEditor`, `MarkdownBody`, `ArtifactCodeEditor`, `ArtifactTree`, `SheetView`, `WordView`, `NotificationQuietHours`, `SpendHeader`, `NshTerminal`, `TerminalPane`/`PdfPane`, `PdfViewer`, `OfficeViewer`, `MarkdownAnnotator`, `RemoteScreenView`; the importers only change the path, and their types become `import type` (an `import { type X }` left alone leaves a side-effect import behind, which pulls the module back). `code-colors.tsx` takes `Highlight` through `code-colors-native.ts` when wanted: on the desktops at start, warming it as before; on the web the first time code is about to show (`warm` does nothing on wasm anyway). `pdfSourceOf` and `officeSourceOf` move into `pdf-source.ts` and `office-source.ts`, which import nothing native (the old modules re-export them), as the file preview asks them for every file. `Select`, `MultiSelect`, menus and popovers stay in `Main.js`: small, and on most screens.
- **Preloading.** A second after the shell first connects, `preloadLazyViews()` fetches, one after another, the views marked `preload`: the message box and a message's text, so the first conversation opened does not wait. The rest load when first shown.
- **The guard.** `phone-package.ts --build` runs `checkFirstScreen` on the package first: it fails, naming them, when `chunkSides["Main.js"]` goes beyond core, menu, overlays, select and combobox, or when `deskfolk/native` (`nsh_ui_set_deskfolk_native`) is no side module of its own (linked into the engine). Run on the package from before this work (8c8f90ba), it says the first screen would load data_table, pickers, editor, rich_editor, term, text_view and tree.
- **Measured.** The same way (headless Chromium to the pairing screen, gzip -9): before, 39 files and 7.07 MB; with only `Files` moved, the same total (`Main.js` still hung everything on); with the views split, 5.83 MB; with `deskfolk/native` out too, 45 files, 10.0 MB raw, **3.57 MB**. What remains is mostly the engine 2.30 MB, `Main.js` 0.65 MB, `runtime.js` 0.20 MB and core 0.11 MB. `Main.js` went from 3.1 MB to 2.1 MB raw. The `deskfolk/native` side module, 4.92 MB and 1.44 MB as br, is now preloaded with a message's text after the first screen. The policy is unchanged: `deploy/remote/csp.mjs` makes the same one from the old and the new page (the `img-src 'self' blob: data:` the packager now prints was there with only `Files` moved, so it is not from this).
- **What the next step was tried on.** One package with `pdf` swapped for a stub of the same interface on wasm: `deskfolk/native` fell from 4.92 MB to 0.43 MB (br 1.44 MB → 0.14 MB). Nine tenths of it is PDF: hayro with the whole `brotli` 8 (its encoder's tables included) that `hayro-cmap` takes, and the embedded fonts. So the next step makes PDF a crate of its own (`deskfolk/pdf`), and opening a conversation downloads only that 0.14 MB part.
- **Checks.** `isolation`, phone-package (five new cases: passes the small parts, names a heavy part, names the app's Rust on the first screen, names it linked into the engine, no `Main.js`), desktop-nsh's `bun test scripts`, the spec lane, the theme check, `tsc` (`app/tsconfig.json` has only the four `Composer.tsx` errors main has) and `nsh check App.tsx` pass; the `.ui` `chat-composer*` files pass, 43 tests; the new package's pairing screen comes up in headless Chromium with a clean console. Not deployed.

## 补记 2026-11-19（六十五）：手机上从名单打开的会话，先写历史记录再显示 / Addendum 2026-11-19 (65): on the phone, a conversation opened from the roster writes its history entry before it shows

用户在安卓手机（Chrome 157 装的 PWA）上发现：从左边缘滑动返回时，底下露出的「上一页」还是当前这个会话；从右边缘滑没有这个动画。左边缘这一下是 Chrome 的返回预览（back/forward transition）：页面滑开，底下是要回去的那条历史记录的截图。右边缘 Chrome 不做预览，这一点是 Chrome 的，不改。

- **原因。** Chrome 在页面离开一条记录时给它截图；同文档导航（`pushState`）截的是导航所在那一帧之前的那块 surface（viz 的 `CompositorFrameSinkSupport::MaybeSubmitCompositorFrame`：带 `screenshot_destination` 的帧来了，复制前一个 surface）。所以截到的是 `pushState` 之前浏览器最后画出来的那一帧。在线上那版构建（`69e5f09f`，NSH 39aec03c）上用 headless Chromium 手机尺寸、真触摸量了：点名单上的一行，会话进 DOM、`UrlScheme.setUrl` 到 NSH 都在 `touchend` 那个任务里；NSH 的 `syncHistorySoon` 用 `setTimeout(0)` 写记录，中间浏览器先画了一帧会话，`pushState` 在那帧画完之后（3 次里 0～2 帧之后）。名单那条记录的截图就成了会话。
- **先试的 NSH 改法，没用。** 让 NSH 在下一帧的动画回调里（画之前）就写记录。点一下打开会话是对的（`pushState` 落在第一帧会话的回调里、画之前），但整轮 e2e 在「项目行的菜单打开目录」那一步红了：菜单（NSH 的 modal，有自己的记录）收起要晚一帧才到 NSH，提前写就把 `?s=` 推在菜单那条上面，菜单一走 NSH `go(-2)`，把会话那条也带走了，返回一次就目录和会话一起没了，再返回离开页面。NSH 的一「轮」不止点按那个任务，单纯提前写会丢记录。上游没提 PR，问题提成了 longbridge/nsh#2568。
- **改法。** 应用这边，只管从名单打开会话：先写地址，等页面到了再显示会话。`PageRoute.entryFirst(id)`：名单原样（没选会话、没文件、没页、没设置）时，用 `write` 同样的 `sessionUrl` 算出地址，`setUrl(push)`，等 `href` 到了（`whenThere`：NSH 的定时器之后看第一次，之后 4 ms 一看，最多 `ENTRY_WAIT_MS` 250 ms，到时照开）。之后的选中由 `write` 发现页面已经在那（`sameQuery` → `land`），不再写。地址已经是它（返回、前进、链接）就不等。名单的行和置顶条都走 `ui/sidebar/open-from-roster.ts` 的 `openFromRoster`：Bot↔Bot 直聊不走（它开的是浮窗、没有自己的地址）；等的时候又点了另一个，最后那个开。
- **代价。** 点一下到会话出来多一帧左右：量的三次 46～71 ms，改前 28～46 ms。
- **没管的。** 别的打开方式还是先画后写：搜索结果、通知、消息里的链接、项目行菜单里的「打开目录」、设置、用量这些页、文件。按返回先关一个地址里没有的浮层（`backMobileLayer`）再把会话那条推回去（`page-route.ts` 的那一处）和 `#floor`：这两处推的时候屏幕本来就是会话，下面那条的截图必然是会话，要改得让这些浮层自己有记录，另议。等记录写好的那一下（一帧左右）里又点了名单上另一行：后点的那个开，但先点的那个的 `?s=` 记录还留着，返回会先到它，没测。
- **检查。** `page-route.test.ts` 新三条：从名单打开先有记录、显示后不再写、返回落回名单；有会话、有页、有设置时什么也不写；地址已经是它就不等（24 条全过）。`phone-web-e2e.ts` 打开 Writer 会话那一步，`pushState(?s=…)` 时会话不能已经在 DOM 里。改前改后整轮 Chromium e2e（跳过 headless 下本来就红的两块触摸滑动，线上构建也一样跳过）：改后全过，改前这一步红；探针三次 `pushState` 时会话都还不在 DOM。WebKit 整轮：每一步都过（含新的这一步），最后只红在早就有的 `blob:` CSP 那个不稳定项上。Chromium 没带 pty helper，终端两步跳过。真手机上的预览没看过，要部署后用户看（部署要用户点头）。

The user found on an Android phone (a Chrome 157 PWA) that swiping back from the left edge showed the same conversation underneath as the "previous page"; from the right edge there is no such animation. The left edge is Chrome's back/forward transition: the page slides away over a picture of the entry it goes back to. Chrome draws no picture for the right edge; that is Chrome's and stays.

- **Cause.** Chrome pictures an entry as the page leaves it. For a same-document navigation (`pushState`) it copies the surface from before the frame that carries the navigation (viz `CompositorFrameSinkSupport::MaybeSubmitCompositorFrame`: a frame with a `screenshot_destination` copies the previous surface), so the picture is the last frame drawn before `pushState`. Measured on the live build (`69e5f09f`, NSH 39aec03c) in headless Chromium at a phone's size with real touches: a press on a roster row puts the conversation in the DOM and `UrlScheme.setUrl` reaches NSH in the `touchend` task; NSH's `syncHistorySoon` writes the entry on a `setTimeout(0)`; the browser drew a frame with the conversation first, and `pushState` ran after that frame's paint (0 to 2 frames later, 3 of 3). The roster's entry was pictured with the conversation.
- **The NSH change tried first did not hold.** Writing the entries in the next frame's animation callbacks, before its paint, fixed the press (`pushState` inside the conversation's first frame's callbacks), but the whole e2e went red at "a project's folder from its row's menu": the menu (an NSH modal with an entry of its own) reaches NSH as leaving a frame later, so the early write put `?s=` on top of the menu's entry, and when the menu went NSH's `go(-2)` took the conversation's entry with it: one Back put away folder and conversation, the next left the page. An NSH turn is more than the press's task; writing earlier alone drops entries. No upstream PR; the problem is filed as longbridge/nsh#2568.
- **The change.** In the app, only for a conversation opened from the roster: the address first, the conversation once the page is there. `PageRoute.entryFirst(id)`: with the roster as it stands (nothing selected, no file, no page, no settings) it computes the address with `write`'s own `sessionUrl`, `setUrl(push)`es it, and waits for `href` to get there (`whenThere`: a first look after NSH's timer, then every 4 ms, at most `ENTRY_WAIT_MS` 250 ms, after which it opens anyway). The selection then finds the page there already in `write` (`sameQuery` → `land`) and writes nothing. An address that names it already (Back, Forward, a link) does not wait. The roster's rows and the pinned strip go through `openFromRoster` (`ui/sidebar/open-from-roster.ts`); a Bot↔Bot direct does not (it opens a popup and has no address of its own); of presses while an entry is on its way, the last one opens.
- **Cost.** About a frame between the press and the conversation: 46–71 ms in the three measured, 28–46 ms before.
- **Not covered.** Everything else still draws first and writes after: search results, notifications, links in messages, Open project folder in a row's menu, settings, the pages from Tools, files. Back that closes a layer the address does not carry (`backMobileLayer`) and pushes the conversation's entry again (`page-route.ts`), and the `#floor`: the screen is the conversation when those push, so the entry under is pictured with it whatever the timing; fixing them means giving those layers entries of their own, a separate change. A second row pressed during the wait (about a frame) opens, but the first one's `?s=` entry stays, so Back goes to it first; not tested.
- **Checks.** `page-route.test.ts` gains three: from the roster the entry comes first, showing writes nothing more and Back lands on the roster; nothing is written over a conversation, a page or settings; an address already there does not wait (24 pass). `phone-web-e2e.ts`'s step that opens the Writer's conversation now fails if the conversation is in the DOM when `pushState(?s=…)` runs. The whole Chromium e2e (skipping the two touch-swipe blocks that are red headless on this NSH, skipped the same way for the live build) passes after the change, and that step is red before it; the probe saw the conversation absent from the DOM at all three pushes. WebKit's whole run passed every step, the new one included, then went red only on the old `blob:` CSP flake. The Chromium runs had no pty helper, so the two terminal steps were skipped. The picture on a real phone is not seen yet: that needs a deploy, which needs the user's OK.
